package com.ttexplo.mobile

import android.Manifest
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.LaunchedEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.remember
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.lifecycle.viewmodel.compose.viewModel
import androidx.navigation.NavType
import androidx.navigation.compose.NavHost
import androidx.navigation.compose.composable

import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.history.ValesHistoryScreen
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.home.HomeScreen
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.license.LicenseScreen
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.settings.SettingsScreen
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.theme.MineDarkBackground

import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import java.net.URLDecoder

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {

    private val bluetoothPermissionLauncher = registerForActivityResult(
        ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()
    ) {
        // Permissions handled
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)

        // Request Bluetooth permissions on Android 12+ (API 31+)
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            val needed = listOf(
                Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT,
                Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_SCAN
            ).filter {
                ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, it) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
            }
            if (needed.isNotEmpty()) {
                bluetoothPermissionLauncher.launch(needed.toTypedArray())
            }
        }

        // Seed initial bundled catalog if database is empty
        lifecycleScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val db = (application as TTExploApp).database
            val repo = CatalogRepositoryImpl(db.productoDao())
            if (repo.countProducts() == 0) {
                try {
                    val csv = assets.open("catalogo_productos.csv").bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
                    repo.importCsv(csv)
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    // Ignore asset loading error
                }
            }
        }

        setContent {
            TTExploTheme {
                Surface(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    color = MineDarkBackground
                ) {
                    TTExploAppNavigation()
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun TTExploAppNavigation() {
    val navController = rememberNavController()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val initialLicense = remember { LicenseManager.checkLicense(context) }
    val isLicensed = initialLicense.status == LicenseStatus.VALID || initialLicense.status == LicenseStatus.EXPIRING_SOON
    val startDest = if (isLicensed) Screen.Home.route else Screen.License.createRoute(canBack = false)

    NavHost(
        navController = navController,
        startDestination = startDest
    ) {
        // HOME SCREEN (Welcome Window)
        composable(Screen.Home.route) {
            HomeScreen(
                onNavigateToEntradas = { navController.navigate(Screen.EntradasHeader.route) },
                onNavigateToDespacho = { navController.navigate(Screen.DespachoHeader.route) },
                onNavigateToAccesorios = { navController.navigate(Screen.AccesoriosHeader.route) },
                onNavigateToDevolucionAccesorios = { navController.navigate(Screen.DevolucionAccesoriosHeader.route) },
                onNavigateToDevoluciones = { navController.navigate(Screen.Devoluciones.createRoute()) },
                onNavigateToSettings = { navController.navigate(Screen.Settings.route) },
                onNavigateToHistory = { navController.navigate(Screen.ValesHistory.route) },
                onNavigateToLicense = { navController.navigate(Screen.License.createRoute(canBack = true)) }
            )
        }

        // 1. ENTRADAS MODULE
        composable(Screen.EntradasHeader.route) {
            EntradasHeaderScreen(
                onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onContinueToScan = { prov, polv, fact ->
                    navController.navigate(Screen.EntradasScan.createRoute(prov, polv, fact))
                }
            )
        }

        composable(
            route = Screen.EntradasScan.route,
            arguments = listOf(
                navArgument("prov") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("polv") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("fact") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("id") { type = NavType.LongType; defaultValue = 0L }
            )
        ) { backStackEntry ->
            val prov = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("prov") ?: ""
            val polv = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("polv") ?: ""
            val fact = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("fact") ?: ""
            val id = backStackEntry.arguments?.getLong("id") ?: 0L
            val entradasVm: EntradasViewModel = viewModel()

            EntradasScanScreen(
                proveedor = prov,
                polvorin = polv,
                factura = fact,
                entradaId = id,
                viewModel = entradasVm,
                onFinished = {
                    navController.popBackStack(Screen.Home.route, inclusive = false)
                }
            )
        }

        // 2. DESPACHO MODULE
        composable(Screen.DespachoHeader.route) {
            DespachoHeaderScreen(
                onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onContinueToScan = { vale, empresa, lugar, transportista, turno, voladura ->
                    navController.navigate(Screen.DespachoScan.createRoute(vale, empresa, lugar, transportista, turno, voladura))
                }
            )
        }

        composable(
            route = Screen.DespachoScan.route,
            arguments = listOf(
                navArgument("vale") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("empresa") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("lugar") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("transportista") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("turno") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("voladura") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("id") { type = NavType.LongType; defaultValue = 0L }
            )
        ) { backStackEntry ->
            val vale = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("vale") ?: ""
            val rawEmpresa = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("empresa") ?: ""
            val rawLugar = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("lugar") ?: ""
            val rawTrans = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("transportista") ?: ""
            val turno = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("turno") ?: ""
            val voladura = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("voladura") ?: ""
            val id = backStackEntry.arguments?.getLong("id") ?: 0L

            val empresa = URLDecoder.decode(rawEmpresa, "UTF-8")
            val lugar = URLDecoder.decode(rawLugar, "UTF-8")
            val transportista = URLDecoder.decode(rawTrans, "UTF-8")

            val despachoVm: DespachoViewModel = viewModel()

            DespachoScanScreen(
                vale = vale,
                empresa = empresa,
                lugar = lugar,
                transportista = transportista,
                turno = turno,
                voladura = voladura,
                despachoId = id,
                viewModel = despachoVm,
                onFinished = {
                    navController.popBackStack(Screen.Home.route, inclusive = false)
                }
            )
        }

        // 3. ACCESORIOS MODULE
        composable(Screen.AccesoriosHeader.route) {
            AccesoriosHeaderScreen(
                onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onContinueToScan = { vale, empresa, lugar, transportista, turno, recibio, entrego, voladura ->
                    navController.navigate(Screen.AccesoriosScan.createRoute(vale, empresa, lugar, transportista, turno, recibio, entrego, voladura))
                }
            )
        }

        composable(
            route = Screen.AccesoriosScan.route,
            arguments = listOf(
                navArgument("vale") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("empresa") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("lugar") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("transportista") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("turno") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("recibio") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("entrego") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("voladura") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("id") { type = NavType.LongType; defaultValue = 0L }
            )
        ) { backStackEntry ->
            val vale = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("vale") ?: ""
            val rawEmpresa = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("empresa") ?: ""
            val rawLugar = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("lugar") ?: ""
            val rawTrans = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("transportista") ?: ""
            val turno = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("turno") ?: ""
            val rawRecibio = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("recibio") ?: ""
            val rawEntrego = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("entrego") ?: ""
            val voladura = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("voladura") ?: ""
            val id = backStackEntry.arguments?.getLong("id") ?: 0L

            val empresa = URLDecoder.decode(rawEmpresa, "UTF-8")
            val lugar = URLDecoder.decode(rawLugar, "UTF-8")
            val transportista = URLDecoder.decode(rawTrans, "UTF-8")
            val recibio = URLDecoder.decode(rawRecibio, "UTF-8")
            val entrego = URLDecoder.decode(rawEntrego, "UTF-8")

            val accesoriosVm: AccesoriosViewModel = viewModel()

            AccesoriosScanScreen(
                vale = vale,
                empresa = empresa,
                lugar = lugar,
                transportista = transportista,
                turno = turno,
                recibio = recibio,
                entrego = entrego,
                voladura = voladura,
                valeId = id,
                viewModel = accesoriosVm,
                onFinished = {
                    navController.popBackStack(Screen.Home.route, inclusive = false)
                }
            )
        }

        // 4. DEVOLUCIONES MODULE
        composable(
            route = Screen.Devoluciones.route,
            arguments = listOf(
                navArgument("id") { type = NavType.LongType; defaultValue = 0L }
            )
        ) { backStackEntry ->
            val id = backStackEntry.arguments?.getLong("id") ?: 0L
            DevolucionScreen(
                onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                devolucionId = id
            )
        }

        // 5. DEVOLUCION DE ACCESORIOS MODULE
        composable(Screen.DevolucionAccesoriosHeader.route) {
            AccesoriosHeaderScreen(
                isDevolucion = true,
                onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onContinueToScan = { vale, empresa, lugar, transportista, turno, recibio, entrego, voladura ->
                    navController.navigate(
                        Screen.DevolucionAccesoriosScan.createRoute(
                            vale,
                            empresa,
                            lugar,
                            transportista,
                            turno,
                            recibio,
                            entrego,
                            voladura
                        )
                    )
                }
            )
        }

        composable(
            route = Screen.DevolucionAccesoriosScan.route,
            arguments = listOf(
                navArgument("vale") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("empresa") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("lugar") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("transportista") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("turno") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("recibio") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("entrego") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("voladura") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("id") { type = NavType.LongType; defaultValue = 0L }
            )
        ) { backStackEntry ->
            val vale = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("vale") ?: ""
            val rawEmpresa = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("empresa") ?: ""
            val rawLugar = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("lugar") ?: ""
            val rawTrans = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("transportista") ?: ""
            val turno = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("turno") ?: ""
            val rawRecibio = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("recibio") ?: ""
            val rawEntrego = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("entrego") ?: ""
            val voladura = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("voladura") ?: ""
            val id = backStackEntry.arguments?.getLong("id") ?: 0L

            val empresa = URLDecoder.decode(rawEmpresa, "UTF-8")
            val lugar = URLDecoder.decode(rawLugar, "UTF-8")
            val transportista = URLDecoder.decode(rawTrans, "UTF-8")
            val recibio = URLDecoder.decode(rawRecibio, "UTF-8")
            val entrego = URLDecoder.decode(rawEntrego, "UTF-8")

            val accesoriosVm: AccesoriosViewModel = viewModel()

            AccesoriosScanScreen(
                vale = vale,
                empresa = empresa,
                lugar = lugar,
                transportista = transportista,
                turno = turno,
                recibio = recibio,
                entrego = entrego,
                voladura = voladura,
                valeId = id,
                isDevolucion = true,
                viewModel = accesoriosVm,
                onFinished = {
                    navController.popBackStack(Screen.Home.route, inclusive = false)
                }
            )
        }

        // 6. SETTINGS & EXPORT MODULE
        composable(Screen.Settings.route) {
            SettingsScreen(
                onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onNavigateCatalog = { navController.navigate(Screen.CatalogManagement.route) },
                onNavigateHistory = { navController.navigate(Screen.ValesHistory.route) },
                onNavigateToLicense = { navController.navigate(Screen.License.createRoute(canBack = true)) }
            )
        }

        // 7. CATALOG CRUD MODULE
        composable(Screen.CatalogManagement.route) {
            CatalogManagementScreen(
                onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() }
            )
        }

        // 8. VALES HISTORY MODULE
        composable(Screen.ValesHistory.route) {
            ValesHistoryScreen(
                onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onEditDespacho = { id ->
                    navController.navigate(Screen.DespachoScan.createRoute("-", "-", "-", "-", "1", "-", id))
                },
                onEditEntrada = { id ->
                    navController.navigate(Screen.EntradasScan.createRoute("-", "-", "-", id))
                },
                onEditAccesorios = { id ->
                    navController.navigate(Screen.AccesoriosScan.createRoute("-", "-", "-", "-", "1", "-", "-", "-", id))
                },
                onEditDevolucionAccesorios = { id ->
                    navController.navigate(Screen.DevolucionAccesoriosScan.createRoute("-", "-", "-", "-", "1", "-", "-", "-", id))
                },
                onEditDevolucion = { id ->
                    navController.navigate(Screen.Devoluciones.createRoute(id))
                }
            )
        }

        // 9. LICENCIA & DISPOSITIVO MODULE
        composable(
            route = Screen.License.route,
            arguments = listOf(
                navArgument("canBack") { type = NavType.BoolType; defaultValue = false }
            )
        ) { backStackEntry ->
            val canBack = backStackEntry.arguments?.getBoolean("canBack") ?: false
            LicenseScreen(
                canNavigateBack = canBack,
                onNavigateBack = { navController.popBackStack() },
                onLicenseActivated = {
                    navController.navigate(Screen.Home.route) {
                        popUpTo(0) { inclusive = true }
                    }
                }
            )
        }
    }
}

package com.ttexplo.mobile

import android.app.Application
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.AppDatabase

class TTExploApp : Application() {

    val database: AppDatabase by lazy {
        AppDatabase.getInstance(this)
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        instance = this
    }

    companion object {
        lateinit var instance: TTExploApp
            private set
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\data\local\AppDatabase.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.dao.*
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.*

@Database(
    entities = [
        ProductoEntity::class,
        EntradaEntity::class,
        EntradaItemEntity::class,
        DespachoEntity::class,
        DespachoItemEntity::class,
        DevolucionEntity::class,
        DevolucionItemEntity::class,
        AccesorioValeEntity::class,
        AccesorioItemEntity::class
    ],
    version = 2,
    exportSchema = false
)
abstract class AppDatabase : RoomDatabase() {

    abstract fun productoDao(): ProductoDao
    abstract fun entradaDao(): EntradaDao
    abstract fun despachoDao(): DespachoDao
    abstract fun devolucionDao(): DevolucionDao
    abstract fun accesorioDao(): AccesorioDao

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: AppDatabase? = null

        fun getInstance(context: Context): AppDatabase {
            return INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                val instance = Room.databaseBuilder(
                    context.applicationContext,
                    AppDatabase::class.java,
                    "ttexplo_database.db"
                ).fallbackToDestructiveMigration()
                .build()
                INSTANCE = instance
                instance
            }
        }
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\data\local\dao\MovementDaos.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.dao

import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.Query
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.*

@Dao
interface EntradaDao {
    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertEntrada(entrada: EntradaEntity): Long

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertItems(items: List<EntradaItemEntity>)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSingleItem(item: EntradaItemEntity): Long

    @Query("SELECT * FROM entradas WHERE id = :entradaId LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getEntradaById(entradaId: Long): EntradaEntity?

    @Query("SELECT * FROM entradas WHERE estado = 'BORRADOR' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getActiveDraft(): EntradaEntity?

    @Query("UPDATE entradas SET estado = :nuevoEstado WHERE id = :entradaId")
    suspend fun updateEstado(entradaId: Long, nuevoEstado: String)

    @Query("DELETE FROM entrada_items WHERE entrada_id = :entradaId AND codigo_barras = :codigo")
    suspend fun deleteItem(entradaId: Long, codigo: String)

    @Query("DELETE FROM entrada_items WHERE entrada_id = :entradaId")
    suspend fun deleteItemsForEntrada(entradaId: Long)

    @Query("DELETE FROM entradas WHERE id = :entradaId")
    suspend fun deleteEntrada(entradaId: Long)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM entradas ORDER BY id DESC")
    suspend fun getAllEntradas(): List<EntradaEntity>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM entrada_items WHERE entrada_id = :entradaId ORDER BY id DESC")
    suspend fun getItemsForEntrada(entradaId: Long): List<EntradaItemEntity>

    @Query("UPDATE entradas SET exportado = 1 WHERE exportado = 0")
    suspend fun markAllExported()
}

@Dao
interface DespachoDao {
    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertDespacho(despacho: DespachoEntity): Long

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertItems(items: List<DespachoItemEntity>)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSingleItem(item: DespachoItemEntity): Long

    @Query("SELECT * FROM despachos WHERE id = :despachoId LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getDespachoById(despachoId: Long): DespachoEntity?

    @Query("SELECT * FROM despachos WHERE estado = 'BORRADOR' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getActiveDraft(): DespachoEntity?

    @Query("UPDATE despachos SET estado = :nuevoEstado WHERE id = :despachoId")
    suspend fun updateEstado(despachoId: Long, nuevoEstado: String)

    @Query("DELETE FROM despacho_items WHERE despacho_id = :despachoId AND codigo_barras = :codigo")
    suspend fun deleteItem(despachoId: Long, codigo: String)

    @Query("DELETE FROM despacho_items WHERE despacho_id = :despachoId")
    suspend fun deleteItemsForDespacho(despachoId: Long)

    @Query("DELETE FROM despachos WHERE id = :despachoId")
    suspend fun deleteDespacho(despachoId: Long)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM despachos ORDER BY id DESC")
    suspend fun getAllDespachos(): List<DespachoEntity>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM despacho_items WHERE despacho_id = :despachoId ORDER BY id DESC")
    suspend fun getItemsForDespacho(despachoId: Long): List<DespachoItemEntity>

    @Query("UPDATE despachos SET exportado = 1 WHERE exportado = 0")
    suspend fun markAllExported()
}

@Dao
interface DevolucionDao {
    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertDevolucion(devolucion: DevolucionEntity): Long

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertItems(items: List<DevolucionItemEntity>)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSingleItem(item: DevolucionItemEntity): Long

    @Query("SELECT * FROM devoluciones WHERE id = :devolucionId LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getDevolucionById(devolucionId: Long): DevolucionEntity?

    @Query("SELECT * FROM devoluciones WHERE estado = 'BORRADOR' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getActiveDraft(): DevolucionEntity?

    @Query("UPDATE devoluciones SET estado = :nuevoEstado WHERE id = :devolucionId")
    suspend fun updateEstado(devolucionId: Long, nuevoEstado: String)

    @Query("DELETE FROM devolucion_items WHERE devolucion_id = :devolucionId AND codigo_barras = :codigo")
    suspend fun deleteItem(devolucionId: Long, codigo: String)

    @Query("DELETE FROM devolucion_items WHERE devolucion_id = :devolucionId")
    suspend fun deleteItemsForDevolucion(devolucionId: Long)

    @Query("DELETE FROM devoluciones WHERE id = :devolucionId")
    suspend fun deleteDevolucion(devolucionId: Long)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM devoluciones ORDER BY id DESC")
    suspend fun getAllDevoluciones(): List<DevolucionEntity>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM devolucion_items WHERE devolucion_id = :devolucionId ORDER BY id DESC")
    suspend fun getItemsForDevolucion(devolucionId: Long): List<DevolucionItemEntity>

    @Query("UPDATE devoluciones SET exportado = 1 WHERE exportado = 0")
    suspend fun markAllExported()
}

@Dao
interface AccesorioDao {
    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertVale(vale: AccesorioValeEntity): Long

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertItems(items: List<AccesorioItemEntity>)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSingleItem(item: AccesorioItemEntity): Long

    @Query("SELECT * FROM accesorios_vales WHERE id = :valeId LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getValeById(valeId: Long): AccesorioValeEntity?

    @Query("SELECT * FROM accesorios_vales WHERE estado = 'BORRADOR' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getActiveDraft(): AccesorioValeEntity?

    @Query("UPDATE accesorios_vales SET estado = :nuevoEstado WHERE id = :valeId")
    suspend fun updateEstado(valeId: Long, nuevoEstado: String)

    @Query("DELETE FROM accesorio_items WHERE vale_id = :valeId AND codigo_accesorio = :codigo")
    suspend fun deleteItem(valeId: Long, codigo: String)

    @Query("DELETE FROM accesorio_items WHERE vale_id = :valeId")
    suspend fun deleteItemsForVale(valeId: Long)

    @Query("DELETE FROM accesorios_vales WHERE id = :valeId")
    suspend fun deleteVale(valeId: Long)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM accesorios_vales ORDER BY id DESC")
    suspend fun getAllVales(): List<AccesorioValeEntity>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM accesorio_items WHERE vale_id = :valeId ORDER BY id DESC")
    suspend fun getItemsForVale(valeId: Long): List<AccesorioItemEntity>

    @Query("UPDATE accesorios_vales SET exportado = 1 WHERE exportado = 0")
    suspend fun markAllExported()
}

package com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.dao

import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.Query
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.ProductoEntity
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Dao
interface ProductoDao {

    @Query("SELECT * FROM productos WHERE id_producto = :id LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getById(id: String): ProductoEntity?

    @Query("SELECT * FROM productos WHERE cod_barras = :codBarras LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getByCodBarras(codBarras: String): ProductoEntity?

    @Query("SELECT * FROM productos ORDER BY id_producto ASC")
    fun getAllFlow(): Flow<List<ProductoEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM productos ORDER BY id_producto ASC")
    suspend fun getAll(): List<ProductoEntity>

    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM productos")
    suspend fun count(): Int

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insert(producto: ProductoEntity)

    @Query("DELETE FROM productos WHERE id_producto = :id")
    suspend fun delete(id: String)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM productos WHERE id_producto LIKE '%' || :query || '%' OR producto LIKE '%' || :query || '%' ORDER BY id_producto ASC")
    suspend fun search(query: String): List<ProductoEntity>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertAll(productos: List<ProductoEntity>)

    @Query("DELETE FROM productos")
    suspend fun deleteAll()
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\data\local\entity\ProductoEntity.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity

import androidx.room.ColumnInfo
import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductInfo
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductType

@Entity(tableName = "productos")
data class ProductoEntity(
    @PrimaryKey
    @ColumnInfo(name = "id_producto")
    val idProducto: String,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "producto")
    val producto: String,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "presentacion")
    val presentacion: String,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "no_sap")
    val noSap: String,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "unidad")
    val unidad: String,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "cantidad")
    val cantidad: Double,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "cod_barras")
    val codBarras: String,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "peso")
    val peso: Double,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "pg")
    val pg: String,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "pu")
    val pu: Double,

    @ColumnInfo(name = "tipo")
    val tipo: String // "ALTO_EXPLOSIVO" | "AGENTE"
) {
    fun toDomain(): ProductInfo {
        val parsedType = try {
            ProductType.valueOf(tipo)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            ProductType.DESCONOCIDO
        }
        return ProductInfo(
            id = idProducto,
            name = producto.trim(),
            presentation = presentacion.trim(),
            noSap = noSap.trim(),
            unit = unidad.trim(),
            weightKg = peso,
            type = parsedType,
            pg = pg.trim()
        )
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\data\repository\CatalogRepository.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository

import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.dao.ProductoDao
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.ProductoEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductClassifier
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductInfo
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductType

interface CatalogRepository {
    suspend fun importCsv(csvContent: String): Int
    suspend fun lookupBarcode(barcode: String): ProductInfo
    suspend fun countProducts(): Int
    suspend fun getAllProducts(): List<ProductInfo>
}

class CatalogRepositoryImpl(
    private val productoDao: ProductoDao
) : CatalogRepository {

    override suspend fun importCsv(csvContent: String): Int {
        val lines = csvContent.lines()
        val entities = mutableListOf<ProductoEntity>()

        for ((index, line) in lines.withIndex()) {
            val trimmed = line.trim()
            if (trimmed.isEmpty()) continue
            if (index == 0 && (trimmed.startsWith("\"IdProducto\"") || trimmed.startsWith("IdProducto"))) {
                continue // Skip header row
            }

            val tokens = parseCsvLine(trimmed)
            if (tokens.size >= 8) {
                val id = tokens.getOrNull(0)?.trim() ?: ""
                val producto = tokens.getOrNull(1)?.trim() ?: ""
                val presentacion = tokens.getOrNull(2)?.trim() ?: ""
                val noSap = tokens.getOrNull(3)?.trim() ?: ""
                val unidad = tokens.getOrNull(4)?.trim() ?: ""
                val cantidad = tokens.getOrNull(5)?.trim()?.toDoubleOrNull() ?: 0.0
                val codBarras = tokens.getOrNull(6)?.trim() ?: id
                val peso = tokens.getOrNull(7)?.trim()?.toDoubleOrNull() ?: 0.0
                val pg = tokens.getOrNull(8)?.trim() ?: ""
                val pu = tokens.getOrNull(9)?.trim()?.toDoubleOrNull() ?: 0.0

                // Infer tipo: Check if there's a 11th column with explicit type, else infer from name
                val explicitTipo = tokens.getOrNull(10)?.trim()?.uppercase()
                val tipo = if (explicitTipo == "AGENTE" || explicitTipo == "ALTO_EXPLOSIVO") {
                    explicitTipo
                } else {
                    ProductClassifier.inferType(producto).name
                }

                if (id.isNotEmpty()) {
                    entities.add(
                        ProductoEntity(
                            idProducto = id,
                            producto = producto,
                            presentacion = presentacion,
                            noSap = noSap,
                            unidad = unidad,
                            cantidad = cantidad,
                            codBarras = codBarras,
                            peso = peso,
                            pg = pg,
                            pu = pu,
                            tipo = tipo
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        if (entities.isNotEmpty()) {
            productoDao.insertAll(entities)
        }
        return entities.size
    }

    override suspend fun lookupBarcode(barcode: String): ProductInfo {
        val trimmed = barcode.trim()
        if (trimmed.startsWith("X", ignoreCase = true)) {
            return ProductInfo(
                id = "ACC",
                name = "Accesorio / Artificio",
                presentation = "Pieza",
                noSap = "",
                unit = "PZA",
                weightKg = 0.0,
                type = ProductType.ACCESORIO,
                pg = ""
            )
        }

        val prefix = ProductClassifier.extractPrefix(trimmed)
        if (prefix.isNotEmpty()) {
            val entity = productoDao.getById(prefix) ?: productoDao.getByCodBarras(prefix)
            if (entity != null) {
                return entity.toDomain()
            }
        }

        return ProductInfo(
            id = if (prefix.isNotEmpty()) prefix else "???",
            name = "Material no catalogado ($prefix)",
            presentation = "Desconocida",
            noSap = "",
            unit = "KG",
            weightKg = 0.0,
            type = ProductType.DESCONOCIDO,
            pg = ""
        )
    }

    override suspend fun countProducts(): Int = productoDao.count()

    override suspend fun getAllProducts(): List<ProductInfo> =
        productoDao.getAll().map { it.toDomain() }

    companion object {
        fun parseCsvLine(line: String): List<String> {
            val tokens = mutableListOf<String>()
            val sb = java.lang.StringBuilder()
            var inQuotes = false
            var i = 0

            while (i < line.length) {
                val c = line[i]
                if (c == '"') {
                    if (inQuotes && i + 1 < line.length && line[i + 1] == '"') {
                        sb.append('"')
                        i++ // Skip escaped quote
                    } else {
                        inQuotes = !inQuotes
                    }
                } else if (c == ',' && !inQuotes) {
                    tokens.add(sb.toString())
                    sb.setLength(0)
                } else {
                    sb.append(c)
                }
                i++
            }
            tokens.add(sb.toString())
            return tokens
        }
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\domain\export\FlatFileFormatter.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.export

import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.*

object FlatFileFormatter {

    private const val CRLF = "\r\n"

    fun formatEntrada(entrada: EntradaRecord): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        val provPrefix = if (entrada.proveedor.startsWith("PRV", ignoreCase = true)) {
            entrada.proveedor
        } else {
            "PRV${entrada.proveedor}"
        }
        sb.append("$provPrefix,${entrada.polvorin},${entrada.factura}").append(CRLF)
        for (item in entrada.items) {
            sb.append("${item.code},${item.timestamp}").append(CRLF)
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }

    fun formatDespacho(despacho: DespachoRecord): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        val refPrefix = if (despacho.vale.startsWith("REF", ignoreCase = true)) {
            despacho.vale
        } else {
            "REF${despacho.vale}"
        }
        sb.append("$refPrefix,${despacho.empresa},${despacho.lugar},${despacho.transportista},${despacho.turno},${despacho.voladura}").append(CRLF)
        for (item in despacho.items) {
            sb.append("${item.code},${item.timestamp}").append(CRLF)
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }

    fun formatDevolucion(devolucion: DevolucionRecord): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        val devPrefix = if (devolucion.empleadoEntrega.startsWith("DEV", ignoreCase = true)) {
            devolucion.empleadoEntrega
        } else {
            "DEV${devolucion.empleadoEntrega}"
        }
        sb.append("$devPrefix,${devolucion.empleadoRecibe}").append(CRLF)
        for (item in devolucion.items) {
            sb.append("${item.code},${item.timestamp}").append(CRLF)
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }

    fun formatAccesorios(accesorios: AccesoriosRecord): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        val accPrefix = if (accesorios.vale.startsWith("ACC", ignoreCase = true)) {
            accesorios.vale
        } else {
            "ACC${accesorios.vale}"
        }
        sb.append("$accPrefix,${accesorios.empresa},${accesorios.lugar},${accesorios.transportista},${accesorios.turno},${accesorios.recibio},${accesorios.entrego},${accesorios.voladura}").append(CRLF)
        for (item in accesorios.items) {
            sb.append("${item.code},${item.quantity},${item.timestamp}").append(CRLF)
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }

    fun generateConsolidatedFile(
        entradas: List<EntradaRecord>,
        despachos: List<DespachoRecord>,
        devoluciones: List<DevolucionRecord>,
        accesorios: List<AccesoriosRecord>
    ): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        for (entrada in entradas) {
            sb.append(formatEntrada(entrada))
        }
        for (despacho in despachos) {
            sb.append(formatDespacho(despacho))
        }
        for (devolucion in devoluciones) {
            sb.append(formatDevolucion(devolucion))
        }
        for (accesorio in accesorios) {
            sb.append(formatAccesorios(accesorio))
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\domain\model\ProductClassification.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model

enum class ProductType(val label: String) {
    ALTO_EXPLOSIVO("Alto Explosivo"),
    AGENTE("Agente de Voladura"),
    ACCESORIO("Accesorio"),
    DESCONOCIDO("Sin catalogar")
}

data class ProductInfo(
    val id: String,
    val name: String,
    val presentation: String,
    val noSap: String,
    val unit: String,
    val weightKg: Double,
    val type: ProductType,
    val pg: String
)

object ProductClassifier {

    private val AGENT_KEYWORDS = listOf(
        "ANFO", "AMEX", "AUSTINITE", "MEXAMON", "NITRATO", "NITRO", "ANFOMIN", "ANFOOVIEDO", "ANFOREY"
    )

    fun inferType(productName: String): ProductType {
        val upper = productName.uppercase()
        for (kw in AGENT_KEYWORDS) {
            if (upper.contains(kw)) {
                return ProductType.AGENTE
            }
        }
        return ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO
    }

    fun extractPrefix(barcode: String): String {
        val trimmed = barcode.trim()
        return when {
            trimmed.startsWith("X", ignoreCase = true) -> "X"
            trimmed.length >= 3 -> trimmed.substring(0, 3)
            else -> trimmed
        }
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\domain\scanner\BarcodeItem.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner

import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductInfo
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

enum class TipoEmpaque(val label: String) {
    CAJA("Caja"),
    SACO("Saco"),
    PIEZA("Cartucho"),
    ACCESORIO("Accesorio"),
    DESCONOCIDO("Desconocido")
}

data class BarcodeItem(
    val code: String,
    val timestamp: String,
    val productInfo: ProductInfo,
    val empaque: TipoEmpaque = TipoEmpaque.DESCONOCIDO
)

data class ScanResult(
    val rawBarcode: String,
    val timestamp: String,
    val productInfo: ProductInfo,
    val isDuplicate: Boolean,
    val isValid: Boolean = true,
    val empaque: TipoEmpaque = TipoEmpaque.DESCONOCIDO,
    val unknownId: String? = null,
    val message: String
)

object DateTimeUtils {
    private val DATE_FORMAT = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US)

    fun currentTimestamp(): String {
        return DATE_FORMAT.format(Date())
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\domain\scanner\ScanValidator.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner

import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.CatalogRepository
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductType

class ScanValidator(
    private val catalogRepository: CatalogRepository
) {
    suspend fun processScan(
        rawBarcode: String,
        currentSessionCodes: Set<String>,
        allowAccessories: Boolean = false
    ): ScanResult {
        val trimmed = rawBarcode.trim().replace("\r", "").replace("\n", "")
        val timestamp = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp()

        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) {
            return ScanResult(
                rawBarcode = "",
                timestamp = timestamp,
                productInfo = catalogRepository.lookupBarcode(""),
                isDuplicate = false,
                isValid = false,
                empaque = TipoEmpaque.DESCONOCIDO,
                message = "Lectura vacÃ­a"
            )
        }

        // Accessory check (X prefix)
        if (trimmed.startsWith("X", ignoreCase = true)) {
            if (!allowAccessories) {
                return ScanResult(
                    rawBarcode = trimmed,
                    timestamp = timestamp,
                    productInfo = catalogRepository.lookupBarcode(trimmed),
                    isDuplicate = false,
                    isValid = false,
                    empaque = TipoEmpaque.ACCESORIO,
                    message = "Los accesorios (cÃ³digo X) solo se permiten en el mÃ³dulo de Salida de Accesorios."
                )
            }
            val isDuplicate = currentSessionCodes.contains(trimmed)
            return ScanResult(
                rawBarcode = trimmed,
                timestamp = timestamp,
                productInfo = catalogRepository.lookupBarcode(trimmed),
                isDuplicate = isDuplicate,
                isValid = true,
                empaque = TipoEmpaque.ACCESORIO,
                message = if (isDuplicate) "Â¡CÃ“DIGO REPETIDO! Este accesorio ya fue escaneado." else "Lectura correcta: Accesorio"
            )
        }

        // Strict length check for Alto Explosivo / Agente:
        // Cajas: 30 dÃ­gitos, Sacos: 18 dÃ­gitos, Cartuchos: 16 dÃ­gitos (compatible con 16 y 17)
        val empaque = when (trimmed.length) {
            30 -> TipoEmpaque.CAJA
            18 -> TipoEmpaque.SACO
            16, 17 -> TipoEmpaque.PIEZA
            else -> null
        }

        if (empaque == null || !trimmed.all { it.isDigit() }) {
            return ScanResult(
                rawBarcode = trimmed,
                timestamp = timestamp,
                productInfo = catalogRepository.lookupBarcode(trimmed),
                isDuplicate = false,
                isValid = false,
                empaque = TipoEmpaque.DESCONOCIDO,
                unknownId = null,
                message = "CÃ³digo invÃ¡lido (${trimmed.length} dÃ­gitos). Solo se permiten Cajas (30 dÃ­gitos), Sacos (18 dÃ­gitos) o Cartuchos (16 dÃ­gitos)."
            )
        }

        // Validate prefix exists in product catalog
        val prefix = trimmed.take(3)
        val productInfo = catalogRepository.lookupBarcode(trimmed)
        if (productInfo.type == ProductType.DESCONOCIDO || productInfo.id.isEmpty() || productInfo.id == "???" || productInfo.name.contains("no catalogado", ignoreCase = true)) {
            return ScanResult(
                rawBarcode = trimmed,
                timestamp = timestamp,
                productInfo = productInfo,
                isDuplicate = false,
                isValid = false,
                empaque = empaque,
                unknownId = prefix,
                message = "Material no reconocido. ID '$prefix' no existe en el catÃ¡logo. Por favor agrÃ©guela al catÃ¡logo para continuar."
            )
        }

        // CANDADO DE COHERENCIA DE EMPAQUE:
        // Alto Explosivo solo existe en Cajas (30 dÃ­gitos) o Cartuchos (16/17 dÃ­gitos), NUNCA en Sacos (18 dÃ­gitos).
        if (productInfo.type == ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO && empaque == TipoEmpaque.SACO) {
            return ScanResult(
                rawBarcode = trimmed,
                timestamp = timestamp,
                productInfo = productInfo,
                isDuplicate = false,
                isValid = false,
                empaque = empaque,
                unknownId = null,
                message = "CÃ³digo invÃ¡lido (18 dÃ­gitos). '${productInfo.name}' es Alto Explosivo y solo se presenta en Cajas (30 dÃ­gitos) o Cartuchos (16 dÃ­gitos), nunca en Sacos."
            )
        }

        // Agente de Voladura solo existe en Sacos (18 dÃ­gitos) o Cajas (30 dÃ­gitos), NUNCA en Cartuchos (16/17 dÃ­gitos).
        if (productInfo.type == ProductType.AGENTE && empaque == TipoEmpaque.PIEZA) {
            return ScanResult(
                rawBarcode = trimmed,
                timestamp = timestamp,
                productInfo = productInfo,
                isDuplicate = false,
                isValid = false,
                empaque = empaque,
                unknownId = null,
                message = "CÃ³digo invÃ¡lido (${trimmed.length} dÃ­gitos). '${productInfo.name}' es Agente de Voladura y solo se presenta en Sacos (18 dÃ­gitos) o Cajas (30 dÃ­gitos), no en Cartuchos."
            )
        }

        // CANDADO DE ESTRUCTURA INTERNA DE CAJAS (30 dÃ­gitos):
        // [3 ID][6 Fecha][6 Inicial][6 Final][9 Serie]
        if (empaque == TipoEmpaque.CAJA) {
            val inicialStr = trimmed.substring(9, 15)
            val finalStr = trimmed.substring(15, 21)
            val inicial = inicialStr.toLongOrNull()
            val final = finalStr.toLongOrNull()

            if (inicial != null && final != null) {
                if (final < inicial) {
                    return ScanResult(
                        rawBarcode = trimmed,
                        timestamp = timestamp,
                        productInfo = productInfo,
                        isDuplicate = false,
                        isValid = false,
                        empaque = empaque,
                        unknownId = null,
                        message = "Estructura de caja invÃ¡lida: cÃ³digo inicial ($inicialStr) no puede ser mayor al final ($finalStr)."
                    )
                }
                val countCartuchos = final - inicial + 1
                if (countCartuchos > 500) {
                    return ScanResult(
                        rawBarcode = trimmed,
                        timestamp = timestamp,
                        productInfo = productInfo,
                        isDuplicate = false,
                        isValid = false,
                        empaque = empaque,
                        unknownId = null,
                        message = "Estructura de caja invÃ¡lida: rango de $countCartuchos cartuchos excede la capacidad mÃ¡xima de embalaje."
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // CANDADO DE FECHA DE FABRICACIÃ“N PLAUSIBLE (6 dÃ­gitos):
        val fechaStr = when (empaque) {
            TipoEmpaque.CAJA, TipoEmpaque.SACO -> trimmed.substring(3, 9)
            TipoEmpaque.PIEZA -> if (trimmed.length == 16) trimmed.substring(4, 10) else null
            else -> null
        }
        if (fechaStr != null && !isValidPlausibleDate(fechaStr)) {
            return ScanResult(
                rawBarcode = trimmed,
                timestamp = timestamp,
                productInfo = productInfo,
                isDuplicate = false,
                isValid = false,
                empaque = empaque,
                unknownId = null,
                message = "Fecha de fabricaciÃ³n invÃ¡lida en el cÃ³digo ('$fechaStr'). Verifique dÃ­a y mes."
            )
        }

        val isDuplicate = currentSessionCodes.contains(trimmed)
        val message = if (isDuplicate) {
            "Â¡CÃ“DIGO REPETIDO! Este material ya fue escaneado."
        } else {
            "Lectura correcta: ${productInfo.name} (${productInfo.presentation})"
        }

        return ScanResult(
            rawBarcode = trimmed,
            timestamp = timestamp,
            productInfo = productInfo,
            isDuplicate = isDuplicate,
            isValid = true,
            empaque = empaque,
            message = message
        )
    }

    private fun isValidPlausibleDate(dateStr: String): Boolean {
        if (dateStr.length != 6 || !dateStr.all { it.isDigit() }) return false
        val p1 = dateStr.substring(0, 2).toIntOrNull() ?: return false
        val p2 = dateStr.substring(2, 4).toIntOrNull() ?: return false
        val p3 = dateStr.substring(4, 6).toIntOrNull() ?: return false

        // DDMMYY (DÃ­a 1..31, Mes 1..12)
        val isDDMMYY = (p1 in 1..31) && (p2 in 1..12)
        // YYMMDD (Mes 1..12, DÃ­a 1..31)
        val isYYMMDD = (p2 in 1..12) && (p3 in 1..31)

        return isDDMMYY || isYYMMDD
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\license\LicenseManager.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.license

import android.content.Context
import android.provider.Settings
import android.util.Base64
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.LocalDate
import java.time.OffsetDateTime
import java.time.ZoneId
import javax.crypto.Mac
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec

object LicenseManager {
    const val MASTER_SECRET = "TTEXPLO_2026_MASTER_SECRET_KEY_MINING_AUTHORIZATION_98124"
    private const val PREFS_NAME = "ttexplo_license_prefs"
    private const val KEY_TOKEN = "active_license_token"

    /**
     * Obtiene el identificador Ãºnico del hardware Android (64-bit hex).
     */
    fun getRawDeviceId(context: Context): String {
        return try {
            val id = Settings.Secure.getString(context.contentResolver, Settings.Secure.ANDROID_ID)
            if (id.isNullOrBlank()) "UNKNOWN" else id.trim()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            "UNKNOWN"
        }
    }

    /**
     * Devuelve el ID de hardware formateado en bloques legibles de 4 caracteres (ej: F020-F600-E29C-76D6).
     */
    fun getFormattedDeviceId(context: Context): String {
        val raw = getRawDeviceId(context).uppercase()
        if (raw == "UNKNOWN" || raw.length < 4) return raw
        return raw.chunked(4).joinToString("-")
    }

    /**
     * Calcula la firma HMAC-SHA256 y retorna el hash en formato hexadecimal en minÃºsculas.
     */
    fun computeHmacSha256(data: String, secret: String): String {
        val mac = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256")
        val secretKey = SecretKeySpec(secret.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8), "HmacSHA256")
        mac.init(secretKey)
        val hashBytes = mac.doFinal(data.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
        val sb = StringBuilder(hashBytes.size * 2)
        for (b in hashBytes) {
            sb.append(String.format("%02x", b.toInt() and 0xFF))
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }

    /**
     * Interpreta fechas en formato ISO-8601 o estÃ¡ndar de fecha.
     */
    fun parseIsoDate(dateStr: String): Instant? {
        if (dateStr.isBlank() || dateStr.equals("PERPETUAL", ignoreCase = true)) return null
        return try {
            Instant.parse(dateStr)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            try {
                OffsetDateTime.parse(dateStr).toInstant()
            } catch (e2: Exception) {
                try {
                    val dateOnly = if (dateStr.length >= 10) dateStr.substring(0, 10) else dateStr
                    LocalDate.parse(dateOnly).atTime(23, 59, 59).atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toInstant()
                } catch (e3: Exception) {
                    null
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Calcula los dÃ­as restantes de vigencia a partir de una fecha de caducidad.
     */
    fun getDaysRemaining(expiresStr: String): Long {
        if (expiresStr.equals("PERPETUAL", ignoreCase = true)) return 9999L
        val expiryInstant = parseIsoDate(expiresStr) ?: return 0L
        val now = Instant.now()
        val diffSeconds = expiryInstant.epochSecond - now.epochSecond
        if (diffSeconds <= 0) return 0L
        return diffSeconds / (24 * 3600)
    }

    /**
     * Valida criptogrÃ¡ficamente el token sin guardarlo aÃºn.
     */
    fun verifyToken(context: Context, tokenString: String): Result<LicenseInfo> {
        val cleanToken = tokenString.trim()
        val prefix = when {
            cleanToken.startsWith("ARIA:") -> "ARIA:"
            cleanToken.startsWith("TTEX:") -> "TTEX:"
            else -> return Result.failure(IllegalArgumentException("El cÃ³digo no tiene el formato oficial ARIA (debe iniciar con 'ARIA:')."))
        }

        val payloadAndSig = cleanToken.removePrefix(prefix)
        val dotIndex = payloadAndSig.lastIndexOf('.')
        if (dotIndex <= 0 || dotIndex >= payloadAndSig.length - 1) {
            return Result.failure(IllegalArgumentException("Estructura de licencia corrupta (falta delimitador de firma)."))
        }

        val b64Payload = payloadAndSig.substring(0, dotIndex).trim()
        val signatureHex = payloadAndSig.substring(dotIndex + 1).trim()

        val jsonString = try {
            val decodedBytes = try {
                Base64.decode(b64Payload, Base64.DEFAULT)
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                java.util.Base64.getDecoder().decode(b64Payload)
            }
            String(decodedBytes, Charsets.UTF_8)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            return Result.failure(IllegalArgumentException("Contenido de licencia ilegible o daÃ±ado: ${e.message}"))
        }

        // 1. VerificaciÃ³n de firma HMAC-SHA256
        val computedFullSig = computeHmacSha256(jsonString, MASTER_SECRET)
        val computedSig32 = computedFullSig.take(32)

        if (!computedSig32.equals(signatureHex, ignoreCase = true)) {
            return Result.failure(SecurityException("Firma criptogrÃ¡fica invÃ¡lida. La licencia no fue generada por el emisor autorizado."))
        }

        // 2. Parseo de datos JSON
        val json = try {
            JSONObject(jsonString)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            return Result.failure(IllegalArgumentException("Estructura de datos invÃ¡lida: ${e.message}"))
        }

        val id = json.optString("id", "LCN-SIN-ID")
        val client = json.optString("client", "Cliente No Especificado")
        val mine = json.optString("mine", "Faena Minera")
        val supervisor = json.optString("supervisor", "Supervisor General")
        val targetDev = json.optString("dev", "*").trim()
        val tier = json.optString("tier", "ANUAL")
        val issued = json.optString("issued", "")
        val expires = json.optString("expires", "PERPETUAL")

        val modsList = mutableListOf<String>()
        val modsArr = json.optJSONArray("mods")
        if (modsArr != null) {
            for (i in 0 until modsArr.length()) {
                modsList.add(modsArr.optString(i))
            }
        }

        // 3. VerificaciÃ³n de candado de hardware (Device ID)
        val currentRawId = getRawDeviceId(context)
        val cleanCurrent = currentRawId.replace("-", "").replace(" ", "").trim()
        val cleanTarget = targetDev.replace("-", "").replace(" ", "").trim()

        val isWildcard = targetDev == "*" || cleanTarget == "*" || cleanTarget.equals("ALL", ignoreCase = true)
        val isMatch = isWildcard || cleanTarget.equals(cleanCurrent, ignoreCase = true)

        if (!isMatch) {
            val formattedCurrent = getFormattedDeviceId(context)
            return Result.failure(
                IllegalStateException("Licencia restringida al dispositivo '$targetDev'. El ID de esta terminal es '$formattedCurrent'.")
            )
        }

        // 4. VerificaciÃ³n de caducidad
        if (!expires.equals("PERPETUAL", ignoreCase = true)) {
            val expiryInstant = parseIsoDate(expires)
            if (expiryInstant != null && expiryInstant.isBefore(Instant.now())) {
                val dateOnly = if (expires.length >= 10) expires.substring(0, 10) else expires
                return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("La licencia venciÃ³ el $dateOnly."))
            }
        }

        val info = LicenseInfo(
            id = id,
            client = client,
            mine = mine,
            supervisor = supervisor,
            deviceId = targetDev,
            tier = tier,
            issuedDate = issued,
            expiresDate = expires,
            modules = modsList,
            rawToken = cleanToken
        )
        return Result.success(info)
    }

    /**
     * Valida y guarda permanentemente el token de licencia en SharedPreferences.
     */
    fun saveLicense(context: Context, tokenString: String): Result<LicenseInfo> {
        val verification = verifyToken(context, tokenString)
        if (verification.isSuccess) {
            val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            prefs.edit().putString(KEY_TOKEN, tokenString.trim()).apply()
        }
        return verification
    }

    /**
     * Elimina la licencia activa instalada.
     */
    fun clearLicense(context: Context) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        prefs.edit().remove(KEY_TOKEN).apply()
    }

    /**
     * Comprueba el estado actual de la licencia instalada en la aplicaciÃ³n.
     */
    fun checkLicense(context: Context): LicenseCheckResult {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val token = prefs.getString(KEY_TOKEN, null)

        if (token.isNullOrBlank()) {
            return LicenseCheckResult(
                status = LicenseStatus.UNLICENSED,
                message = "La aplicaciÃ³n no cuenta con una licencia instalada. Registre una clave o escanee el cÃ³digo QR de activaciÃ³n."
            )
        }

        val verifyResult = verifyToken(context, token)
        if (verifyResult.isFailure) {
            val err = verifyResult.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "Licencia no vÃ¡lida"
            val status = when {
                err.contains("dispositivo", ignoreCase = true) || err.contains("terminal", ignoreCase = true) ->
                    LicenseStatus.DEVICE_MISMATCH
                err.contains("venciÃ³", ignoreCase = true) || err.contains("expirÃ³", ignoreCase = true) ->
                    LicenseStatus.EXPIRED
                err.contains("Firma", ignoreCase = true) ->
                    LicenseStatus.CORRUPTED
                else ->
                    LicenseStatus.UNLICENSED
            }
            return LicenseCheckResult(
                status = status,
                message = err
            )
        }

        val info = verifyResult.getOrThrow()
        val days = getDaysRemaining(info.expiresDate)

        return when {
            !info.expiresDate.equals("PERPETUAL", ignoreCase = true) && days <= 0L -> {
                val expDateOnly = if (info.expiresDate.length >= 10) info.expiresDate.substring(0, 10) else info.expiresDate
                LicenseCheckResult(
                    status = LicenseStatus.EXPIRED,
                    info = info,
                    daysRemaining = 0L,
                    message = "La licencia de ${info.client} venciÃ³ el $expDateOnly."
                )
            }
            !info.expiresDate.equals("PERPETUAL", ignoreCase = true) && days <= 30L -> {
                val expDateOnly = if (info.expiresDate.length >= 10) info.expiresDate.substring(0, 10) else info.expiresDate
                LicenseCheckResult(
                    status = LicenseStatus.EXPIRING_SOON,
                    info = info,
                    daysRemaining = days,
                    message = "AVISO DE VIGENCIA: La licencia vencerÃ¡ en $days dÃ­as ($expDateOnly). Contacte a ARIA para renovaciÃ³n."
                )
            }
            else -> {
                LicenseCheckResult(
                    status = LicenseStatus.VALID,
                    info = info,
                    daysRemaining = days,
                    message = "Licencia autorizada para ${info.client} (${info.mine})."
                )
            }
        }
    }
}
