package com.ttexplo.mobile.license

enum class LicenseStatus {
    VALID,           // Activa y con vigencia mayor a 30 dÃ­as
    EXPIRING_SOON,   // Activa pero faltan <= 30 dÃ­as para vencer
    EXPIRED,         // Fecha de vigencia superada
    DEVICE_MISMATCH, // La licencia no corresponde al Hardware ID de este telÃ©fono
    UNLICENSED,      // No hay licencia instalada
    CORRUPTED        // Firma criptogrÃ¡fica invÃ¡lida o alterada
}

data class LicenseInfo(
    val id: String,
    val client: String,
    val mine: String,
    val supervisor: String,
    val deviceId: String,
    val tier: String,
    val issuedDate: String,
    val expiresDate: String,
    val modules: List<String>,
    val rawToken: String
)

data class LicenseCheckResult(
    val status: LicenseStatus,
    val info: LicenseInfo? = null,
    val daysRemaining: Long = 0L,
    val message: String = ""
)

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\printer\BluetoothPrinterManager.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.printer

import android.Manifest
import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothAdapter
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothDevice
import android.bluetooth.BluetoothSocket
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.SettingsRepository
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.IOException
import java.util.UUID

class BluetoothPrinterManager(private val context: Context) {

    private val bluetoothAdapter: BluetoothAdapter? by lazy {
        BluetoothAdapter.getDefaultAdapter()
    }

    private val SPP_UUID: UUID = UUID.fromString("00001101-0000-1000-8000-00805F9B34FB")

    fun hasBluetoothPermission(): Boolean {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else {
            true
        }
    }

    fun isBluetoothEnabled(): Boolean {
        return bluetoothAdapter?.isEnabled == true
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    fun getPairedPrinters(): List<BluetoothDevice> {
        val adapter = bluetoothAdapter ?: return emptyList()
        if (!adapter.isEnabled) return emptyList()
        if (!hasBluetoothPermission()) return emptyList()

        return try {
            val bonded = adapter.bondedDevices.toList()
            // Prioritize printers/thermal devices at top, but show ALL bonded devices so user can pick any device
            bonded.sortedByDescending { device ->
                val name = try { device.name?.lowercase() ?: "" } catch (e: Exception) { "" }
                name.contains("zebra") || name.contains("printer") || name.contains("print") ||
                name.contains("pos") || name.contains("mpt") || name.contains("zq") ||
                name.contains("thermal") || name.contains("spp") || name.contains("bt")
            }
        } catch (e: SecurityException) {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    fun findPreferredPrinter(): BluetoothDevice? {
        val preferredMac = SettingsRepository(context).getPreferredPrinterMac()
        val adapter = bluetoothAdapter ?: return null
        if (!adapter.isEnabled || !hasBluetoothPermission()) return null

        val paired = getPairedPrinters()
        if (preferredMac.isNotBlank()) {
            val found = paired.find { it.address.equals(preferredMac, ignoreCase = true) }
            if (found != null) return found
            try {
                val direct = adapter.getRemoteDevice(preferredMac)
                if (direct != null) return direct
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // Fallback
            }
        }
        return paired.firstOrNull()
    }

    suspend fun printRawBytes(device: BluetoothDevice, data: ByteArray): Boolean {
        val result = printBytes(device.address, data)
        return result.isSuccess
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    suspend fun printBytes(
        deviceAddress: String,
        data: ByteArray
    ): Result<Unit> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val adapter = bluetoothAdapter ?: return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Bluetooth no disponible en este dispositivo"))
        if (!adapter.isEnabled) {
            return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Bluetooth apagado. Por favor enciÃ©ndalo."))
        }
        if (!hasBluetoothPermission()) {
            return@withContext Result.failure(SecurityException("Permiso de Bluetooth no concedido en el telÃ©fono."))
        }

        val device = try {
            adapter.getRemoteDevice(deviceAddress)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalArgumentException("DirecciÃ³n Bluetooth no vÃ¡lida: $deviceAddress"))
        }

        // Cancel discovery immediately before connecting to free up RFCOMM bandwidth
        try {
            adapter.cancelDiscovery()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}

        var socket: BluetoothSocket? = null
        var lastException: Exception? = null

        // Attempt 1: Standard SECURE RFCOMM socket with SPP UUID
        // Crucial for Zebra and paired Android devices: connects in ~100-200ms without security downgrade timeouts
        try {
            socket = device.createRfcommSocketToServiceRecord(SPP_UUID)
            socket.connect()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            lastException = e
            try { socket?.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            socket = null
        }

        // Attempt 2: Insecure RFCOMM socket fallback (for generic non-secure Bluetooth adapters)
        if (socket == null || !socket.isConnected) {
            try {
                socket = device.createInsecureRfcommSocketToServiceRecord(SPP_UUID)
                socket.connect()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                lastException = e
                try { socket?.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
                socket = null
            }
        }

        // Attempt 3: Reflection fallback on RFCOMM Channel 1 (classic Android POS/Zebra workaround)
        if (socket == null || !socket.isConnected) {
            try {
                val method = device.javaClass.getMethod("createRfcommSocket", Int::class.javaPrimitiveType)
                socket = method.invoke(device, 1) as BluetoothSocket
                socket.connect()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                lastException = e
                try { socket?.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
                socket = null
            }
        }

        if (socket == null || !socket.isConnected) {
            val devName = try { device.name ?: deviceAddress } catch (_: Exception) { deviceAddress }
            val errorMsg = lastException?.localizedMessage ?: "No se pudo establecer conexiÃ³n"
            return@withContext Result.failure(IOException("No se pudo conectar con $devName: $errorMsg"))
        }

        try {
            val outputStream = socket.outputStream
            // Fast direct write and flush â€” Zebra and thermal buffers handle standard ticket payloads instantly
            outputStream.write(data)
            outputStream.flush()

            // Brief delay (250ms) to ensure hardware transmit buffer is flushed before closing RFCOMM link
            Thread.sleep(250)
            Result.success(Unit)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            val devName = try { device.name ?: deviceAddress } catch (_: Exception) { deviceAddress }
            Result.failure(IOException("Fallo al transmitir datos a $devName: ${e.localizedMessage}", e))
        } finally {
            try {
                socket.close()
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\printer\TicketGenerator.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.printer

import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.AccesoriosRecord
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.DespachoRecord
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.DateTimeUtils
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream

enum class PaperWidth(val lineLength: Int) {
    WIDTH_3_INCH(42), // 80mm
    WIDTH_4_INCH(56)  // 104mm (Zebra standard)
}

object TicketGenerator {

    // Standard ASCII charset safe across all printer models and firmware
    private val CHARSET = Charsets.US_ASCII

    // Universal ESC/POS Command Constants (standard subset supported on 100% of thermal POS printers)
    private val ESC_INIT = byteArrayOf(0x1B, 0x40)
    private val ESC_ALIGN_LEFT = byteArrayOf(0x1B, 0x61, 0x00)
    private val ESC_ALIGN_CENTER = byteArrayOf(0x1B, 0x61, 0x01)
    private val ESC_ALIGN_RIGHT = byteArrayOf(0x1B, 0x61, 0x02)
    private val ESC_BOLD_ON = byteArrayOf(0x1B, 0x45, 0x01)
    private val ESC_BOLD_OFF = byteArrayOf(0x1B, 0x45, 0x00)

    // Standard ESC ! n (ESC ! 0x30 = double height + double width; ESC ! 0x00 = normal)
    // NEVER use GS ! (0x1D 0x21) as it freezes or breaks on cheap portable printers
    private val ESC_DOUBLE_SIZE = byteArrayOf(0x1B, 0x21, 0x30)
    private val ESC_NORMAL_SIZE = byteArrayOf(0x1B, 0x21, 0x00)

    // ESC d n (Print and feed n lines: universal across ESC/POS models)
    private val ESC_FEED_6_LINES = byteArrayOf(0x1B, 0x64, 0x06)

    fun stripAccents(text: String): String {
        return java.text.Normalizer.normalize(text, java.text.Normalizer.Form.NFD)
            .replace("\\p{InCombiningDiacriticalMarks}+".toRegex(), "")
    }

    data class CpclFont(
        val fontNumber: Int,
        val fontSize: Int,
        val charWidthDots: Int,
        val lineHeightDots: Int
    )

    /**
     * AutoShrink para impresiÃ³n tÃ©rmica CPCL:
     * Selecciona dinÃ¡micamente la fuente y tamaÃ±o Ã³ptimo segÃºn el ancho disponible en dots
     * para evitar truncamientos, traslapes de lÃ­neas o desbordes en papel continuo.
     */
    fun selectCpclFont(text: String, maxWidthDots: Int, preferLarge: Boolean = false): CpclFont {
        val len = text.length.coerceAtLeast(1)
        if (preferLarge && len * 16 <= maxWidthDots) {
            // Fuente grande legible (Font 4, size 0): ~16 dots/char ancho, ~32 dots alto
            return CpclFont(fontNumber = 4, fontSize = 0, charWidthDots = 16, lineHeightDots = 42)
        }
        if (len * 14 <= maxWidthDots) {
            // Fuente mediana destacada (Font 5, size 0): ~14 dots/char ancho, ~24 dots alto
            return CpclFont(fontNumber = 5, fontSize = 0, charWidthDots = 14, lineHeightDots = 32)
        }
        if (len * 11 <= maxWidthDots) {
            // Fuente estÃ¡ndar nÃ­tida (Font 7, size 0): ~11 dots/char ancho, ~24 dots alto
            return CpclFont(fontNumber = 7, fontSize = 0, charWidthDots = 11, lineHeightDots = 26)
        }
        // Fuente compacta para textos largos / cÃ³digos extensos (Font 0, size 2)
        return CpclFont(fontNumber = 0, fontSize = 2, charWidthDots = 9, lineHeightDots = 22)
    }

    fun generateDespachoEscPos(
        record: DespachoRecord,
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH,
        mineName: String = "ARIA",
        permitNumber: String = "",
        headerNote: String = "",
        includeSignatures: Boolean = true,
        includeBreakdown: Boolean = true,
        includeCodeDetails: Boolean = true
    ): ByteArray {
        val out = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        val w = paperWidth.lineLength
        val divider = "=".repeat(w)
        val thinDivider = "-".repeat(w)

        out.write(ESC_INIT)

        // Header
        out.write(ESC_ALIGN_CENTER)
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_ON)
        val cleanMine = stripAccents(mineName.ifEmpty { "ARIA" })
        out.write("$cleanMine\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        if (permitNumber.isNotBlank()) {
            val cleanPermit = stripAccents(permitNumber)
            out.write("PERMISO GENERAL: $cleanPermit\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }
        if (headerNote.isNotBlank()) {
            val cleanNote = stripAccents(headerNote)
            out.write("$cleanNote\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }
        out.write("VALE DE DESPACHO\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_OFF)
        out.write("$divider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))

        // Metadata
        out.write(ESC_ALIGN_LEFT)
        val ref = if (record.vale.startsWith("REF", ignoreCase = true)) record.vale else "REF${record.vale}"
        out.write("VALE: $ref\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("EMPRESA: ${stripAccents(record.empresa)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("LUGAR: ${stripAccents(record.lugar)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("TRANSPORTE: ${stripAccents(record.transportista)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("TURNO: ${record.turno}    VOLADURA: ${record.voladura}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        val timestamp = record.items.firstOrNull()?.timestamp ?: ""
        if (timestamp.isNotEmpty()) {
            out.write("FECHA: $timestamp\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }
        out.write("$thinDivider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))

        // Material Grouping
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_ON)
        out.write("RESUMEN DE MATERIALES:\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_OFF)

        val grouped = record.items.groupBy {
            val name = it.productName.ifEmpty { "Material ID ${it.code.take(3)}" }
            val pres = it.presentation.ifEmpty { "Pieza" }
            "$name ($pres)"
        }

        for ((productDesc, items) in grouped) {
            val count = items.size
            out.write("- ${stripAccents(productDesc)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("  CANTIDAD: $count\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }

        out.write("$thinDivider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))

        // Detailed Scanned Codes List with Timestamp
        if (includeCodeDetails && record.items.isNotEmpty()) {
            out.write(ESC_BOLD_ON)
            out.write("LISTADO DE CODIGOS ESCANEADOS (${record.items.size}):\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write(ESC_BOLD_OFF)
            out.write("$thinDivider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            record.items.forEachIndexed { index, item ->
                val num = String.format("%02d", index + 1)
                val timeStr = if (item.timestamp.isNotBlank()) item.timestamp else ""
                out.write("[$num] ${item.code}".toByteArray(CHARSET))
                if (timeStr.isNotBlank()) {
                    val pad = " ".repeat((w - num.length - item.code.length - timeStr.length - 4).coerceAtLeast(1))
                    out.write("$pad$timeStr\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
                } else {
                    out.write("\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
                }
                val desc = buildString {
                    if (item.productName.isNotBlank()) append(stripAccents(item.productName))
                    if (item.presentation.isNotBlank()) append(" (${stripAccents(item.presentation)})")
                }
                if (desc.isNotBlank()) {
                    out.write("     $desc\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
                }
            }
            out.write("$thinDivider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }

        val cajas = record.items.count { it.code.length == 30 }
        val sacos = record.items.count { it.code.length == 18 }
        val cartuchos = record.items.count { it.code.length == 16 || it.code.length == 17 }

        out.write(ESC_BOLD_ON)
        out.write("TOTAL DE PIEZAS / BULTOS: ${record.items.size}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        if (includeBreakdown) {
            out.write("CAJAS: $cajas  SACOS: $sacos  CARTUCHOS: $cartuchos\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_OFF)
        out.write("$divider\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))

        // Signatures
        if (includeSignatures) {
            out.write("ENTREGO:\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("___________________________\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("Firma Responsable Polvorin\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))

            out.write("RECIBIO:\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("___________________________\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("${stripAccents(record.transportista)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("$divider\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }

        // Print buffer and advance paper past tear bar
        out.write(ESC_FEED_6_LINES)
        out.write("\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        return out.toByteArray()
    }

    fun generateDespachoPlain(
        record: DespachoRecord,
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH,
        mineName: String = "ARIA",
        permitNumber: String = "",
        headerNote: String = "",
        includeSignatures: Boolean = true,
        includeBreakdown: Boolean = true,
        includeCodeDetails: Boolean = true
    ): ByteArray {
        val w = paperWidth.lineLength
        val divider = "=".repeat(w)
        val thinDivider = "-".repeat(w)
        val sb = StringBuilder()

        sb.append("\r\n")
        sb.append(stripAccents(mineName.ifEmpty { "ARIA" })).append("\r\n")
        if (permitNumber.isNotBlank()) {
            sb.append("PERMISO GENERAL: ").append(stripAccents(permitNumber)).append("\r\n")
        }
        if (headerNote.isNotBlank()) {
            sb.append(stripAccents(headerNote)).append("\r\n")
        }
        sb.append("VALE DE DESPACHO\r\n")
        sb.append(divider).append("\r\n")

        val ref = if (record.vale.startsWith("REF", ignoreCase = true)) record.vale else "REF${record.vale}"
        sb.append("VALE: ").append(ref).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("EMPRESA: ").append(stripAccents(record.empresa)).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("LUGAR: ").append(stripAccents(record.lugar)).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("TRANSPORTE: ").append(stripAccents(record.transportista)).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("TURNO: ").append(record.turno).append("    VOLADURA: ").append(record.voladura).append("\r\n")
        val timestamp = record.items.firstOrNull()?.timestamp ?: ""
        if (timestamp.isNotEmpty()) {
            sb.append("FECHA: ").append(timestamp).append("\r\n")
        }
        sb.append(thinDivider).append("\r\n")

        sb.append("RESUMEN DE MATERIALES:\r\n")
        val grouped = record.items.groupBy {
            val name = it.productName.ifEmpty { "Material ID ${it.code.take(3)}" }
            val pres = it.presentation.ifEmpty { "Pieza" }
            "$name ($pres)"
        }
        for ((productDesc, items) in grouped) {
            sb.append("- ").append(stripAccents(productDesc)).append("\r\n")
            sb.append("  CANTIDAD: ").append(items.size).append("\r\n")
        }
        sb.append(thinDivider).append("\r\n")

        // Detailed Scanned Codes List with Timestamp
        if (includeCodeDetails && record.items.isNotEmpty()) {
            sb.append("LISTADO DE CODIGOS ESCANEADOS (${record.items.size}):\r\n")
            sb.append(thinDivider).append("\r\n")
            record.items.forEachIndexed { index, item ->
                val num = String.format("%02d", index + 1)
                val timeStr = if (item.timestamp.isNotBlank()) item.timestamp else ""
                sb.append("[$num] ${item.code}")
                if (timeStr.isNotBlank()) {
                    val pad = " ".repeat((w - num.length - item.code.length - timeStr.length - 4).coerceAtLeast(1))
                    sb.append(pad).append(timeStr).append("\r\n")
                } else {
                    sb.append("\r\n")
                }
                val desc = buildString {
                    if (item.productName.isNotBlank()) append(stripAccents(item.productName))
                    if (item.presentation.isNotBlank()) append(" (${stripAccents(item.presentation)})")
                }
                if (desc.isNotBlank()) {
                    sb.append("     ").append(desc).append("\r\n")
                }
            }
            sb.append(thinDivider).append("\r\n")
        }

        val cajas = record.items.count { it.code.length == 30 }
        val sacos = record.items.count { it.code.length == 18 }
        val cartuchos = record.items.count { it.code.length == 16 || it.code.length == 17 }

        sb.append("TOTAL DE PIEZAS / BULTOS: ").append(record.items.size).append("\r\n")
        if (includeBreakdown) {
            sb.append("CAJAS: ").append(cajas).append("  SACOS: ").append(sacos).append("  CARTUCHOS: ").append(cartuchos).append("\r\n")
        }
        sb.append(divider).append("\r\n\r\n")

        if (includeSignatures) {
            sb.append("ENTREGO:\r\n\r\n\r\n")
            sb.append("___________________________\r\n")
            sb.append("Firma Responsable Polvorin\r\n\r\n\r\n")

            sb.append("RECIBIO:\r\n\r\n\r\n")
            sb.append("___________________________\r\n")
            sb.append(stripAccents(record.transportista)).append("\r\n")
            sb.append(divider).append("\r\n\r\n")
        }

        sb.append("\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n")
        return sb.toString().toByteArray(CHARSET)
    }

    fun generateDespachoTicket(
        despacho: DespachoRecord,
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH
    ): ByteArray = generateDespachoEscPos(despacho, paperWidth)

    fun generateTestTicket(
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH,
        mineName: String = "ARIA",
        permitNumber: String = ""
    ): ByteArray {
        val out = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        val w = paperWidth.lineLength
        val divider = "=".repeat(w)
        val thinDivider = "-".repeat(w)

        out.write(ESC_INIT)
        out.write(ESC_ALIGN_CENTER)
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_ON)
        out.write("${stripAccents(mineName.ifEmpty { "ARIA" })}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("PRUEBA DE IMPRESION TERMICA POS\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_OFF)
        out.write("$divider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write(ESC_ALIGN_LEFT)
        out.write("ESTADO: Impresora conectada OK\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("ANCHO: ${if (paperWidth == PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH) "4 Pulgadas (104mm)" else "3 Pulgadas (80mm)"}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("FORMATO: ESC/POS Termico Estandar\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        if (permitNumber.isNotBlank()) {
            out.write("PERMISO: ${stripAccents(permitNumber)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }
        out.write("FECHA/HORA: ${DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp()}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("$thinDivider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("COMUNICACION BLUETOOTH OK\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("T&T EXPLO MOBILE - Control en Sitio\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("$divider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write(ESC_FEED_6_LINES)
        out.write("\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        return out.toByteArray()
    }

    fun generatePlainTestTicket(
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH,
        mineName: String = "ARIA"
    ): ByteArray {
        val w = paperWidth.lineLength
        val divider = "=".repeat(w)
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        sb.append("\r\n")
        sb.append("${stripAccents(mineName.ifEmpty { "ARIA" })}\r\n")
        sb.append("PRUEBA MODO TEXTO PLANO (RAW)\r\n")
        sb.append(divider).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("ESTADO: Impresora conectada OK\r\n")
        sb.append("FORMATO: Texto Plano Universal (Sin comandos ESC)\r\n")
        sb.append("FECHA/HORA: ").append(DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp()).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("Si puede leer esto, su impresora funciona en modo RAW.\r\n")
        sb.append(divider).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n")
        return sb.toString().toByteArray(CHARSET)
    }

    fun generateCpclTestTicket(
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH,
        mineName: String = "ARIA"
    ): ByteArray {
        val pageWidth = if (paperWidth == PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH) 832 else 576
        val lineEnd = pageWidth - 20
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        var y = 20

        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
        lines.add("CENTER")
        lines.add("TEXT 4 0 0 $y ${stripAccents(mineName.ifEmpty { "ARIA" })}")
        y += 48
        lines.add("TEXT 5 0 0 $y PRUEBA DE IMPRESION ZEBRA CPCL")
        y += 38
        lines.add("LEFT")
        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 3")
        y += 20
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y ESTADO: CONEXION BLUETOOTH OK")
        y += 30
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y PROTOCOLO: ZEBRA CPCL INDUSTRIAL")
        y += 30
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y ANCHO DE PAPEL: ${if (paperWidth == PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH) "4 Pulgadas (104mm)" else "3 Pulgadas (80mm)"}")
        y += 30
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y FECHA/HORA: ${DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp()}")
        y += 30
        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 1")
        y += 20
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y COMUNICACION INSTANTANEA OK")
        y += 30
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y T&T EXPLO MOBILE - Control en Sitio")
        y += 35
        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 3")
        y += 40

        val totalHeight = y + 20
        sb.append("! 0 200 200 $totalHeight 1\r\n")
        sb.append("PAGE-WIDTH $pageWidth\r\n")
        for (line in lines) {
            sb.append(line).append("\r\n")
        }
        sb.append("PRINT\r\n")
        return sb.toString().toByteArray(CHARSET)
    }

    fun generateAccesoriosEscPos(
        record: AccesoriosRecord,
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH,
        mineName: String = "ARIA",
        permitNumber: String = "",
        includeSignatures: Boolean = true
    ): ByteArray {
        val out = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        val w = paperWidth.lineLength
        val divider = "=".repeat(w)
        val thinDivider = "-".repeat(w)

        out.write(ESC_INIT)

        // Header
        out.write(ESC_ALIGN_CENTER)
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_ON)
        out.write("${stripAccents(mineName.ifEmpty { "ARIA" })}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        if (permitNumber.isNotBlank()) {
            out.write("PERMISO GENERAL: ${stripAccents(permitNumber)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }
        val isDev = record.items.any { it.quantity < 0 }
        val titleText = if (isDev) "DEVOLUCION DE ACCESORIOS" else "SALIDA DE ACCESORIOS"
        out.write("$titleText\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_OFF)
        out.write("$divider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))

        // Metadata
        out.write(ESC_ALIGN_LEFT)
        val acc = if (record.vale.startsWith("ACC", ignoreCase = true)) record.vale else "ACC${record.vale}"
        out.write("VALE: $acc\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("EMPRESA: ${stripAccents(record.empresa)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("LUGAR: ${stripAccents(record.lugar)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("TURNO: ${record.turno}    VOLADURA: ${record.voladura}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("ENTREGO: ${stripAccents(record.entrego)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("RECIBIO: ${stripAccents(record.recibio)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write("$thinDivider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))

        // Items list
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_ON)
        val sectionLabel = if (isDev) "ACCESORIOS DEVUELTOS:\r\n" else "ACCESORIOS DESPACHADOS:\r\n"
        out.write(sectionLabel.toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_OFF)

        var totalPieces = 0
        for (item in record.items) {
            out.write("- ${item.code}   CANT: ${item.quantity}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            totalPieces += item.quantity
        }

        out.write("$thinDivider\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_ON)
        val totalLabel = if (isDev) "TOTAL DEVUELTO: $totalPieces PZAS\r\n" else "TOTAL ACCESORIOS: $totalPieces PZAS\r\n"
        out.write(totalLabel.toByteArray(CHARSET))
        out.write(ESC_BOLD_OFF)
        out.write("$divider\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))

        // Signatures
        if (includeSignatures) {
            out.write("ENTREGO:\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("___________________________\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("${stripAccents(record.entrego)}\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))

            out.write("RECIBIO:\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("___________________________\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("${stripAccents(record.recibio)}\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
            out.write("$divider\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        }

        out.write(ESC_FEED_6_LINES)
        out.write("\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n".toByteArray(CHARSET))
        return out.toByteArray()
    }

    fun generateAccesoriosPlain(
        record: AccesoriosRecord,
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH,
        mineName: String = "ARIA",
        permitNumber: String = "",
        includeSignatures: Boolean = true
    ): ByteArray {
        val w = paperWidth.lineLength
        val divider = "=".repeat(w)
        val thinDivider = "-".repeat(w)
        val sb = StringBuilder()

        sb.append("\r\n")
        sb.append(stripAccents(mineName.ifEmpty { "ARIA" })).append("\r\n")
        if (permitNumber.isNotBlank()) {
            sb.append("PERMISO GENERAL: ").append(stripAccents(permitNumber)).append("\r\n")
        }
        val isDev = record.items.any { it.quantity < 0 }
        val titleText = if (isDev) "DEVOLUCION DE ACCESORIOS" else "SALIDA DE ACCESORIOS"
        sb.append(titleText).append("\r\n")
        sb.append(divider).append("\r\n")

        val acc = if (record.vale.startsWith("ACC", ignoreCase = true)) record.vale else "ACC${record.vale}"
        sb.append("VALE: ").append(acc).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("EMPRESA: ").append(stripAccents(record.empresa)).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("LUGAR: ").append(stripAccents(record.lugar)).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("TURNO: ").append(record.turno).append("    VOLADURA: ").append(record.voladura).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("ENTREGO: ").append(stripAccents(record.entrego)).append("\r\n")
        sb.append("RECIBIO: ").append(stripAccents(record.recibio)).append("\r\n")
        sb.append(thinDivider).append("\r\n")

        val sectionLabel = if (isDev) "ACCESORIOS DEVUELTOS:\r\n" else "ACCESORIOS DESPACHADOS:\r\n"
        sb.append(sectionLabel)
        var totalPieces = 0
        for (item in record.items) {
            sb.append("- ").append(item.code).append("   CANT: ").append(item.quantity).append("\r\n")
            totalPieces += item.quantity
        }

        sb.append(thinDivider).append("\r\n")
        val totalLabel = if (isDev) "TOTAL DEVUELTO: $totalPieces PZAS\r\n" else "TOTAL ACCESORIOS: $totalPieces PZAS\r\n"
        sb.append(totalLabel)
        sb.append(divider).append("\r\n\r\n")

        if (includeSignatures) {
            sb.append("ENTREGO:\r\n\r\n\r\n")
            sb.append("___________________________\r\n")
            sb.append(stripAccents(record.entrego)).append("\r\n\r\n\r\n")

            sb.append("RECIBIO:\r\n\r\n\r\n")
            sb.append("___________________________\r\n")
            sb.append(stripAccents(record.recibio)).append("\r\n")
            sb.append(divider).append("\r\n\r\n")
        }

        sb.append("\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n")
        return sb.toString().toByteArray(CHARSET)
    }

    fun generateAccesoriosTicket(
        accesorios: AccesoriosRecord,
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH
    ): ByteArray = generateAccesoriosEscPos(accesorios, paperWidth)

    fun generateDespachoCpcl(
        despacho: DespachoRecord,
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH,
        mineName: String = "ARIA",
        permitNumber: String = "",
        headerNote: String = "",
        includeSignatures: Boolean = true,
        includeBreakdown: Boolean = true,
        includeCodeDetails: Boolean = true
    ): ByteArray {
        val totalItems = despacho.items.size
        val cajas = despacho.items.count { it.code.length == 30 }
        val sacos = despacho.items.count { it.code.length == 18 }
        val cartuchos = despacho.items.count { it.code.length == 16 || it.code.length == 17 }

        val grouped = despacho.items.groupBy {
            val name = it.productName.ifEmpty { "Material ID ${it.code.take(3)}" }
            val pres = it.presentation.ifEmpty { "Pieza" }
            "$name ($pres)"
        }

        val pageWidth = if (paperWidth == PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH) 832 else 576
        val lineEnd = pageWidth - 20

        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
        var y = 20

        // 1. Header con AutoShrink (sin colisiones verticales)
        lines.add("CENTER")
        val cleanMine = stripAccents(mineName.ifEmpty { "ARIA" })
        val mineFont = selectCpclFont(cleanMine, lineEnd - 20, preferLarge = true)
        lines.add("TEXT ${mineFont.fontNumber} ${mineFont.fontSize} 0 $y $cleanMine")
        y += mineFont.lineHeightDots

        if (permitNumber.isNotBlank()) {
            val permitText = "PERMISO GENERAL: ${stripAccents(permitNumber)}"
            val permitFont = selectCpclFont(permitText, lineEnd - 20, preferLarge = false)
            lines.add("TEXT ${permitFont.fontNumber} ${permitFont.fontSize} 0 $y $permitText")
            y += permitFont.lineHeightDots
        }
        if (headerNote.isNotBlank()) {
            val noteText = stripAccents(headerNote)
            val noteFont = selectCpclFont(noteText, lineEnd - 20, preferLarge = false)
            lines.add("TEXT ${noteFont.fontNumber} ${noteFont.fontSize} 0 $y $noteText")
            y += noteFont.lineHeightDots
        }
        lines.add("TEXT 5 0 0 $y VALE DE DESPACHO")
        y += 34

        // 2. Metadatos
        lines.add("LEFT")
        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 2")
        y += 16

        val ref = if (despacho.vale.startsWith("REF", ignoreCase = true)) despacho.vale else "REF${despacho.vale}"
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y VALE: $ref")
        y += 26
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y EMPRESA: ${stripAccents(despacho.empresa)}")
        y += 26
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y LUGAR: ${stripAccents(despacho.lugar)}")
        y += 26
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y TRANSPORTE: ${stripAccents(despacho.transportista)}")
        y += 26
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y TURNO: ${despacho.turno}  VOLADURA: ${despacho.voladura}")
        y += 26

        val firstTimestamp = despacho.items.firstOrNull()?.timestamp ?: ""
        if (firstTimestamp.isNotEmpty()) {
            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y FECHA: $firstTimestamp")
            y += 26
        }

        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 1")
        y += 16

        // 3. Resumen de Materiales
        lines.add("TEXT 5 0 10 $y RESUMEN DE MATERIALES:")
        y += 28

        for ((productDesc, items) in grouped) {
            val count = items.size
            val cleanDesc = "- ${stripAccents(productDesc)}"
            val descFont = selectCpclFont(cleanDesc, lineEnd - 20, preferLarge = false)
            lines.add("TEXT ${descFont.fontNumber} ${descFont.fontSize} 10 $y $cleanDesc")
            y += descFont.lineHeightDots
            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 30 $y CANTIDAD: $count")
            y += 26
        }

        // 4. Listado Detallado de CÃ³digos Escaneados con Fecha y Hora (AutoShrink)
        if (includeCodeDetails && despacho.items.isNotEmpty()) {
            lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 1")
            y += 16
            lines.add("TEXT 5 0 10 $y LISTADO DE CODIGOS ESCANEADOS ($totalItems):")
            y += 30
            lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 1")
            y += 14

            despacho.items.forEachIndexed { index, item ->
                val num = String.format("%02d", index + 1)
                val timeStr = if (item.timestamp.isNotBlank()) item.timestamp else "S/H"

                if (paperWidth == PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH) {
                    // Papel 4 Pulgadas (832 dots): CÃ³digo a la izquierda, fecha/hora a la derecha
                    lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y [$num] ${item.code}")
                    lines.add("TEXT 7 0 570 $y $timeStr")
                    y += 24

                    val desc = buildString {
                        if (item.productName.isNotBlank()) append(stripAccents(item.productName))
                        if (item.presentation.isNotBlank()) append(" (${stripAccents(item.presentation)})")
                    }
                    if (desc.isNotBlank()) {
                        val descFont = selectCpclFont(desc, lineEnd - 45, preferLarge = false)
                        lines.add("TEXT ${descFont.fontNumber} ${descFont.fontSize} 45 $y $desc")
                        y += descFont.lineHeightDots + 4
                    } else {
                        y += 6
                    }
                } else {
                    // Papel 3 Pulgadas (576 dots)
                    lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y [$num] ${item.code}")
                    y += 24
                    val desc = buildString {
                        if (item.productName.isNotBlank()) append(stripAccents(item.productName))
                        if (item.presentation.isNotBlank()) append(" (${stripAccents(item.presentation)})")
                    }
                    val line2 = if (desc.isNotBlank()) "$desc â€¢ $timeStr" else "Hora: $timeStr"
                    val descFont = selectCpclFont(line2, lineEnd - 35, preferLarge = false)
                    lines.add("TEXT ${descFont.fontNumber} ${descFont.fontSize} 35 $y $line2")
                    y += descFont.lineHeightDots + 4
                }
            }
        }

        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 1")
        y += 16

        // 5. Totales
        lines.add("TEXT 5 0 10 $y TOTAL MATERIALES: $totalItems PIEZAS")
        y += 28

        if (includeBreakdown) {
            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y CAJAS: $cajas  SACOS: $sacos  CARTUCHOS: $cartuchos")
            y += 28
        }

        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 2")
        y += 20

        // 6. Firmas
        if (includeSignatures) {
            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y ENTREGO:")
            y += 50
            lines.add("LINE 10 $y 350 $y 1")
            y += 15
            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y Firma Responsable Polvorin")
            y += 35

            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y RECIBIO:")
            y += 50
            lines.add("LINE 10 $y 350 $y 1")
            y += 15
            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y ${stripAccents(despacho.transportista)}")
            y += 30
            lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 1")
            y += 20
        }

        val totalHeight = y + 40
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        sb.append("! 0 200 200 $totalHeight 1\r\n")
        sb.append("PAGE-WIDTH $pageWidth\r\n")
        for (line in lines) {
            sb.append(line).append("\r\n")
        }
        sb.append("PRINT\r\n")
        return sb.toString().toByteArray(CHARSET)
    }

    fun generateAccesoriosCpcl(
        accesorios: AccesoriosRecord,
        paperWidth: PaperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH,
        mineName: String = "ARIA",
        permitNumber: String = "",
        includeSignatures: Boolean = true,
        includeCodeDetails: Boolean = true
    ): ByteArray {
        val totalPieces = accesorios.items.sumOf { it.quantity }
        val pageWidth = if (paperWidth == PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH) 832 else 576
        val lineEnd = pageWidth - 20

        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
        var y = 20

        lines.add("CENTER")
        val cleanMine = stripAccents(mineName.ifEmpty { "ARIA" })
        val mineFont = selectCpclFont(cleanMine, lineEnd - 20, preferLarge = true)
        lines.add("TEXT ${mineFont.fontNumber} ${mineFont.fontSize} 0 $y $cleanMine")
        y += mineFont.lineHeightDots

        if (permitNumber.isNotBlank()) {
            val permitText = "PERMISO GENERAL: ${stripAccents(permitNumber)}"
            val permitFont = selectCpclFont(permitText, lineEnd - 20, preferLarge = false)
            lines.add("TEXT ${permitFont.fontNumber} ${permitFont.fontSize} 0 $y $permitText")
            y += permitFont.lineHeightDots
        }
        val isDev = accesorios.items.any { it.quantity < 0 }
        val titleText = if (isDev) "DEVOLUCION DE ACCESORIOS" else "SALIDA DE ACCESORIOS"
        lines.add("TEXT 5 0 0 $y $titleText")
        y += 34

        lines.add("LEFT")
        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 2")
        y += 16

        val acc = if (accesorios.vale.startsWith("ACC", ignoreCase = true)) accesorios.vale else "ACC${accesorios.vale}"
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y VALE: $acc")
        y += 26
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y EMPRESA: ${stripAccents(accesorios.empresa)}")
        y += 26
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y LUGAR: ${stripAccents(accesorios.lugar)}")
        y += 26
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y TURNO: ${accesorios.turno}  VOLADURA: ${accesorios.voladura}")
        y += 26
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y ENTREGO: ${stripAccents(accesorios.entrego)}")
        y += 26
        lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y RECIBIO: ${stripAccents(accesorios.recibio)}")
        y += 26

        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 1")
        y += 16

        if (includeCodeDetails && accesorios.items.isNotEmpty()) {
            val sectionText = if (isDev) "ACCESORIOS DEVUELTOS:" else "ACCESORIOS DESPACHADOS:"
            lines.add("TEXT 5 0 10 $y $sectionText")
            y += 28

            for (item in accesorios.items) {
                lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y - ${item.code}   CANT: ${item.quantity}")
                if (item.timestamp.isNotBlank() && paperWidth == PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH) {
                    lines.add("TEXT 7 0 570 $y ${item.timestamp}")
                }
                y += 26
            }

            lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 1")
            y += 16
        }

        val totalLabel = if (isDev) "TOTAL DEVUELTO: $totalPieces PZAS" else "TOTAL ACCESORIOS: $totalPieces PZAS"
        lines.add("TEXT 5 0 10 $y $totalLabel")
        y += 28

        lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 2")
        y += 20

        if (includeSignatures) {
            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y ENTREGO:")
            y += 50
            lines.add("LINE 10 $y 350 $y 1")
            y += 15
            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y ${stripAccents(accesorios.entrego)}")
            y += 35

            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y RECIBIO:")
            y += 50
            lines.add("LINE 10 $y 350 $y 1")
            y += 15
            lines.add("TEXT 7 0 10 $y ${stripAccents(accesorios.recibio)}")
            y += 30
            lines.add("LINE 10 $y $lineEnd $y 1")
            y += 20
        }

        val totalHeight = y + 40
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        sb.append("! 0 200 200 $totalHeight 1\r\n")
        sb.append("PAGE-WIDTH $pageWidth\r\n")
        for (line in lines) {
            sb.append(line).append("\r\n")
        }
        sb.append("PRINT\r\n")
        return sb.toString().toByteArray(CHARSET)
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\scanner\ZebraScannerReceiver.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.scanner

import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.os.Build
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat

class ZebraScannerReceiver(
    private val onBarcodeScanned: (String) -> Unit
) : BroadcastReceiver() {

    override fun onReceive(context: Context?, intent: Intent?) {
        if (intent == null) return

        // Extract scanned string from various OEM / Zebra SE4710 DataWedge Intent extras
        val barcode = intent.getStringExtra("com.symbol.datawedge.data_string")
            ?: intent.getStringExtra("data")
            ?: intent.getStringExtra("scannerdata")
            ?: intent.getStringExtra("barcode_string")
            ?: intent.getStringExtra("scan_result")

        if (!barcode.isNullOrBlank()) {
            onBarcodeScanned(barcode.trim())
        }
    }

    companion object {
        val INTENT_ACTIONS = listOf(
            "com.ttexplo.mobile.SCAN_EVENT",
            "com.symbol.datawedge.data_string",
            "android.intent.ACTION_DECODE_DATA",
            "com.xcheng.scanner.action.BARCODE_DECODING_BROADCAST",
            "com.android.server.scannerservice.broadcast"
        )

        fun register(context: Context, receiver: ZebraScannerReceiver) {
            val filter = IntentFilter()
            for (action in INTENT_ACTIONS) {
                filter.addAction(action)
            }
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
                ContextCompat.registerReceiver(
                    context,
                    receiver,
                    filter,
                    ContextCompat.RECEIVER_EXPORTED
                )
            } else {
                context.registerReceiver(receiver, filter)
            }
        }

        fun unregister(context: Context, receiver: ZebraScannerReceiver) {
            try {
                context.unregisterReceiver(receiver)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // Already unregistered or not registered
            }
        }
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\ui\common\AutoShrinkText.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.common

import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.drawWithContent
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.text.TextStyle
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.TextUnit
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.theme.MineTextPrimary

/**
 * Composable reutilizable de texto auto-escalable que reduce su tamaÃ±o de fuente
 * progresivamente si el contenido excede el ancho disponible para evitar recortes o desbordes.
 */
@Composable
fun AutoShrinkText(
    text: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    color: Color = MineTextPrimary,
    maxLines: Int = 1,
    softWrap: Boolean = true,
    minTextSize: TextUnit = 8.sp,
    initialTextSize: TextUnit = 13.sp,
    targetTextSize: TextUnit = initialTextSize,
    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
    textAlign: androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign? = null,
    style: TextStyle = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium
) {
    val startSize = if (targetTextSize != initialTextSize) targetTextSize else initialTextSize
    var textSize by remember(text, startSize) { mutableStateOf(startSize) }
    var readyToDraw by remember(text, startSize) { mutableStateOf(false) }

    Text(
        text = text,
        color = color,
        maxLines = maxLines,
        softWrap = softWrap,
        fontWeight = fontWeight,
        fontSize = textSize,
        textAlign = textAlign,
        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
        style = style,
        modifier = modifier.drawWithContent {
            if (readyToDraw) {
                drawContent()
            }
        },
        onTextLayout = { textLayoutResult ->
            if (textLayoutResult.hasVisualOverflow && textSize > minTextSize) {
                val nextSize = (textSize.value - 1f).coerceAtLeast(minTextSize.value).sp
                textSize = nextSize
            } else {
                readyToDraw = true
            }
        }
    )
}

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.common

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*

import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.ProductoEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductClassifier
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductType
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.theme.*

@Composable
fun ProductEditDialog(
    initialId: String? = null,
    initial: ProductoEntity? = null,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onSave: (ProductoEntity) -> Unit
) {
    var id by remember { mutableStateOf(initial?.idProducto ?: initialId ?: "") }
    var nombre by remember { mutableStateOf(initial?.producto ?: "") }
    var presentacion by remember { mutableStateOf(initial?.presentacion ?: "") }
    var noSap by remember { mutableStateOf(initial?.noSap ?: "") }
    var unidad by remember { mutableStateOf(initial?.unidad ?: "KG") }
    var pesoText by remember { mutableStateOf(initial?.peso?.toString() ?: "25.0") }
    var isAgente by remember { mutableStateOf(initial?.tipo == ProductType.AGENTE.name) }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                text = if (initial == null) "Agregar Producto a CatÃ¡logo" else "Editar Producto",
                color = MineTextPrimary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = id,
                    onValueChange = { if (it.length <= 3) id = it },
                    label = { Text("ID Prefijo (3 dÃ­gitos)") },
                    enabled = initial == null && initialId == null,
                    singleLine = true,
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                        focusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                        unfocusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary
                    )
                )
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = nombre,
                    onValueChange = {
                        nombre = it
                        isAgente = ProductClassifier.inferType(it) == ProductType.AGENTE
                    },
                    label = { Text("Nombre del Producto (ej. EMULEX 1)") },
                    singleLine = true,
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                        focusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                        unfocusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary
                    )
                )
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = presentacion,
                    onValueChange = { presentacion = it },
                    label = { Text("Medida / PresentaciÃ³n (ej. 1 1/4\" X 16\")") },
                    singleLine = true,
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                        focusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                        unfocusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary
                    )
                )
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = noSap,
                    onValueChange = { noSap = it },
                    label = { Text("No. SAP (opcional)") },
                    singleLine = true,
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                        focusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                        unfocusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary
                    )
                )
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    FilterChip(
                        selected = !isAgente,
                        onClick = { isAgente = false },
                        label = { Text("Alto Explosivo") }
                    )
                    FilterChip(
                        selected = isAgente,
                        onClick = { isAgente = true },
                        label = { Text("Agente") }
                    )
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    if (id.length == 3 && nombre.isNotBlank()) {
                        val entity = ProductoEntity(
                            idProducto = id.trim(),
                            producto = nombre.trim(),
                            presentacion = presentacion.trim(),
                            noSap = noSap.trim(),
                            unidad = unidad.trim(),
                            cantidad = 0.0,
                            codBarras = id.trim(),
                            peso = pesoText.toDoubleOrNull() ?: 25.0,
                            pg = "",
                            pu = 0.0,
                            tipo = if (isAgente) ProductType.AGENTE.name else ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO.name
                        )
                        onSave(entity)
                    }
                },
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = MineSafetyYellow, contentColor = MineDarkBackground)
            ) {
                Text("Guardar en CatÃ¡logo", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
                Text("Cancelar", color = MineTextMuted)
            }
        },
        containerColor = MineDarkSurface
    )
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\ui\common\SoundFeedbackManager.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.common

import android.content.Context
import android.media.AudioManager
import android.media.ToneGenerator
import android.os.Build
import android.os.VibrationEffect
import android.os.Vibrator
import android.os.VibratorManager

class SoundFeedbackManager(private val context: Context) {

    private var toneGenerator: ToneGenerator? = null

    init {
        try {
            toneGenerator = ToneGenerator(AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC, 100)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Audio track may not be available on some devices
        }
    }

    private val vibrator: Vibrator? by lazy {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            val manager = context.getSystemService(Context.VIBRATOR_MANAGER_SERVICE) as? VibratorManager
            manager?.defaultVibrator
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            context.getSystemService(Context.VIBRATOR_SERVICE) as? Vibrator
        }
    }

    fun playSuccess() {
        try {
            toneGenerator?.startTone(ToneGenerator.TONE_PROP_BEEP, 120)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Ignore tone failure
        }
        vibrate(80)
    }

    fun playWarning() {
        try {
            // Low pitch tone for error or duplicate
            toneGenerator?.startTone(ToneGenerator.TONE_PROP_NACK, 350)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Ignore tone failure
        }
        vibratePattern(longArrayOf(0, 150, 80, 150))
    }

    fun playError() {
        try {
            toneGenerator?.startTone(ToneGenerator.TONE_SUP_ERROR, 500)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Ignore tone failure
        }
        vibratePattern(longArrayOf(0, 250, 100, 250))
    }

    private fun vibrate(durationMs: Long) {
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                vibrator?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createOneShot(durationMs, VibrationEffect.DEFAULT_AMPLITUDE))
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                vibrator?.vibrate(durationMs)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Ignore vibration error
        }
    }

    private fun vibratePattern(pattern: LongArray) {
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                vibrator?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createWaveform(pattern, -1))
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                vibrator?.vibrate(pattern, -1)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Ignore vibration error
        }
    }

    fun release() {
        try {
            toneGenerator?.release()
            toneGenerator = null
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            // Ignore release error
        }
    }
}

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.common

import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.DeleteForever
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Warning
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontFamily
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.theme.*

/**
 * DiÃ¡logo de confirmaciÃ³n en 2 pasos secuenciales para prevenir
 * borrados accidentales de cÃ³digos o borradores en operaciÃ³n de campo/mina.
 */
@Composable
fun TwoStepDeleteDialog(
    itemDescription: String,
    itemCode: String? = null,
    onConfirmDelete: () -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    var step by remember { mutableStateOf(1) }

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = MineDarkSurface,
        titleContentColor = MineTextPrimary,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
        icon = {
            if (step == 1) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Warning,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = MineSafetyYellow,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp)
                )
            } else {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.DeleteForever,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = MineErrorRed,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(36.dp)
                )
            }
        },
        title = {
            Column(
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
            ) {
                Surface(
                    color = if (step == 1) MineSafetyYellow.copy(alpha = 0.2f) else MineErrorRed.copy(alpha = 0.25f),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(4.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 6.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = if (step == 1) "CONFIRMACIÃ“N 1 DE 2" else "CONFIRMACIÃ“N 2 DE 2 â€¢ FINAL",
                        color = if (step == 1) MineSafetyYellow else MineErrorRed,
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                    )
                }
                AutoShrinkText(
                    text = if (step == 1) "Â¿Retirar cÃ³digo escaneado?" else "Â¡Advertencia: AcciÃ³n definitiva!",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = MineTextPrimary,
                    targetTextSize = 16.sp,
                    minTextSize = 13.sp
                )
            }
        },
        text = {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = if (step == 1) {
                        "Â¿Desea eliminar este cÃ³digo de la lista activa?"
                    } else {
                        "El cÃ³digo se eliminarÃ¡ del registro. Esta acciÃ³n no se puede deshacer. Â¿EstÃ¡ completamente seguro de borrarlo?"
                    },
                    color = MineTextSecondary,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    lineHeight = 17.sp
                )

                // Tarjeta con detalle del Ã­tem
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .background(MineDarkBackground)
                        .border(
                            1.dp,
                            if (step == 1) MineBorderGray else MineErrorRed.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
                            RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                        )
                        .padding(10.dp)
                ) {
                    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                        Text(
                            text = itemDescription,
                            color = MineTextPrimary,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            fontSize = 13.sp,
                            maxLines = 2
                        )
                        if (!itemCode.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            Text(
                                text = itemCode,
                                color = if (step == 1) MineSafetyYellow else MineErrorRed,
                                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                maxLines = 1
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            if (step == 1) {
                Button(
                    onClick = { step = 2 },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                        containerColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                        contentColor = MineDarkBackground
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "CONTINUAR (1/2)",
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        fontSize = 12.sp
                    )
                }
            } else {
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        onConfirmDelete()
                        onDismiss()
                    },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                        containerColor = MineErrorRed,
                        contentColor = MineTextPrimary
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "SÃ, ELIMINAR (2/2)",
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        fontSize = 12.sp
                    )
                }
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            OutlinedButton(
                onClick = onDismiss,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.outlinedButtonColors(
                    contentColor = MineTextSecondary
                ),
                border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, MineBorderGray)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "Cancelar",
                    fontSize = 12.sp
                )
            }
        }
    )
}

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.navigation

sealed class Screen(val route: String) {
    object Home : Screen("home")
    object EntradasHeader : Screen("entradas_header")
    object EntradasScan : Screen("entradas_scan/{prov}/{polv}/{fact}?id={id}") {
        fun createRoute(prov: String, polv: String, fact: String, id: Long = 0L): String {
            val encProv = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(prov.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encPolv = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(polv.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encFact = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(fact.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            return "entradas_scan/$encProv/$encPolv/$encFact?id=$id"
        }
    }
    object DespachoHeader : Screen("despacho_header")
    object DespachoScan : Screen("despacho_scan/{vale}/{empresa}/{lugar}/{transportista}/{turno}/{voladura}?id={id}") {
        fun createRoute(vale: String, empresa: String, lugar: String, transportista: String, turno: String, voladura: String, id: Long = 0L): String {
            val encVale = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(vale.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encEmp = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(empresa.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encLugar = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(lugar.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encTrans = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(transportista.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encVol = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(voladura.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            return "despacho_scan/$encVale/$encEmp/$encLugar/$encTrans/$turno/$encVol?id=$id"
        }
    }
    object AccesoriosHeader : Screen("accesorios_header")
    object AccesoriosScan : Screen("accesorios_scan/{vale}/{empresa}/{lugar}/{transportista}/{turno}/{recibio}/{entrego}/{voladura}?id={id}") {
        fun createRoute(
            vale: String, empresa: String, lugar: String, transportista: String,
            turno: String, recibio: String, entrego: String, voladura: String, id: Long = 0L
        ): String {
            val encVale = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(vale.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encEmp = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(empresa.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encLugar = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(lugar.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encTrans = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(transportista.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encRec = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(recibio.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encEnt = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(entrego.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encVol = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(voladura.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            return "accesorios_scan/$encVale/$encEmp/$encLugar/$encTrans/$turno/$encRec/$encEnt/$encVol?id=$id"
        }
    }
    object DevolucionAccesoriosHeader : Screen("devolucion_accesorios_header")
    object DevolucionAccesoriosScan : Screen("devolucion_accesorios_scan/{vale}/{empresa}/{lugar}/{transportista}/{turno}/{recibio}/{entrego}/{voladura}?id={id}") {
        fun createRoute(
            vale: String, empresa: String, lugar: String, transportista: String,
            turno: String, recibio: String, entrego: String, voladura: String, id: Long = 0L
        ): String {
            val encVale = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(vale.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encEmp = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(empresa.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encLugar = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(lugar.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encTrans = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(transportista.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encRec = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(recibio.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encEnt = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(entrego.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            val encVol = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(voladura.trim().ifBlank { "-" }, "UTF-8")
            return "devolucion_accesorios_scan/$encVale/$encEmp/$encLugar/$encTrans/$turno/$encRec/$encEnt/$encVol?id=$id"
        }
    }
    object Devoluciones : Screen("devoluciones?id={id}") {
        fun createRoute(id: Long = 0L): String = "devoluciones?id=$id"
    }
    object Settings : Screen("settings")
    object CatalogManagement : Screen("catalog_management")
    object ValesHistory : Screen("vales_history")
    object License : Screen("license?canBack={canBack}") {
        fun createRoute(canBack: Boolean = false): String = "license?canBack=$canBack"
    }
}

fun AccesoriosHeaderScreen(
    isDevolucion: Boolean = false,

    var voladura by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .padding(16.dp)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),

                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),

                    targetTextSize = 15.sp,
                    minTextSize = 11.sp,
                    maxLines = 2,
                    softWrap = true,
                    textAlign = androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign.Center,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.ArrowForward,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = if (isDevolucion) MineDarkBackground else MineTextPrimary
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.accesorios

import android.Manifest
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.activity.compose.rememberLauncherForActivityResult
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.compose.foundation.background

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun AccesoriosScanScreen(
    vale: String,
    empresa: String,
    lugar: String,
    transportista: String,
    turno: String,
    recibio: String,
    entrego: String,
    voladura: String,
    valeId: Long = 0L,
    isDevolucion: Boolean = false,
    viewModel: AccesoriosViewModel,
    onFinished: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val uiState by viewModel.uiState.collectAsState()

    val accentColor = if (isDevolucion) MineAgentCyan else MineAccessoryPurple
    val accentLightColor = if (isDevolucion) MineAgentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.85f) else MineAccessoryPurpleLight

    var cantidadText by remember { mutableStateOf("1") }
    var codeInput by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var itemToDelete by remember { mutableStateOf<AccessoryItemRecord?>(null) }

    val btPrintLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()
    ) { permissions ->
        val granted = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            permissions[Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT] == true
        } else true
        if (granted) {
            viewModel.printTicket { msg -> Toast.makeText(context, msg, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show() }
        } else {
            Toast.makeText(context, "Se requiere permiso de Bluetooth para imprimir", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }

    fun requestPrint() {
        val hasPerm = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else true

        if (!hasPerm && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            btPrintLauncher.launch(
                arrayOf(
                    Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT,
                    Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_SCAN
                )
            )
        } else {
            viewModel.printTicket { msg ->
                Toast.makeText(context, msg, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            }
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(isDevolucion) {
        viewModel.setDevolucionMode(isDevolucion)
    }

    LaunchedEffect(valeId) {
        if (valeId > 0L) {
            viewModel.loadExistingVale(valeId)
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(vale) {
        if (valeId <= 0L) {
            viewModel.updateHeader(vale, empresa, lugar, transportista, turno, recibio, entrego, voladura)
        }
    }

    // Hardware Zebra SE4710 listener
    DisposableEffect(context) {
        val receiver = ZebraScannerReceiver { scannedCode ->
            val qty = cantidadText.toIntOrNull() ?: 1
            viewModel.onAccessoryScanned(scannedCode, qty)
            codeInput = ""
        }
        ZebraScannerReceiver.register(context, receiver)
        onDispose {
            ZebraScannerReceiver.unregister(context, receiver)
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = {
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            text = if (isDevolucion) "DevoluciÃ³n: ${if (vale.startsWith("ACC")) vale else "ACC$vale"}" else "Accesorios: ${if (vale.startsWith("ACC")) vale else "ACC$vale"}",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = MineTextPrimary
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "$empresa â€¢ $lugar",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            color = accentColor
                        )
                    }
                },
                actions = {
                    IconButton(
                        onClick = { requestPrint() },
                        enabled = !uiState.isPrinting && uiState.items.isNotEmpty()
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Print,
                            contentDescription = "Imprimir Ticket",
                            tint = if (uiState.items.isNotEmpty()) MineSafetyYellow else MineTextMuted
                        )
                    }
                },
                colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(containerColor = MineDarkBackground)
            )
        },
        containerColor = MineDarkBackground
    ) { padding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
        ) {
            // Draft Restored Banner (Crash / Power-off recovery)
            if (uiState.wasRestoredFromDraft) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .background(accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.15f))
                        .border(1.dp, accentColor, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .padding(8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                ) {
                    Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Info, contentDescription = null, tint = accentColor)
                    Text(
                        text = if (isDevolucion) "DevoluciÃ³n en progreso recuperada (${kotlin.math.abs(uiState.totalPieces)} pzas)" else "Vale en progreso recuperado (${uiState.totalPieces} pzas)",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = accentLightColor,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                    )
                }
            }

            // Big total counter
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .background(MineDarkSurface)
                    .border(2.dp, accentColor, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {

                        text = if (isDevolucion) "TOTAL DEVUELTO" else "TOTAL ARTIFICIOS",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
