                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = "${if (isDevolucion) kotlin.math.abs(uiState.totalPieces) else uiState.totalPieces}",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.displayLarge.copy(fontSize = 50.sp),
                        color = accentColor,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold
                    )
                }

                Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End) {
                    Text(
                        text = "RecibiÃ³: $recibio",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = MineTextSecondary
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = "EntregÃ³: $entrego",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = MineTextSecondary
                    )
                }
            }

            // Input Row: Cantidad + CÃ³digo de Barras (Manual / Keystroke)
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = cantidadText,
                    onValueChange = { cantidadText = it.filter { c -> c.isDigit() } },
                    label = { Text(if (isDevolucion) "1. Cant. DevoluciÃ³n" else "1. Cantidad") },
                    modifier = Modifier.width(if (isDevolucion) 140.dp else 110.dp),
                    singleLine = true,
                    keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number, imeAction = ImeAction.Next),
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = accentColor,

                    )
                )

                    onValueChange = { codeInput = it },
                    label = { Text(if (isDevolucion) "2. CÃ³digo + ENTER" else "2. CÃ³digo (X...) + ENTER") },
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    singleLine = true,
                    keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(imeAction = ImeAction.Send),

                        }

                text = if (isDevolucion) "ACCESORIOS DEVUELTOS EN ESTE VALE:" else "ACCESORIOS EN ESTE VALE:",

                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)

                    AccessoryRowCard(

                        text = if (uiState.isPrinting) "Imprimiendo..." else "Imprimir Ticket",

                        color = accentColor,

                Button(

                        .weight(1.3f)

                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Save,

                        tint = if (isDevolucion) MineDarkBackground else MineTextPrimary
                    )

                    AutoShrinkText(
                        text = if (isDevolucion) "Guardar DevoluciÃ³n (${kotlin.math.abs(uiState.totalPieces)})" else "Guardar Vale (${uiState.totalPieces})",
                        initialTextSize = 13.sp,
                        minTextSize = 9.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = if (isDevolucion) MineDarkBackground else MineTextPrimary,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        softWrap = false
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    itemToDelete?.let { target ->
        TwoStepDeleteDialog(
            itemDescription = "Accesorio: ${target.code} (${target.quantity} pzas)",
            itemCode = "Hora: ${target.timestamp.substringAfter(" ")}",
            onConfirmDelete = {
                viewModel.deleteItem(target)
                itemToDelete = null
            },
            onDismiss = { itemToDelete = null }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun AccessorySummaryCard(
    summary: AccessorySummary,
    accentColor: androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color = MineAccessoryPurple
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .background(MineDarkSurface)
            .border(1.dp, accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.6f), RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 6.dp)) {
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = summary.code,
                initialTextSize = 13.sp,
                minTextSize = 9.sp,
                color = MineTextPrimary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                maxLines = 2,
                softWrap = true
            )
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = "${summary.countEntries} registro(s)",
                initialTextSize = 11.sp,
                minTextSize = 8.sp,
                color = MineTextSecondary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
                maxLines = 1,
                softWrap = true
            )
        }

        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                .background(accentColor)
                .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 3.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                Text(
                    text = "${summary.totalQuantity}",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = if (accentColor == MineAgentCyan) MineDarkBackground else MineTextPrimary,
                    fontSize = 15.sp
                )
                Text(
                    text = "Pzas",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = if (accentColor == MineAgentCyan) MineDarkBackground else MineTextPrimary,
                    fontSize = 9.sp
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun AccessoryRowCard(
    item: AccessoryItemRecord,
    accentColor: androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color = MineAccessoryPurple,
    onDelete: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .background(MineDarkSurface)
            .border(1.dp, MineBorderGray, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 6.dp)) {
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = item.code,
                initialTextSize = 14.sp,
                minTextSize = 10.sp,
                color = MineTextPrimary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                maxLines = 2,
                softWrap = true
            )
            Text(
                text = "Hora: ${item.timestamp.substringAfter(" ")}",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                color = MineTextMuted,
                fontSize = 12.sp
            )
        }

        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                    .background(accentColor)
                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "${item.quantity} pzas",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = if (accentColor == MineAgentCyan) MineDarkBackground else MineTextPrimary
                )
            }

            IconButton(onClick = onDelete) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Delete,
                    contentDescription = "Eliminar",
                    tint = MineErrorRed
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.accesorios

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.ttexplo.mobile.TTExploApp
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.AccesorioItemEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.AccesorioValeEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.SettingsRepository
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.AccesoriosRecord
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.AccessoryItemRecord
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.DateTimeUtils
import com.ttexplo.mobile.printer.BluetoothPrinterManager
import com.ttexplo.mobile.printer.PaperWidth
import com.ttexplo.mobile.printer.TicketGenerator
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.common.SoundFeedbackManager
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class AccessorySummary(
    val code: String,
    val totalQuantity: Int,
    val countEntries: Int
)

data class AccesoriosUiState(
    val draftId: Long? = null,
    val isDevolucion: Boolean = false,
    val vale: String = "",
    val empresa: String = "",
    val lugar: String = "",
    val transportista: String = "",
    val turno: String = "",
    val recibio: String = "",
    val entrego: String = "",
    val voladura: String = "",
    val items: List<AccessoryItemRecord> = emptyList(),
    val summaries: List<AccessorySummary> = emptyList(),
    val totalPieces: Int = 0,
    val wasRestoredFromDraft: Boolean = false,
    val isPrinting: Boolean = false,
    val lastScanMessage: String? = null
)

class AccesoriosViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {

    private val db = (application as TTExploApp).database
    private val soundFeedback = SoundFeedbackManager(application)
    val printerManager = BluetoothPrinterManager(application)
    val settingsRepository = SettingsRepository(application)

    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(AccesoriosUiState())
    val uiState: StateFlow<AccesoriosUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()

    init {
        checkForDraft()
    }

    fun loadExistingVale(id: Long) {
        if (id <= 0) return
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val vale = db.accesorioDao().getValeById(id)
            if (vale != null) {
                val dbItems = db.accesorioDao().getItemsForVale(vale.id)
                val domainItems = dbItems.map {
                    AccessoryItemRecord(
                        code = it.codigoAccesorio,
                        quantity = it.cantidad,
                        timestamp = it.fechaEscaneo
                    )
                }
                val total = domainItems.sumOf { it.quantity }
                val isDev = domainItems.any { it.quantity < 0 }
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                    draftId = vale.id,
                    isDevolucion = isDev,
                    vale = vale.vale,
                    empresa = vale.empresa,
                    lugar = vale.lugar,
                    transportista = vale.transportista,
                    turno = vale.turno,
                    recibio = vale.recibio,
                    entrego = vale.entrego,
                    voladura = vale.voladura,
                    items = domainItems,
                    summaries = calculateSummaries(domainItems),
                    totalPieces = total,
                    wasRestoredFromDraft = true
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun setDevolucionMode(isDevolucion: Boolean) {
        _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(isDevolucion = isDevolucion)
    }

    private fun checkForDraft() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draft = db.accesorioDao().getActiveDraft()
            if (draft != null) {
                val dbItems = db.accesorioDao().getItemsForVale(draft.id)
                val domainItems = dbItems.map {
                    AccessoryItemRecord(
                        code = it.codigoAccesorio,
                        quantity = it.cantidad,
                        timestamp = it.fechaEscaneo
                    )
                }
                val total = domainItems.sumOf { it.quantity }
                val isDev = domainItems.any { it.quantity < 0 }
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                    draftId = draft.id,
                    isDevolucion = isDev,
                    vale = draft.vale,
                    empresa = draft.empresa,
                    lugar = draft.lugar,
                    transportista = draft.transportista,
                    turno = draft.turno,
                    recibio = draft.recibio,
                    entrego = draft.entrego,
                    voladura = draft.voladura,
                    items = domainItems,
                    summaries = calculateSummaries(domainItems),
                    totalPieces = total,
                    wasRestoredFromDraft = true
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun updateHeader(
        vale: String, empresa: String, lugar: String, transportista: String,
        turno: String, recibio: String, entrego: String, voladura: String
    ) {
        val current = _uiState.value
        _uiState.value = current.copy(
            vale = if (vale.isNotBlank()) vale else current.vale,
            empresa = if (empresa.isNotBlank()) empresa else current.empresa,
            lugar = if (lugar.isNotBlank()) lugar else current.lugar,
            transportista = if (transportista.isNotBlank()) transportista else current.transportista,
            turno = if (turno.isNotBlank()) turno else current.turno,
            recibio = if (recibio.isNotBlank()) recibio else current.recibio,
            entrego = if (entrego.isNotBlank()) entrego else current.entrego,
            voladura = if (voladura.isNotBlank()) voladura else current.voladura
        )
    }

    private suspend fun ensureDraftCreated(): Long {
        val current = _uiState.value
        if (current.draftId != null) return current.draftId

        val newId = db.accesorioDao().insertVale(
            AccesorioValeEntity(
                vale = current.vale,
                empresa = current.empresa,
                lugar = current.lugar,
                transportista = current.transportista,
                turno = current.turno,
                recibio = current.recibio,
                entrego = current.entrego,
                voladura = current.voladura,
                fechaCreacion = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp(),
                estado = "BORRADOR"
            )
        )
        _uiState.value = current.copy(draftId = newId)
        return newId
    }

    fun onAccessoryScanned(code: String, quantity: Int) {
        val trimmed = code.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty() || quantity == 0) return

        if (!trimmed.startsWith("X", ignoreCase = true)) {
            soundFeedback.playError()
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                lastScanMessage = "Error: El cÃ³digo de accesorio debe iniciar con 'X'"
            )
            return
        }

        val finalQty = if (_uiState.value.isDevolucion) -Math.abs(quantity) else Math.abs(quantity)

        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draftId = ensureDraftCreated()
            val timestamp = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp()

            db.accesorioDao().insertSingleItem(
                AccesorioItemEntity(
                    valeId = draftId,
                    codigoAccesorio = trimmed,
                    cantidad = finalQty,
                    fechaEscaneo = timestamp
                )
            )

            val newItem = AccessoryItemRecord(
                code = trimmed,
                quantity = finalQty,
                timestamp = timestamp
            )
            val updatedItems = listOf(newItem) + _uiState.value.items
            val total = updatedItems.sumOf { it.quantity }

            soundFeedback.playSuccess()

            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                items = updatedItems,
                summaries = calculateSummaries(updatedItems),
                totalPieces = total,
                lastScanMessage = if (_uiState.value.isDevolucion) "Devuelto: $trimmed (${finalQty} pzas)" else "Agregado: $trimmed x $finalQty pzas"
            )
        }
    }

    fun deleteItem(item: AccessoryItemRecord) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draftId = _uiState.value.draftId
            if (draftId != null) {
                db.accesorioDao().deleteItem(draftId, item.code)
            }
            val updated = _uiState.value.items.filterNot { it.code == item.code && it.timestamp == item.timestamp }
            val total = updated.sumOf { it.quantity }
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                items = updated,
                summaries = calculateSummaries(updated),
                totalPieces = total,
                lastScanMessage = "Accesorio ${item.code} eliminado"
            )
        }
    }

    private fun calculateSummaries(items: List<AccessoryItemRecord>): List<AccessorySummary> {
        return items
            .groupBy { it.code }
            .map { (code, group) ->
                AccessorySummary(
                    code = code,
                    totalQuantity = group.sumOf { it.quantity },
                    countEntries = group.size
                )
            }
            .sortedByDescending { it.totalQuantity }
    }

    fun finishAndSave(onSuccess: () -> Unit) {
        val state = _uiState.value
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draftId = state.draftId
            if (draftId != null) {
                db.accesorioDao().updateEstado(draftId, "FINALIZADO")
            } else if (state.items.isNotEmpty()) {
                val newId = db.accesorioDao().insertVale(
                    AccesorioValeEntity(
                        vale = state.vale,
                        empresa = state.empresa,
                        lugar = state.lugar,
                        transportista = state.transportista,
                        turno = state.turno,
                        recibio = state.recibio,
                        entrego = state.entrego,
                        voladura = state.voladura,
                        fechaCreacion = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp(),
                        estado = "FINALIZADO"
                    )
                )
                val items = state.items.map {
                    AccesorioItemEntity(
                        valeId = newId,
                        codigoAccesorio = it.code,
                        cantidad = it.quantity,
                        fechaEscaneo = it.timestamp
                    )
                }
                db.accesorioDao().insertItems(items)
            }
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(draftId = null)
            onSuccess()
        }
    }

    fun printTicket(onComplete: (String) -> Unit) {
        val state = _uiState.value
        val pairedPrinters = printerManager.getPairedPrinters()
        if (pairedPrinters.isEmpty()) {
            onComplete("No hay dispositivos Bluetooth vinculados en Android.")
            return
        }

        val targetDevice = printerManager.findPreferredPrinter() ?: pairedPrinters.first()
        val targetName = try { targetDevice.name ?: targetDevice.address } catch (_: Exception) { targetDevice.address }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(isPrinting = true)

            val record = AccesoriosRecord(
                vale = state.vale,
                empresa = state.empresa,
                lugar = state.lugar,
                transportista = state.transportista,
                turno = state.turno,
                recibio = state.recibio,
                entrego = state.entrego,
                voladura = state.voladura,
                items = state.items
            )

            val mineName = settingsRepository.getMineName()
            val permitNumber = settingsRepository.getPermitNumber()
            val paperWidth = settingsRepository.getPaperWidth()
            val protocol = settingsRepository.getPrinterProtocol()

            // Check if target is Zebra (CPCL)
            val isZebra = protocol == "CPCL" || (protocol == "AUTO" && targetName.lowercase().let {
                it.contains("zebra") || it.contains("zq") || it.contains("rw") || it.contains("qln") || it.contains("zd") || it.contains("imz")
            })

            val bytes = when (protocol) {
                "CPCL" -> TicketGenerator.generateAccesoriosCpcl(
                    accesorios = record,
                    paperWidth = paperWidth,
                    mineName = mineName,
                    permitNumber = permitNumber,
                    includeSignatures = settingsRepository.getPrintSignatures(),
                    includeCodeDetails = settingsRepository.getPrintCodeDetails()
                )
                "PLAIN" -> TicketGenerator.generateAccesoriosPlain(
                    record = record,
                    paperWidth = paperWidth,
                    mineName = mineName,
                    permitNumber = permitNumber,
                    includeSignatures = settingsRepository.getPrintSignatures()
                )
                else -> {
                    if (isZebra && protocol != "ESCPOS") {
                        TicketGenerator.generateAccesoriosCpcl(
                            accesorios = record,
                            paperWidth = paperWidth,
                            mineName = mineName,
                            permitNumber = permitNumber,
                            includeSignatures = settingsRepository.getPrintSignatures(),
                            includeCodeDetails = settingsRepository.getPrintCodeDetails()
                        )
                    } else {
                        TicketGenerator.generateAccesoriosEscPos(
                            record = record,
                            paperWidth = paperWidth,
                            mineName = mineName,
                            permitNumber = permitNumber,
                            includeSignatures = settingsRepository.getPrintSignatures()
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            val result = printerManager.printBytes(targetDevice.address, bytes)

            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(isPrinting = false)

            if (result.isSuccess) {
                onComplete("ImpresiÃ³n exitosa en $targetName")
            } else {
                val err = result.exceptionOrNull()?.localizedMessage ?: "Fallo al conectar"
                onComplete("Error en $targetName: $err")
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        super.onCleared()
        soundFeedback.release()
    }
}

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.catalog

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun CatalogManagementScreen(
    onNavigateBack: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val dao = (context.applicationContext as TTExploApp).database.productoDao()

    var searchQuery by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var productList by remember { mutableStateOf<List<ProductoEntity>>(emptyList()) }
    var showDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var editingProduct by remember { mutableStateOf<ProductoEntity?>(null) }

    fun refresh() {
        scope.launch {
            productList = if (searchQuery.isBlank()) dao.getAll() else dao.search(searchQuery.trim())
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(searchQuery) {
        refresh()
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = {
                    AutoShrinkText(
                        text = "CatÃ¡logo de Alto y Agente",
                        color = MineTextPrimary,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        targetTextSize = 18.sp,
                        minTextSize = 14.sp
                    )
                },
                navigationIcon = {
                    IconButton(onClick = onNavigateBack) {
                        Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "AtrÃ¡s", tint = MineTextPrimary)
                    }
                },
                actions = {
                    IconButton(onClick = {
                        editingProduct = null
                        showDialog = true
                    }) {
                        Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Add, contentDescription = "Agregar Producto", tint = MineSafetyYellow)
                    }
                },
                colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(containerColor = MineDarkBackground)
            )
        },
        containerColor = MineDarkBackground
    ) { padding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
        ) {
            // Search Bar
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = searchQuery,
                onValueChange = { searchQuery = it },
                label = { Text("Buscar por ID o Nombre...") },
                leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Default.Search, contentDescription = null, tint = MineSafetyYellow) },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                singleLine = true,
                colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                    focusedBorderColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                    unfocusedBorderColor = MineBorderGray,
                    focusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                    unfocusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                    focusedLabelColor = MineSafetyYellow
                )
            )

            Text(
                text = "TOTAL PRODUCTOS: ${productList.size}",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                color = MineTextMuted,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )

            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                items(productList, key = { it.idProducto }) { prod ->
                    ProductCardItem(
                        product = prod,
                        onEdit = {
                            editingProduct = prod
                            showDialog = true
                        },
                        onDelete = {
                            scope.launch {
                                dao.delete(prod.idProducto)
                                Toast.makeText(context, "Producto ${prod.idProducto} eliminado", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                refresh()
                            }
                        }
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showDialog) {
        ProductEditDialog(
            initial = editingProduct,
            onDismiss = { showDialog = false },
            onSave = { entity ->
                scope.launch {
                    dao.insert(entity)
                    Toast.makeText(context, "Producto ${entity.idProducto} guardado", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    showDialog = false
                    refresh()
                }
            }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ProductCardItem(
    product: ProductoEntity,
    onEdit: () -> Unit,
    onDelete: () -> Unit
) {
    val isAlto = product.tipo == ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO.name
    val badgeColor = if (isAlto) MineHighExplosiveOrange else MineAgentCyan

    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MineDarkSurface),
        border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, badgeColor.copy(alpha = 0.4f))
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(12.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                            .background(badgeColor)
                            .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "ID: ${product.idProducto}",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = MineDarkBackground
                        )
                    }
                    AutoShrinkText(
                        text = product.producto,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = MineTextPrimary,
                        targetTextSize = 14.sp,
                        minTextSize = 10.sp,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false)
                    )
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                AutoShrinkText(
                    text = "Medida: ${product.presentacion} â€¢ SAP: ${product.noSap}",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                    color = MineTextSecondary,
                    targetTextSize = 12.sp,
                    minTextSize = 9.sp
                )
                AutoShrinkText(
                    text = "Tipo: ${if (isAlto) "Alto Explosivo" else "Agente de Voladura"} â€¢ ${product.peso} ${product.unidad}",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                    color = MineSafetyYellow,
                    targetTextSize = 12.sp,
                    minTextSize = 9.sp
                )
            }

            Row {
                IconButton(onClick = onEdit) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.Edit, contentDescription = "Editar", tint = MineSafetyYellow)
                }
                IconButton(onClick = onDelete) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.Delete, contentDescription = "Eliminar", tint = MineErrorRed)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

                    keyboardType = KeyboardType.Text,

                    capitalization = KeyboardCapitalization.Words
                ),
                keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(onNext = { focusManager.moveFocus(FocusDirection.Down) }),
                colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                    focusedBorderColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                    unfocusedBorderColor = MineBorderGray,
                    focusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                    unfocusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                    focusedLabelColor = MineSafetyYellow
                )
            )

            // Turno & Voladura row
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = turno,
                    onValueChange = { turno = it },
                    label = { Text("Turno (1, 2, 3)") },
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    singleLine = true,
                    keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number, imeAction = ImeAction.Next),
                    keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(onNext = { focusManager.moveFocus(FocusDirection.Right) }),
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                        unfocusedBorderColor = MineBorderGray,
                        focusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                        unfocusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                        focusedLabelColor = MineSafetyYellow
                    )
                )

                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = voladura,
                    onValueChange = { voladura = it },
                    label = { Text("Voladura / Mina") },
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    singleLine = true,
                    keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Text, imeAction = ImeAction.Done),
                    keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(
                        onDone = {
                            focusManager.clearFocus()
                            if (vale.isNotBlank() && empresa.isNotBlank() && lugar.isNotBlank() && transportista.isNotBlank()) {
                                onContinueToScan(vale.trim(), empresa.trim(), lugar.trim(), transportista.trim(), turno.trim(), voladura.trim())
                            } else {
                                showError = true
                            }
                        }
                    ),
                    colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedBorderColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                        unfocusedBorderColor = MineBorderGray,
                        focusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                        unfocusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                        focusedLabelColor = MineSafetyYellow
                    )
                )
            }

            if (showError) {
                Text(
                    text = "Por favor capture los campos principales (Vale, Empresa, Lugar y Transportista).",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    color = MineErrorRed
                )
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            Button(
                onClick = {
                    if (vale.isNotBlank() && empresa.isNotBlank() && lugar.isNotBlank() && transportista.isNotBlank()) {
                        onContinueToScan(vale.trim(), empresa.trim(), lugar.trim(), transportista.trim(), turno.trim(), voladura.trim())
                    } else {
                        showError = true
                    }
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(56.dp),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = MineHighExplosiveOrange)
            ) {
                AutoShrinkText(
                    text = "CONTINUAR A DESPACHO â†µ",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = MineTextPrimary,
                    targetTextSize = 15.sp,
                    minTextSize = 12.sp
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.ArrowForward,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = MineTextPrimary
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun DespachoScanScreen(
    vale: String,
    empresa: String,
    lugar: String,
    transportista: String,
    turno: String,
    voladura: String,
    despachoId: Long = 0L,
    viewModel: DespachoViewModel,
    onFinished: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val uiState by viewModel.uiState.collectAsState()
    var manualInput by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var showCatalogDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var catalogAddInitialId by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var showPrinterPickerDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var preferredPrinterName by remember { mutableStateOf(viewModel.settingsRepository.getPreferredPrinterName()) }
    var itemToDelete by remember { mutableStateOf<BarcodeItem?>(null) }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        preferredPrinterName = viewModel.settingsRepository.getPreferredPrinterName()
    }

    val btPrintLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()
    ) { permissions ->
        val granted = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            permissions[Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT] == true
        } else true
        if (granted) {
            viewModel.printTicket { msg -> Toast.makeText(context, msg, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show() }
        } else {
            Toast.makeText(context, "Se requiere permiso de Bluetooth para imprimir", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }

    fun requestPrint() {
        val hasPerm = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else true

        if (!hasPerm && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            btPrintLauncher.launch(
                arrayOf(
                    Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT,
                    Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_SCAN
                )
            )
        } else {
            val paired = viewModel.printerManager.getPairedPrinters()
            if (paired.isEmpty()) {
                Toast.makeText(context, "No hay dispositivos Bluetooth vinculados en Android. ConÃ©ctelo en Ajustes Bluetooth.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                return
            }

            val currentMac = viewModel.settingsRepository.getPreferredPrinterMac()
            if (currentMac.isBlank() && paired.size > 1) {
                showPrinterPickerDialog = true
            } else {
                if (currentMac.isBlank() && paired.size == 1) {
                    val single = paired.first()
                    val sName = try { single.name ?: single.address } catch (_: Exception) { single.address }
                    viewModel.savePreferredPrinter(single.address, sName)
                    preferredPrinterName = sName
                }
                viewModel.printTicket { msg ->
                    Toast.makeText(context, msg, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(despachoId) {
        if (despachoId > 0L) {
            viewModel.loadExistingDespacho(despachoId)
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(vale, empresa, lugar, transportista, turno, voladura) {
        if (despachoId <= 0L) {
            viewModel.updateHeader(vale, empresa, lugar, transportista, turno, voladura)
        }
    }

    // Register hardware Zebra SE4710 broadcast receiver
    DisposableEffect(context) {
        val receiver = ZebraScannerReceiver { scannedBarcode ->
            viewModel.onBarcodeScanned(scannedBarcode)
        }
        ZebraScannerReceiver.register(context, receiver)
        onDispose {
            ZebraScannerReceiver.unregister(context, receiver)
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = {
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            text = "Vale: ${if (vale.startsWith("REF")) vale else "REF$vale"}",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = MineTextPrimary
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "$empresa â€¢ $lugar",

                        )
                    }

            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
        ) {
            // Draft Restored Banner (Crash / Power-off recovery)
            if (uiState.wasRestoredFromDraft) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                        .background(MineHighExplosiveOrange.copy(alpha = 0.15f))
                        .border(1.dp, MineHighExplosiveOrange, RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))

                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)

                        text = "Vale recuperado (${uiState.scannedItems.size} Ã­tems)",

                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                    )
                }
            }

            // CONTADOR COMPACTO OPTIMIZADO (TOTAL, CAJAS, SACOS, CARTUCHOS)
            val totalCajas = uiState.scannedItems.count { it.empaque == TipoEmpaque.CAJA }
            val totalSacos = uiState.scannedItems.count { it.empaque == TipoEmpaque.SACO }
            val totalCartuchos = uiState.scannedItems.count { it.empaque == TipoEmpaque.PIEZA }

            // 1. CASILLA DE ESCANEO COMPACTA

            }

            var selectedMarkerKey by remember { mutableStateOf<Triple<String, String, TipoEmpaque>?>(null) }

            val filteredItems = remember(uiState.scannedItems, selectedMarkerKey) {
                if (selectedMarkerKey == null) {
                    uiState.scannedItems
                } else {
                    val (name, pres, emp) = selectedMarkerKey!!

            // Barra de estado de impresora rÃ¡pida
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically

                Row(

                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,

                    Icon(

    }

    if (showPrinterPickerDialog) {
        val pairedDevices = remember { viewModel.printerManager.getPairedPrinters() }
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showPrinterPickerDialog = false },
            title = {

            },
            text = {
                if (pairedDevices.isEmpty()) {

                    )
                } else {

                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().heightIn(max = 280.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                    ) {
                        items(pairedDevices) { device ->
                            val devName = try { device.name ?: device.address } catch (_: Exception) { device.address }
                            val isSelected = device.address.equals(viewModel.settingsRepository.getPreferredPrinterMac(), ignoreCase = true)

                            Card(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .clickable {
                                        viewModel.savePreferredPrinter(device.address, devName)
                                        preferredPrinterName = devName
                                        showPrinterPickerDialog = false
                                        // Mandar a imprimir de inmediato con la impresora seleccionada
                                        viewModel.printTicket { msg ->
                                            Toast.makeText(context, msg, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                                        }
                                    },
                                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = if (isSelected) MineSafetyYellow.copy(alpha = 0.15f) else MineDarkSurface),
                                border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, if (isSelected) MineSafetyYellow else MineBorderGray),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(10.dp),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                        Text(devName, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = MineTextPrimary, fontSize = 14.sp)
                                        Text(device.address, color = MineTextMuted, fontSize = 11.sp)
                                    }
                                    if (isSelected) {
                                        Icon(Icons.Default.Check, contentDescription = null, tint = MineSafetyYellow)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { showPrinterPickerDialog = false }) {
                    Text("Cerrar", color = MineSafetyYellow)
                }
            },
            containerColor = MineDarkBackground
        )
    }

    if (showCatalogDialog) {
        ProductEditDialog(
            initialId = catalogAddInitialId,
            initial = null,
            onDismiss = { showCatalogDialog = false },
            onSave = { entity ->
                viewModel.registerProductInCatalog(entity)
                showCatalogDialog = false
            }
        )
    }

    itemToDelete?.let { target ->
        TwoStepDeleteDialog(
            itemDescription = "${target.productInfo.name} (${target.productInfo.presentation})",
            itemCode = "CÃ³d: ${target.code}",
            onConfirmDelete = {
                viewModel.deleteItem(target)
                itemToDelete = null
            },
            onDismiss = { itemToDelete = null }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun CompactDespachoMetric(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    label: String,
    count: Int,
    borderColor: Color,
    countColor: Color
) {
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .background(MineDarkBackground)
            .border(1.dp, borderColor, RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .padding(vertical = 4.dp, horizontal = 2.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Text(
            text = "$count",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontSize = 18.sp),
            color = countColor,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold
        )
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontSize = 9.sp),
            color = MineTextMuted,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun DespachoProductCounterCard(
    marker: ProductMarker,
    isSelected: Boolean = false,
    onClick: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    // Colores normalizados segÃºn el tipo de empaque:
    // CAJA: Amarillo Oro (#FFD600)
    // SACO: Cian ElÃ©ctrico (#00E5FF)
    // CARTUCHO / PIEZA: Coral / Naranja Rojizo (#FF916F)
    // ACCESORIO: Morado (#BA68C8)
    val empaqueColor = when (marker.empaque) {
        TipoEmpaque.CAJA -> EmpaqueCajaColor
        TipoEmpaque.SACO -> EmpaqueSacoColor
        TipoEmpaque.PIEZA -> EmpaqueCartuchoColor
        TipoEmpaque.ACCESORIO -> EmpaqueAccesorioColor
        else -> MineBorderGray
    }
    val empaqueLabel = when (marker.empaque) {
        TipoEmpaque.SACO -> "Sacos"
        TipoEmpaque.PIEZA -> "Cartuchos"
        else -> "Cajas"
    }

    val borderWidth = if (isSelected) 2.dp else 1.dp
    val borderColor = if (isSelected) empaqueColor else empaqueColor.copy(alpha = 0.7f)
    val cardBg = if (isSelected) empaqueColor.copy(alpha = 0.18f) else MineDarkSurface

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .background(cardBg)
            .border(borderWidth, borderColor, RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        // Badge de conteo siempre a la izquierda con el color de su empaque
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                .background(empaqueColor)
                .padding(horizontal = 5.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "${marker.count}",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = MineDarkBackground
                )
                Text(
                    text = empaqueLabel,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = MineDarkBackground,
                    fontSize = 9.sp
                )
            }
        }

        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = marker.productName,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                color = if (isSelected) empaqueColor else MineTextPrimary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                maxLines = 1
            )
            Text(
                text = if (marker.presentation.isNotBlank()) marker.presentation else marker.type.label,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                color = MineTextSecondary,
                maxLines = 1
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun DespachoScannedItemRow(
    item: BarcodeItem,
    onDelete: () -> Unit
) {
    val empaqueColor = when (item.empaque) {
        TipoEmpaque.CAJA -> EmpaqueCajaColor
        TipoEmpaque.SACO -> EmpaqueSacoColor
        TipoEmpaque.PIEZA -> EmpaqueCartuchoColor
        TipoEmpaque.ACCESORIO -> EmpaqueAccesorioColor
        else -> MineBorderGray
    }

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .background(MineDarkSurface)
            .border(1.dp, empaqueColor.copy(alpha = 0.6f), RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 6.dp)) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                        .background(empaqueColor.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                        .border(0.5.dp, empaqueColor, RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                        .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 1.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = item.empaque.label,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                        color = empaqueColor,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        fontSize = 10.sp,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        softWrap = false
                    )
                }
                AutoShrinkText(
                    text = item.productInfo.name,
                    initialTextSize = 13.sp,
                    minTextSize = 9.sp,
                    color = MineTextPrimary,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    maxLines = 1
                )
            }
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = "Medida: ${item.productInfo.presentation} â€¢ ${item.productInfo.type.label}",
                initialTextSize = 11.sp,
                minTextSize = 8.sp,
                color = MineTextSecondary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
                maxLines = 1
            )
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = "CÃ³d: ${item.code}",
                initialTextSize = 11.sp,
                minTextSize = 8.sp,
                color = MineTextSecondary,
                maxLines = 1
            )
        }

        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = item.timestamp.substringAfter(" "),
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                color = MineTextMuted,
                fontSize = 12.sp
            )
            IconButton(onClick = onDelete) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Delete,
                    contentDescription = "Eliminar",
                    tint = MineErrorRed
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\ui\screens\despacho\DespachoViewModel.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.despacho

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.ttexplo.mobile.TTExploApp
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.DespachoEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.DespachoItemEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.ProductoEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.CatalogRepositoryImpl
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.SettingsRepository
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.DespachoRecord
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductInfo
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductType
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ScannedCodeRecord
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.BarcodeItem
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.DateTimeUtils
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.ScanResult
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.ScanValidator
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.TipoEmpaque
import com.ttexplo.mobile.printer.BluetoothPrinterManager
import com.ttexplo.mobile.printer.TicketGenerator
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.common.SoundFeedbackManager
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class ProductMarker(
    val productName: String,
    val presentation: String,
    val count: Int,
    val type: ProductType = ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO,
    val empaque: TipoEmpaque = TipoEmpaque.CAJA
)

data class DespachoUiState(
    val draftId: Long? = null,
    val vale: String = "",
    val empresa: String = "",
    val lugar: String = "",
    val transportista: String = "",
    val turno: String = "",
    val voladura: String = "",
    val scannedItems: List<BarcodeItem> = emptyList(),
    val latestScanResult: ScanResult? = null,
    val markers: List<ProductMarker> = emptyList(),
    val wasRestoredFromDraft: Boolean = false,
    val isPrinting: Boolean = false,
    val printStatusMessage: String? = null
)

class DespachoViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {

    private val db = (application as TTExploApp).database
    private val catalogRepository = CatalogRepositoryImpl(db.productoDao())
    val settingsRepository = SettingsRepository(application)
    private val scanValidator = ScanValidator(catalogRepository)
    private val soundFeedback = SoundFeedbackManager(application)
    val printerManager = BluetoothPrinterManager(application)

    fun savePreferredPrinter(mac: String, name: String) {
        settingsRepository.setPreferredPrinterMac(mac)
        settingsRepository.setPreferredPrinterName(name)
    }

    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(DespachoUiState())
    val uiState: StateFlow<DespachoUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()

    init {
        checkForDraft()
    }

    fun loadExistingDespacho(id: Long) {
        if (id <= 0) return
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val despacho = db.despachoDao().getDespachoById(id)
            if (despacho != null) {
                val dbItems = db.despachoDao().getItemsForDespacho(despacho.id)
                val domainItems = dbItems.map { entity ->
                    val product = catalogRepository.lookupBarcode(entity.codigoBarras)
                    BarcodeItem(
                        code = entity.codigoBarras,
                        timestamp = entity.fechaEscaneo,
                        productInfo = product,
                        empaque = when (entity.codigoBarras.length) {
                            30 -> TipoEmpaque.CAJA
                            18 -> TipoEmpaque.SACO
                            16, 17 -> TipoEmpaque.PIEZA
                            else -> TipoEmpaque.DESCONOCIDO
                        }
                    )
                }
                val markers = calculateMarkers(domainItems)
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                    draftId = despacho.id,
                    vale = despacho.vale,
                    empresa = despacho.empresa,
                    lugar = despacho.lugar,
                    transportista = despacho.transportista,
                    turno = despacho.turno,
                    voladura = despacho.voladura,
                    scannedItems = domainItems,
                    markers = markers,
                    wasRestoredFromDraft = true
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun registerProductInCatalog(entity: ProductoEntity) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            db.productoDao().insert(entity)
            val pendingCode = _uiState.value.latestScanResult?.rawBarcode
            if (!pendingCode.isNullOrBlank() && _uiState.value.latestScanResult?.unknownId == entity.idProducto) {
                onBarcodeScanned(pendingCode)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun checkForDraft() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draft = db.despachoDao().getActiveDraft()
            if (draft != null) {
                val dbItems = db.despachoDao().getItemsForDespacho(draft.id)
                val domainItems = dbItems.map { entity ->
                    val product = catalogRepository.lookupBarcode(entity.codigoBarras)
                    BarcodeItem(
                        code = entity.codigoBarras,
                        timestamp = entity.fechaEscaneo,
                        productInfo = product,
                        empaque = when (entity.codigoBarras.length) {
                            30 -> TipoEmpaque.CAJA
                            18 -> TipoEmpaque.SACO
                            16, 17 -> TipoEmpaque.PIEZA
                            else -> TipoEmpaque.DESCONOCIDO
                        }
                    )
                }
                val markers = calculateMarkers(domainItems)
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                    draftId = draft.id,
                    vale = draft.vale,
                    empresa = draft.empresa,
                    lugar = draft.lugar,
                    transportista = draft.transportista,
                    turno = draft.turno,
                    voladura = draft.voladura,
                    scannedItems = domainItems,
                    markers = markers,
                    wasRestoredFromDraft = true
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun updateHeader(
        vale: String, empresa: String, lugar: String,
        transportista: String, turno: String, voladura: String
    ) {
        val current = _uiState.value
        _uiState.value = current.copy(
            vale = if (vale.isNotBlank()) vale else current.vale,
            empresa = if (empresa.isNotBlank()) empresa else current.empresa,
            lugar = if (lugar.isNotBlank()) lugar else current.lugar,
            transportista = if (transportista.isNotBlank()) transportista else current.transportista,
            turno = if (turno.isNotBlank()) turno else current.turno,
            voladura = if (voladura.isNotBlank()) voladura else current.voladura
        )
    }

    private suspend fun ensureDraftCreated(): Long {
        val current = _uiState.value
        if (current.draftId != null) return current.draftId

        val newId = db.despachoDao().insertDespacho(
            DespachoEntity(
                vale = current.vale,
                empresa = current.empresa,
                lugar = current.lugar,
                transportista = current.transportista,
                turno = current.turno,
                voladura = current.voladura,
                fechaCreacion = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp(),
                estado = "BORRADOR"
            )
        )
        _uiState.value = current.copy(draftId = newId)
        return newId
    }

    fun onBarcodeScanned(barcode: String) {
        val trimmed = barcode.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return

        viewModelScope.launch {
            val sessionCodes = _uiState.value.scannedItems.map { it.code }.toSet()
            val result = scanValidator.processScan(trimmed, sessionCodes, allowAccessories = false)

            if (!result.isValid) {
                soundFeedback.playError()
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(latestScanResult = result)
                return@launch
            }

            if (result.isDuplicate) {
                soundFeedback.playWarning()
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(latestScanResult = result)
            } else {
                soundFeedback.playSuccess()
                val draftId = ensureDraftCreated()

                // Persist immediately in SQLite draft
                db.despachoDao().insertSingleItem(
                    DespachoItemEntity(
                        despachoId = draftId,
                        codigoBarras = result.rawBarcode,
                        fechaEscaneo = result.timestamp,
                        productoId = result.productInfo.id,
                        productoNombre = result.productInfo.name,
                        presentacion = result.productInfo.presentation,
                        tipo = result.productInfo.type.name
                    )
                )

                val newItem = BarcodeItem(
                    code = result.rawBarcode,
                    timestamp = result.timestamp,
                    productInfo = result.productInfo,
                    empaque = result.empaque
                )
                val updatedList = listOf(newItem) + _uiState.value.scannedItems
                val updatedMarkers = calculateMarkers(updatedList)

                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                    scannedItems = updatedList,
                    markers = updatedMarkers,
                    latestScanResult = result
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun deleteItem(item: BarcodeItem) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draftId = _uiState.value.draftId
            if (draftId != null) {
                db.despachoDao().deleteItem(draftId, item.code)
            }
            val updated = _uiState.value.scannedItems.filterNot { it.code == item.code }
            val markers = calculateMarkers(updated)
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                scannedItems = updated,
                markers = markers,
                latestScanResult = ScanResult(
                    rawBarcode = item.code,
                    timestamp = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp(),
                    productInfo = item.productInfo,
                    isDuplicate = false,
                    isValid = true,
                    message = "CÃ³digo ${item.code} eliminado"
                )
            )
        }
    }

    private fun calculateMarkers(items: List<BarcodeItem>): List<ProductMarker> {
        return items
            .groupBy { Triple(it.productInfo.name, it.productInfo.presentation, it.empaque) }
            .map { (key, group) ->
                ProductMarker(
                    productName = key.first,
                    presentation = key.second,
                    count = group.size,
                    type = group.firstOrNull()?.productInfo?.type ?: ProductType.DESCONOCIDO,
                    empaque = key.third
                )
            }
            .sortedWith(compareBy<ProductMarker> { it.empaque.ordinal }.thenByDescending { it.count })
    }

    fun finishAndSave(onSuccess: () -> Unit) {
        val state = _uiState.value
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draftId = state.draftId
            if (draftId != null) {
                db.despachoDao().updateEstado(draftId, "FINALIZADO")
            } else if (state.scannedItems.isNotEmpty()) {
                val newId = db.despachoDao().insertDespacho(
                    DespachoEntity(
                        vale = state.vale,
                        empresa = state.empresa,
                        lugar = state.lugar,
                        transportista = state.transportista,
                        turno = state.turno,
                        voladura = state.voladura,
                        fechaCreacion = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp(),
                        estado = "FINALIZADO"
                    )
                )
                val items = state.scannedItems.map {
                    DespachoItemEntity(
                        despachoId = newId,
                        codigoBarras = it.code,
                        fechaEscaneo = it.timestamp,
                        productoId = it.productInfo.id,
                        productoNombre = it.productInfo.name,
                        presentacion = it.productInfo.presentation,
                        tipo = it.productInfo.type.name
                    )
                }
                db.despachoDao().insertItems(items)
            }
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(draftId = null)
            onSuccess()
        }
    }

    fun printTicket(onComplete: (String) -> Unit) {
        val state = _uiState.value
        val pairedPrinters = printerManager.getPairedPrinters()
        if (pairedPrinters.isEmpty()) {
            onComplete("No hay dispositivos Bluetooth vinculados en Android.")
            return
        }

        val targetDevice = printerManager.findPreferredPrinter() ?: pairedPrinters.first()
        val targetName = try { targetDevice.name ?: targetDevice.address } catch (_: Exception) { targetDevice.address }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(isPrinting = true)

            val record = DespachoRecord(
                vale = state.vale,
                empresa = state.empresa,
                lugar = state.lugar,
                transportista = state.transportista,
                turno = state.turno,
                voladura = state.voladura,
                items = state.scannedItems.map {
                    ScannedCodeRecord(
                        code = it.code,
                        timestamp = it.timestamp,
                        productName = it.productInfo.name,
                        presentation = it.productInfo.presentation
                    )
                }
            )

            val mineName = settingsRepository.getMineName()
            val permitNumber = settingsRepository.getPermitNumber()
            val ticketHeader = settingsRepository.getTicketHeader()
            val paperWidth = settingsRepository.getPaperWidth()
            val protocol = settingsRepository.getPrinterProtocol()

            // Check if target is Zebra (CPCL)
            val isZebra = protocol == "CPCL" || (protocol == "AUTO" && targetName.lowercase().let {
                it.contains("zebra") || it.contains("zq") || it.contains("rw") || it.contains("qln") || it.contains("zd") || it.contains("imz")
            })

            val bytes = when (protocol) {
                "CPCL" -> TicketGenerator.generateDespachoCpcl(
                    despacho = record,
                    paperWidth = paperWidth,
                    mineName = mineName,
                    permitNumber = permitNumber,
                    headerNote = ticketHeader,
                    includeSignatures = settingsRepository.getPrintSignatures(),
                    includeBreakdown = settingsRepository.getPrintBreakdown(),
                    includeCodeDetails = settingsRepository.getPrintCodeDetails()
                )
                "PLAIN" -> TicketGenerator.generateDespachoPlain(
                    record = record,
                    paperWidth = paperWidth,
                    mineName = mineName,
                    permitNumber = permitNumber,
                    headerNote = ticketHeader,
                    includeSignatures = settingsRepository.getPrintSignatures(),
                    includeBreakdown = settingsRepository.getPrintBreakdown(),
                    includeCodeDetails = settingsRepository.getPrintCodeDetails()
                )
                else -> {
                    if (isZebra && protocol != "ESCPOS") {
                        TicketGenerator.generateDespachoCpcl(
                            despacho = record,
                            paperWidth = paperWidth,
                            mineName = mineName,
                            permitNumber = permitNumber,
                            headerNote = ticketHeader,
                            includeSignatures = settingsRepository.getPrintSignatures(),
                            includeBreakdown = settingsRepository.getPrintBreakdown(),
                            includeCodeDetails = settingsRepository.getPrintCodeDetails()
                        )
                    } else {
                        TicketGenerator.generateDespachoEscPos(
                            record = record,
                            paperWidth = paperWidth,
                            mineName = mineName,
                            permitNumber = permitNumber,
                            headerNote = ticketHeader,
                            includeSignatures = settingsRepository.getPrintSignatures(),
                            includeBreakdown = settingsRepository.getPrintBreakdown(),
                            includeCodeDetails = settingsRepository.getPrintCodeDetails()
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            val result = printerManager.printBytes(targetDevice.address, bytes)

            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                isPrinting = false,
                printStatusMessage = if (result.isSuccess) "Ticket impreso en $targetName" else "Error en $targetName: ${result.exceptionOrNull()?.localizedMessage}"
            )

            if (result.isSuccess) {
                onComplete("ImpresiÃ³n exitosa en $targetName")
            } else {
                val err = result.exceptionOrNull()?.localizedMessage ?: "Fallo al conectar"
                onComplete("Error en $targetName: $err")
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        super.onCleared()
        soundFeedback.release()
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\ui\screens\devolucion\DevolucionScreen.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.devolucion

import android.app.Application
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.text.KeyboardActions
import androidx.compose.foundation.text.KeyboardOptions
