@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun EntradasScanScreen(
    proveedor: String,
    polvorin: String,
    factura: String,
    entradaId: Long = 0L,
    viewModel: EntradasViewModel,
    onFinished: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val uiState by viewModel.uiState.collectAsState()
    val productSummaries by viewModel.productSummaries.collectAsState()
    var manualInput by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var showCatalogDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var catalogAddInitialId by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var itemToDelete by remember { mutableStateOf<BarcodeItem?>(null) }

    LaunchedEffect(entradaId) {
        if (entradaId > 0L) {
            viewModel.loadExistingEntrada(entradaId)
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(proveedor, polvorin, factura) {
        if (entradaId <= 0L) {
            viewModel.updateHeader(proveedor, polvorin, factura)
        }
    }

    DisposableEffect(context) {
        val receiver = ZebraScannerReceiver { scannedBarcode ->
            viewModel.onBarcodeScanned(scannedBarcode)
        }
        ZebraScannerReceiver.register(context, receiver)
        onDispose {
            ZebraScannerReceiver.unregister(context, receiver)
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = {
                    Column {
                        AutoShrinkText(
                            text = "Entradas: PolvorÃ­n ${uiState.polvorin.ifEmpty { polvorin }}",
                            targetTextSize = 15.sp,
                            minTextSize = 12.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = MineTextPrimary
                        )
                        AutoShrinkText(
                            text = "Prov: ${uiState.proveedor.ifEmpty { proveedor }} â€¢ Factura: ${uiState.factura.ifEmpty { factura }}",
                            targetTextSize = 12.sp,
                            minTextSize = 9.sp,
                            color = MineSafetyYellow
                        )
                    }
                },
                colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(containerColor = MineDarkBackground)
            )
        },
        containerColor = MineDarkBackground
    ) { padding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            // Draft Restored Banner (Crash / Power-off recovery)
            if (uiState.wasRestoredFromDraft) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .background(MineHighExplosiveOrange.copy(alpha = 0.15f))
                        .border(1.dp, MineHighExplosiveOrange, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .padding(8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                ) {
                    Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Info, contentDescription = null, tint = MineHighExplosiveOrange)
                    Text(
                        text = "Vale en progreso recuperado (${uiState.scannedItems.size} Ã­tems)",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = MineHighExplosiveOrangeLight,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                    )
                }
            }

            // CONTADOR COMPACTO OPTIMIZADO (TOTAL, CAJAS, SACOS, CARTUCHOS)
            val totalCajas = uiState.scannedItems.count { it.empaque == TipoEmpaque.CAJA || it.code.length == 30 }
            val totalSacos = uiState.scannedItems.count { it.empaque == TipoEmpaque.SACO || it.code.length == 18 }
            val totalCartuchos = uiState.scannedItems.count { it.empaque == TipoEmpaque.PIEZA || it.code.length in 16..17 }

            Card(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MineDarkSurface),
                border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, MineBorderGray),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
            ) {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        AutoShrinkText(
                            text = "Factura: ${uiState.factura.ifEmpty { factura }} â€¢ Prov: ${uiState.proveedor.ifEmpty { proveedor }}",
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 4.dp),
                            color = MineTextSecondary,
                            targetTextSize = 12.sp,
                            minTextSize = 9.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = if (uiState.scannedItems.isEmpty()) "Listo para escanear" else "${uiState.scannedItems.size} ingresados",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                            color = if (uiState.scannedItems.isEmpty()) MineSuccessGreenLight else MineSafetyYellow,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                        )
                    }

                    // 4 Contadores en 1 sola fila compacta con colores normalizados
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                    ) {
                        CompactMetric(
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            label = "TOTAL",
                            count = uiState.scannedItems.size,
                            borderColor = MineBorderGray,
                            countColor = EmpaqueTotalColor
                        )
                        CompactMetric(
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            label = "CAJAS",
                            count = totalCajas,
                            borderColor = EmpaqueCajaColor,
                            countColor = EmpaqueCajaColor
                        )
                        CompactMetric(
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            label = "SACOS",
                            count = totalSacos,
                            borderColor = EmpaqueSacoColor,
                            countColor = EmpaqueSacoColor
                        )
                        CompactMetric(
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            label = "CARTUCHOS",
                            count = totalCartuchos,
                            borderColor = EmpaqueCartuchoColor,
                            countColor = EmpaqueCartuchoColor
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // 1. CASILLA DE ESCANEO COMPACTA
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = manualInput,
                onValueChange = { manualInput = it },
                placeholder = { Text("Escanear codigo de barras o entrada manual...", fontSize = 13.sp, color = MineTextMuted) },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .heightIn(min = 48.dp),
                singleLine = true,
                keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(imeAction = ImeAction.Send),
                keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(
                    onSend = {
                        if (manualInput.isNotBlank()) {
                            viewModel.onBarcodeScanned(manualInput.trim())
                            manualInput = ""
                        }
                    }
                ),
                colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                    focusedBorderColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                    unfocusedBorderColor = MineBorderGray,
                    focusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                    unfocusedTextColor = MineTextPrimary,
                    focusedPlaceholderColor = MineTextMuted
                )
            )

            // 2. BANNER COMPACTO DE RESULTADO
            uiState.latestScanResult?.let { result ->
                val isError = !result.isValid
                val isDup = result.isDuplicate
                val bannerBg = when {
                    isError -> MineErrorRed.copy(alpha = 0.2f)
                    isDup -> MineWarningAmber.copy(alpha = 0.2f)
                    else -> MineSuccessGreen.copy(alpha = 0.2f)
                }
                val bannerBorder = when {
                    isError -> MineErrorRed
                    isDup -> MineWarningAmber
                    else -> MineSuccessGreen
                }
                val bannerTint = when {
                    isError -> MineErrorRed
                    isDup -> MineWarningAmber
                    else -> MineSuccessGreenLight
                }

                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                        .background(bannerBg)
                        .border(1.dp, bannerBorder, RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
                        .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = when {
                            isError || isDup -> Icons.Default.Warning
                            else -> Icons.Default.CheckCircle
                        },
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = bannerTint,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = result.message,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        color = MineTextPrimary,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    )
                    if (result.unknownId != null) {
                        Button(
                            onClick = {
                                catalogAddInitialId = result.unknownId
                                showCatalogDialog = true
                            },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = MineSafetyYellow, contentColor = MineDarkBackground),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(4.dp),
                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
                            modifier = Modifier.height(28.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text("+ Agregar ID", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 10.sp)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            var selectedSummaryKey by remember { mutableStateOf<Triple<String, String, TipoEmpaque>?>(null) }

            val filteredItems = remember(uiState.scannedItems, selectedSummaryKey) {
                if (selectedSummaryKey == null) {
                    uiState.scannedItems
                } else {
                    val (id, pres, emp) = selectedSummaryKey!!
                    uiState.scannedItems.filter {
                        it.productInfo.id == id && it.productInfo.presentation == pres && it.empaque == emp
                    }
                }
            }

            // 3. DESGLOSE POR PRODUCTO (CUADRÃCULA DE 2 COLUMNAS DINÃMICA E INTERACTIVA)
            if (productSummaries.isNotEmpty()) {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .heightIn(max = 145.dp)
                        .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                ) {
                    productSummaries.chunked(2).forEach { rowItems ->
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                        ) {
                            rowItems.forEach { summary ->
                                val key = Triple(summary.id, summary.presentation, summary.empaque)
                                val isSelected = selectedSummaryKey == key
                                Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    ProductCounterCard(
                                        summary = summary,
                                        isSelected = isSelected,
                                        onClick = {
                                            selectedSummaryKey = if (isSelected) null else key
                                        }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            if (rowItems.size == 1) {
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Encabezado de lecturas con filtro interactivo
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                if (selectedSummaryKey != null) {
                    val selectedSummary = productSummaries.firstOrNull {
                        it.id == selectedSummaryKey!!.first &&
                        it.presentation == selectedSummaryKey!!.second &&
                        it.empaque == selectedSummaryKey!!.third
                    }
                    Text(
                        text = "FILTRADO: ${selectedSummary?.name ?: ""} (${filteredItems.size})",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                        color = MineSafetyYellow,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = "[VER TODOS (${uiState.scannedItems.size})]",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                        color = MineAgentCyan,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clickable { selectedSummaryKey = null }
                            .padding(4.dp)
                    )
                } else {
                    Text(
                        text = "HISTORIAL DE LECTURAS (${uiState.scannedItems.size})",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                        color = MineTextMuted,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = "Toca un producto para filtrar",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                        color = MineTextMuted,
                        fontSize = 10.sp
                    )
                }
            }

            // 4. Lista de cÃ³digos escaneados (muestra filteredItems, se ajusta automÃ¡ticamente)
            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .fillMaxWidth(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
            ) {
                items(filteredItems, key = { it.code + it.timestamp }) { item ->
                    EntradaScannedItemCard(
                        item = item,
                        onDelete = { itemToDelete = item }
                    )
                }
            }

            // BotÃ³n inferior para Finalizar
            Button(
                onClick = { viewModel.finishAndSave(onFinished) },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(48.dp),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                    containerColor = MineSuccessGreen,
                    contentColor = MineTextPrimary
                )
            ) {
                Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Save, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                AutoShrinkText(
                    text = "GUARDAR Y FINALIZAR ENTRADA (${uiState.scannedItems.size})",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    targetTextSize = 14.sp,
                    minTextSize = 11.sp,
                    color = MineTextPrimary
                )
            }
        }
    }

    if (showCatalogDialog) {
        ProductEditDialog(
            initialId = catalogAddInitialId,
            initial = null,
            onDismiss = { showCatalogDialog = false },
            onSave = { entity ->
                viewModel.registerProductInCatalog(entity)
                showCatalogDialog = false
            }
        )
    }

    itemToDelete?.let { target ->
        TwoStepDeleteDialog(
            itemDescription = "${target.productInfo.name} (${target.productInfo.presentation})",
            itemCode = "CÃ³d: ${target.code}",
            onConfirmDelete = {
                viewModel.deleteItem(target)
                itemToDelete = null
            },
            onDismiss = { itemToDelete = null }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CompactMetric(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    label: String,
    count: Int,
    borderColor: Color,
    countColor: Color
) {
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .background(MineDarkBackground)
            .border(1.dp, borderColor, RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .padding(vertical = 4.dp, horizontal = 2.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Text(
            text = "$count",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontSize = 18.sp),
            color = countColor,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold
        )
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontSize = 9.sp),
            color = MineTextMuted,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
        )
    }
}

/**
 * Tarjeta de contador individual por producto para LazyRow horizontal
 * con AutoShrinkText y badge de empaque de alto contraste.
 */
@Composable
private fun ProductCounterCard(
    summary: ProductSummary,
    isSelected: Boolean = false,
    onClick: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    // Colores normalizados segÃºn el tipo de empaque:
    // CAJA: Amarillo Oro (#FFD600)
    // SACO: Cian ElÃ©ctrico (#00E5FF)
    // CARTUCHO / PIEZA: Coral / Naranja Rojizo (#FF916F)
    // ACCESORIO: Morado (#BA68C8)
    val empaqueColor = when (summary.empaque) {
        TipoEmpaque.CAJA -> EmpaqueCajaColor
        TipoEmpaque.SACO -> EmpaqueSacoColor
        TipoEmpaque.PIEZA -> EmpaqueCartuchoColor
        TipoEmpaque.ACCESORIO -> EmpaqueAccesorioColor
        else -> MineBorderGray
    }
    val empaqueLabel = when (summary.empaque) {
        TipoEmpaque.SACO -> "Sacos"
        TipoEmpaque.PIEZA -> "Cartuchos"
        else -> "Cajas"
    }

    val borderWidth = if (isSelected) 2.dp else 1.dp
    val borderColor = if (isSelected) empaqueColor else empaqueColor.copy(alpha = 0.7f)
    val cardBg = if (isSelected) empaqueColor.copy(alpha = 0.18f) else MineDarkSurface

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .background(cardBg)
            .border(borderWidth, borderColor, RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        // Badge de conteo siempre a la izquierda con el color de su empaque
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                .background(empaqueColor)
                .padding(horizontal = 5.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "${summary.count}",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = MineDarkBackground
                )
                Text(
                    text = empaqueLabel,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = MineDarkBackground,
                    fontSize = 9.sp
                )
            }
        }

        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = summary.name,
                initialTextSize = 12.sp,
                minTextSize = 8.sp,
                color = if (isSelected) empaqueColor else MineTextPrimary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                maxLines = 1
            )
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = if (summary.presentation.isNotBlank()) summary.presentation else "ID: ${summary.id}",
                initialTextSize = 10.sp,
                minTextSize = 7.sp,
                color = MineTextSecondary,
                maxLines = 1
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun EntradaScannedItemCard(
    item: BarcodeItem,
    onDelete: () -> Unit
) {
    val empaqueColor = when (item.empaque) {
        TipoEmpaque.CAJA -> EmpaqueCajaColor
        TipoEmpaque.SACO -> EmpaqueSacoColor
        TipoEmpaque.PIEZA -> EmpaqueCartuchoColor
        TipoEmpaque.ACCESORIO -> EmpaqueAccesorioColor
        else -> MineBorderGray
    }

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .background(MineDarkSurface)
            .border(1.dp, empaqueColor.copy(alpha = 0.6f), RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 6.dp)) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                        .background(empaqueColor.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                        .border(0.5.dp, empaqueColor, RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                        .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 1.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = item.empaque.label,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                        color = empaqueColor,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        fontSize = 10.sp,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        softWrap = false
                    )
                }
                AutoShrinkText(
                    text = item.productInfo.name,
                    initialTextSize = 13.sp,
                    minTextSize = 9.sp,
                    color = MineTextPrimary,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    maxLines = 1
                )
            }
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = "Medida: ${item.productInfo.presentation} â€¢ ${item.productInfo.type.label}",
                initialTextSize = 11.sp,
                minTextSize = 8.sp,
                color = MineTextSecondary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
                maxLines = 1
            )
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = "CÃ³d: ${item.code}",
                initialTextSize = 11.sp,
                minTextSize = 8.sp,
                color = MineTextSecondary,
                maxLines = 1
            )
        }

        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = item.timestamp.substringAfter(" "),
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                color = MineTextMuted,
                fontSize = 12.sp
            )
            IconButton(onClick = onDelete) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Delete,
                    contentDescription = "Eliminar",
                    tint = MineErrorRed
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\ui\screens\entradas\EntradasViewModel.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.entradas

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.ttexplo.mobile.TTExploApp
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.EntradaEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.EntradaItemEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.local.entity.ProductoEntity
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.CatalogRepositoryImpl
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductInfo
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductType
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.BarcodeItem
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.DateTimeUtils
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.ScanResult
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.ScanValidator
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.TipoEmpaque
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.common.SoundFeedbackManager
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class ProductSummary(
    val id: String,
    val name: String,
    val presentation: String,
    val type: ProductType,
    val count: Int,
    val empaque: TipoEmpaque
)

data class EntradasUiState(
    val draftId: Long? = null,
    val proveedor: String = "",
    val polvorin: String = "",
    val factura: String = "",
    val scannedItems: List<BarcodeItem> = emptyList(),
    val latestScanResult: ScanResult? = null,
    val wasRestoredFromDraft: Boolean = false,
    val isFinished: Boolean = false
)

class EntradasViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {

    private val db = (application as TTExploApp).database
    private val catalogRepository = CatalogRepositoryImpl(db.productoDao())
    private val scanValidator = ScanValidator(catalogRepository)
    private val soundFeedback = SoundFeedbackManager(application)

    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(EntradasUiState())
    val uiState: StateFlow<EntradasUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()

    val itemsByProduct: StateFlow<Map<String, Int>> = _uiState.map { state ->
        state.scannedItems
            .groupBy { "${it.productInfo.name} (${it.productInfo.presentation})" }
            .mapValues { it.value.size }
    }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000), emptyMap())

    val productSummaries: StateFlow<List<ProductSummary>> = _uiState.map { state ->
        state.scannedItems
            .groupBy { Triple(it.productInfo.id, it.productInfo.presentation, it.empaque) }
            .map { (key, items) ->
                val first = items.first()
                ProductSummary(
                    id = key.first,
                    name = first.productInfo.name,
                    presentation = key.second,
                    type = first.productInfo.type,
                    count = items.size,
                    empaque = key.third
                )
            }
            .sortedWith(compareBy<ProductSummary> { it.empaque.ordinal }.thenByDescending { it.count })
    }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000), emptyList())

    init {
        checkForDraft()
    }

    fun loadExistingEntrada(id: Long) {
        if (id <= 0) return
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val entrada = db.entradaDao().getEntradaById(id)
            if (entrada != null) {
                val dbItems = db.entradaDao().getItemsForEntrada(entrada.id)
                val domainItems = dbItems.map { entity ->
                    val product = catalogRepository.lookupBarcode(entity.codigoBarras)
                    BarcodeItem(
                        code = entity.codigoBarras,
                        timestamp = entity.fechaEscaneo,
                        productInfo = product,
                        empaque = when (entity.codigoBarras.length) {
                            30 -> TipoEmpaque.CAJA
                            18 -> TipoEmpaque.SACO
                            16, 17 -> TipoEmpaque.PIEZA
                            else -> TipoEmpaque.DESCONOCIDO
                        }
                    )
                }
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                    draftId = entrada.id,
                    proveedor = entrada.proveedor,
                    polvorin = entrada.polvorin,
                    factura = entrada.factura,
                    scannedItems = domainItems,
                    wasRestoredFromDraft = true
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun registerProductInCatalog(entity: ProductoEntity) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            db.productoDao().insert(entity)
            val pendingCode = _uiState.value.latestScanResult?.rawBarcode
            if (!pendingCode.isNullOrBlank() && _uiState.value.latestScanResult?.unknownId == entity.idProducto) {
                onBarcodeScanned(pendingCode)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun checkForDraft() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draft = db.entradaDao().getActiveDraft()
            if (draft != null) {
                val dbItems = db.entradaDao().getItemsForEntrada(draft.id)
                val domainItems = dbItems.map { entity ->
                    val product = catalogRepository.lookupBarcode(entity.codigoBarras)
                    BarcodeItem(
                        code = entity.codigoBarras,
                        timestamp = entity.fechaEscaneo,
                        productInfo = product,
                        empaque = when (entity.codigoBarras.length) {
                            30 -> TipoEmpaque.CAJA
                            18 -> TipoEmpaque.SACO
                            16, 17 -> TipoEmpaque.PIEZA
                            else -> TipoEmpaque.DESCONOCIDO
                        }
                    )
                }
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                    draftId = draft.id,
                    proveedor = draft.proveedor,
                    polvorin = draft.polvorin,
                    factura = draft.factura,
                    scannedItems = domainItems,
                    wasRestoredFromDraft = true
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun updateHeader(prov: String, polv: String, fact: String) {
        val current = _uiState.value
        _uiState.value = current.copy(
            proveedor = if (prov.isNotBlank()) prov else current.proveedor,
            polvorin = if (polv.isNotBlank()) polv else current.polvorin,
            factura = if (fact.isNotBlank()) fact else current.factura
        )
    }

    private suspend fun ensureDraftCreated(): Long {
        val current = _uiState.value
        if (current.draftId != null) return current.draftId

        val newId = db.entradaDao().insertEntrada(
            EntradaEntity(
                proveedor = current.proveedor,
                polvorin = current.polvorin,
                factura = current.factura,
                fechaCreacion = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp(),
                estado = "BORRADOR"
            )
        )
        _uiState.value = current.copy(draftId = newId)
        return newId
    }

    fun onBarcodeScanned(barcode: String) {
        val trimmed = barcode.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return

        viewModelScope.launch {
            val sessionCodes = _uiState.value.scannedItems.map { it.code }.toSet()
            val result = scanValidator.processScan(trimmed, sessionCodes, allowAccessories = false)

            if (!result.isValid) {
                soundFeedback.playError()
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(latestScanResult = result)
                return@launch
            }

            if (result.isDuplicate) {
                soundFeedback.playWarning()
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(latestScanResult = result)
            } else {
                soundFeedback.playSuccess()
                val draftId = ensureDraftCreated()

                // Persist immediately in SQLite draft
                db.entradaDao().insertSingleItem(
                    EntradaItemEntity(
                        entradaId = draftId,
                        codigoBarras = result.rawBarcode,
                        fechaEscaneo = result.timestamp,
                        productoId = result.productInfo.id,
                        productoNombre = result.productInfo.name,
                        presentacion = result.productInfo.presentation,
                        tipo = result.productInfo.type.name
                    )
                )

                val newItem = BarcodeItem(
                    code = result.rawBarcode,
                    timestamp = result.timestamp,
                    productInfo = result.productInfo,
                    empaque = result.empaque
                )
                val updatedList = listOf(newItem) + _uiState.value.scannedItems
                _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                    scannedItems = updatedList,
                    latestScanResult = result
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun deleteItem(item: BarcodeItem) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draftId = _uiState.value.draftId
            if (draftId != null) {
                db.entradaDao().deleteItem(draftId, item.code)
            }
            val updated = _uiState.value.scannedItems.filterNot { it.code == item.code }
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                scannedItems = updated,
                latestScanResult = ScanResult(
                    rawBarcode = item.code,
                    timestamp = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp(),
                    productInfo = item.productInfo,
                    isDuplicate = false,
                    isValid = true,
                    message = "CÃ³digo ${item.code} eliminado"
                )
            )
        }
    }

    fun finishAndSave(onSuccess: () -> Unit) {
        val state = _uiState.value
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val draftId = state.draftId
            if (draftId != null) {
                db.entradaDao().updateEstado(draftId, "FINALIZADO")
            } else if (state.scannedItems.isNotEmpty()) {
                val newId = db.entradaDao().insertEntrada(
                    EntradaEntity(
                        proveedor = state.proveedor,
                        polvorin = state.polvorin,
                        factura = state.factura,
                        fechaCreacion = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp(),
                        estado = "FINALIZADO"
                    )
                )
                val items = state.scannedItems.map {
                    EntradaItemEntity(
                        entradaId = newId,
                        codigoBarras = it.code,
                        fechaEscaneo = it.timestamp
                    )
                }
                db.entradaDao().insertItems(items)
            }
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(isFinished = true, draftId = null)
            onSuccess()
        }
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        super.onCleared()
        soundFeedback.release()
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\ui\screens\history\ValesHistoryScreen.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.history

import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.activity.compose.rememberLauncherForActivityResult
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.text.KeyboardOptions
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.ArrowBack
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Delete
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Download
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Edit
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Share
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Warning
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.ImeAction
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.KeyboardType
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import com.ttexplo.mobile.TTExploApp
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.export.FlatFileFormatter
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.*
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.DateTimeUtils
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.common.AutoShrinkText
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.theme.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import java.io.File

data class ValeSummaryItem(
    val id: Long,
    val tipo: String, // "DESPACHO", "ENTRADA", "ACCESORIOS", "DEV. ACCESORIOS", "DEVOLUCION"
    val folio: String,
    val descripcion: String,
    val fecha: String,
    val totalItems: Int
)

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun ValesHistoryScreen(
    onNavigateBack: () -> Unit,
    onEditDespacho: (Long) -> Unit = {},
    onEditEntrada: (Long) -> Unit = {},
    onEditAccesorios: (Long) -> Unit = {},
    onEditDevolucionAccesorios: (Long) -> Unit = {},
    onEditDevolucion: (Long) -> Unit = {}
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val db = (context.applicationContext as TTExploApp).database

    var valesList by remember { mutableStateOf<List<ValeSummaryItem>>(emptyList()) }
    val selectedIds = remember { mutableStateListOf<Pair<String, Long>>() }
    var pendingExportText by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    var showDeleteDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var valesToDelete by remember { mutableStateOf<List<ValeSummaryItem>>(emptyList()) }
    var securityCode by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var inputCode by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    fun loadVales() {
        scope.launch {
            val list = mutableListOf<ValeSummaryItem>()

            // Despachos
            val despachos = db.despachoDao().getAllDespachos()
            for (d in despachos) {
                val items = db.despachoDao().getItemsForDespacho(d.id)
                list.add(
                    ValeSummaryItem(
                        id = d.id,
                        tipo = "DESPACHO",
                        folio = if (d.vale.startsWith("REF")) d.vale else "REF${d.vale}",
                        descripcion = "${d.empresa} â€¢ ${d.lugar}",
                        fecha = d.fechaCreacion,
                        totalItems = items.size
                    )
                )
            }

            // Entradas
            val entradas = db.entradaDao().getAllEntradas()
            for (e in entradas) {
                val items = db.entradaDao().getItemsForEntrada(e.id)
                list.add(
                    ValeSummaryItem(
                        id = e.id,
                        tipo = "ENTRADA",
                        folio = "PRV${e.proveedor}",
                        descripcion = "Factura: ${e.factura} â€¢ PolvorÃ­n: ${e.polvorin}",
                        fecha = e.fechaCreacion,
                        totalItems = items.size
                    )
                )
            }

            // Accesorios y DevoluciÃ³n de Accesorios
            val accesorios = db.accesorioDao().getAllVales()
            for (a in accesorios) {
                val items = db.accesorioDao().getItemsForVale(a.id)
                val isDev = a.empresa.startsWith("DEV:") || items.any { it.cantidad < 0 }
                val tipoLabel = if (isDev) "DEV. ACCESORIOS" else "ACCESORIOS"
                val folioFormatted = if (a.vale.startsWith("ACC")) a.vale else "ACC${a.vale}"
                val cleanDesc = if (a.empresa.startsWith("DEV:")) a.empresa.removePrefix("DEV: ") else a.empresa
                list.add(
                    ValeSummaryItem(
                        id = a.id,
                        tipo = tipoLabel,
                        folio = folioFormatted,
                        descripcion = "$cleanDesc â€¢ ${a.lugar}",
                        fecha = a.fechaCreacion,
                        totalItems = items.sumOf { it.cantidad }
                    )
                )
            }

            // Devoluciones
            val devoluciones = db.devolucionDao().getAllDevoluciones()
            for (dev in devoluciones) {
                val items = db.devolucionDao().getItemsForDevolucion(dev.id)
                list.add(
                    ValeSummaryItem(
                        id = dev.id,
                        tipo = "DEVOLUCION",
                        folio = if (dev.empleadoEntrega.startsWith("DEV")) dev.empleadoEntrega else "DEV${dev.empleadoEntrega}",
                        descripcion = "Recibe: ${dev.empleadoRecibe}",
                        fecha = dev.fechaCreacion,
                        totalItems = items.size
                    )
                )
            }

            valesList = list.sortedByDescending { it.fecha }
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        loadVales()
    }

    val createFileLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.CreateDocument("text/plain")
    ) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null && pendingExportText != null) {
            try {
                context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(uri)?.use { out ->
                    out.write(pendingExportText!!.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
                    out.flush()
                }
                Toast.makeText(context, "Archivo consolidado guardado exitosamente", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Toast.makeText(context, "Error al guardar archivo: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            }
        }
    }

    fun exportConsolidated() {
        scope.launch {
            val sb = StringBuilder()
            val toExport = if (selectedIds.isEmpty()) valesList else valesList.filter { selectedIds.contains(it.tipo to it.id) }

            for (vale in toExport) {
                when (vale.tipo) {
                    "DESPACHO" -> {
                        val d = db.despachoDao().getAllDespachos().find { it.id == vale.id }
                        if (d != null) {
                            val items = db.despachoDao().getItemsForDespacho(d.id).map {
                                ScannedCodeRecord(it.codigoBarras, it.fechaEscaneo, it.productoNombre, it.presentacion)
                            }
                            val rec = DespachoRecord(d.vale, d.empresa, d.lugar, d.transportista, d.turno, d.voladura, items)
                            sb.append(FlatFileFormatter.formatDespacho(rec))
                            sb.append("\r\n")
                        }
                    }
                    "ENTRADA" -> {
                        val e = db.entradaDao().getAllEntradas().find { it.id == vale.id }
                        if (e != null) {
                            val items = db.entradaDao().getItemsForEntrada(e.id).map {
                                ScannedCodeRecord(it.codigoBarras, it.fechaEscaneo)
                            }
                            val rec = EntradaRecord(e.proveedor, e.polvorin, e.factura, items)
                            sb.append(FlatFileFormatter.formatEntrada(rec))
                            sb.append("\r\n")
                        }
                    }
                    "ACCESORIOS", "DEV. ACCESORIOS" -> {
                        val a = db.accesorioDao().getAllVales().find { it.id == vale.id }
                        if (a != null) {
                            val items = db.accesorioDao().getItemsForVale(a.id).map {
                                AccessoryItemRecord(it.codigoAccesorio, it.cantidad, it.fechaEscaneo)
                            }
                            val isDev = a.empresa.startsWith("DEV:") || items.any { it.quantity < 0 }
                            val cleanEmpresa = if (a.empresa.startsWith("DEV:")) a.empresa.removePrefix("DEV: ") else a.empresa
                            val rec = AccesoriosRecord(a.vale, cleanEmpresa, a.lugar, a.transportista, a.turno, a.recibio, a.entrego, a.voladura, items)
                            sb.append(FlatFileFormatter.formatAccesorios(rec))
                            sb.append("\r\n")
                        }
                    }
                    "DEVOLUCION" -> {
                        val dev = db.devolucionDao().getAllDevoluciones().find { it.id == vale.id }
                        if (dev != null) {
                            val items = db.devolucionDao().getItemsForDevolucion(dev.id).map {
                                ScannedCodeRecord(it.codigoBarras, it.fechaEscaneo)
                            }
                            val rec = DevolucionRecord(dev.empleadoEntrega, dev.empleadoRecibe, items)
                            sb.append(FlatFileFormatter.formatDevolucion(rec))
                            sb.append("\r\n")
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            val text = sb.toString().trim()
            if (text.isEmpty()) {
                Toast.makeText(context, "No hay vales para exportar", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                return@launch
            }

            pendingExportText = text
            val timestamp = DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp().replace(":", "-").replace(" ", "_")
            createFileLauncher.launch("TTEXPLO_CONSOLIDADO_$timestamp.txt")
        }
    }

    fun shareVale(vale: ValeSummaryItem) {
        scope.launch {
            val text = when (vale.tipo) {
                "DESPACHO" -> {
                    val d = db.despachoDao().getAllDespachos().find { it.id == vale.id } ?: return@launch
                    val items = db.despachoDao().getItemsForDespacho(d.id).map {
                        ScannedCodeRecord(it.codigoBarras, it.fechaEscaneo, it.productoNombre, it.presentacion)
                    }
                    FlatFileFormatter.formatDespacho(DespachoRecord(d.vale, d.empresa, d.lugar, d.transportista, d.turno, d.voladura, items))
                }
                "ENTRADA" -> {
                    val e = db.entradaDao().getAllEntradas().find { it.id == vale.id } ?: return@launch
                    val items = db.entradaDao().getItemsForEntrada(e.id).map {
                        ScannedCodeRecord(it.codigoBarras, it.fechaEscaneo)
                    }
                    FlatFileFormatter.formatEntrada(EntradaRecord(e.proveedor, e.polvorin, e.factura, items))
                }
                "ACCESORIOS", "DEV. ACCESORIOS" -> {
                    val a = db.accesorioDao().getAllVales().find { it.id == vale.id } ?: return@launch
                    val items = db.accesorioDao().getItemsForVale(a.id).map {
                        AccessoryItemRecord(it.codigoAccesorio, it.cantidad, it.fechaEscaneo)
                    }
                    val isDev = a.empresa.startsWith("DEV:") || items.any { it.quantity < 0 }
                    val cleanEmpresa = if (a.empresa.startsWith("DEV:")) a.empresa.removePrefix("DEV: ") else a.empresa
                    FlatFileFormatter.formatAccesorios(AccesoriosRecord(a.vale, cleanEmpresa, a.lugar, a.transportista, a.turno, a.recibio, a.entrego, a.voladura, items))
                }
                "DEVOLUCION" -> {
                    val dev = db.devolucionDao().getAllDevoluciones().find { it.id == vale.id } ?: return@launch
                    val items = db.devolucionDao().getItemsForDevolucion(dev.id).map {
                        ScannedCodeRecord(it.codigoBarras, it.fechaEscaneo)
                    }
                    FlatFileFormatter.formatDevolucion(DevolucionRecord(dev.empleadoEntrega, dev.empleadoRecibe, items))
                }
                else -> ""
            }

            try {
                val file = File(context.cacheDir, "${vale.folio}_${DateTimeUtils.currentTimestamp().replace(":", "-").replace(" ", "_")}.txt")
                file.writeText(text, Charsets.UTF_8)
                val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.fileprovider", file)
                val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                    type = "text/plain"
                    putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                }
                context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, "Compartir Vale ${vale.folio}"))
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Toast.makeText(context, "Error al compartir: ${e.message}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
    }

    fun promptDelete(vales: List<ValeSummaryItem>) {
        if (vales.isEmpty()) return
        valesToDelete = vales
        securityCode = (1000..9999).random().toString()
        inputCode = ""
        showDeleteDialog = true
    }

    fun executeDelete() {
        scope.launch {
            for (vale in valesToDelete) {
                when (vale.tipo) {
                    "DESPACHO" -> {
                        db.despachoDao().deleteItemsForDespacho(vale.id)
                        db.despachoDao().deleteDespacho(vale.id)
                    }
                    "ENTRADA" -> {
                        db.entradaDao().deleteItemsForEntrada(vale.id)
                        db.entradaDao().deleteEntrada(vale.id)
                    }
                    "ACCESORIOS", "DEV. ACCESORIOS" -> {
                        db.accesorioDao().deleteItemsForVale(vale.id)
                        db.accesorioDao().deleteVale(vale.id)
                    }
                    "DEVOLUCION" -> {
                        db.devolucionDao().deleteItemsForDevolucion(vale.id)
                        db.devolucionDao().deleteDevolucion(vale.id)
                    }
                }
                selectedIds.remove(vale.tipo to vale.id)
            }
            val count = valesToDelete.size
            showDeleteDialog = false
            valesToDelete = emptyList()
            loadVales()
            Toast.makeText(context, "$count vale(s) eliminado(s) permanentemente", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = { Text("Historial de Vales", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) },
                navigationIcon = {
                    IconButton(onClick = onNavigateBack) {

                    }
                },
                colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(
                    containerColor = MineDarkSurface,
                    titleContentColor = MineTextPrimary,
                    navigationIconContentColor = MineTextPrimary
                )
            )
        },
        bottomBar = {
            Surface(
                color = MineDarkSurface,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
            ) {
                Button(
                    onClick = { exportConsolidated() },
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(16.dp)
                        .height(54.dp),
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                        containerColor = MineSafetyYellow,
                        contentColor = MineDarkBackground
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.Download, contentDescription = null)
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = if (selectedIds.isEmpty()) {
                            "GUARDAR ARCHIVO CONSOLIDADO (${valesList.size} VALES)"
                        } else {
                            "GUARDAR ARCHIVO CONSOLIDADO (${selectedIds.size} SELECCIONADOS)"
                        },
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        fontSize = 15.sp
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    ) { padding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(MineDarkBackground)
                .padding(padding)
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
        ) {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            // Barra de conteo y seleccionar todo
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "VALES REGISTRADOS (${valesList.size})",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                    color = MineSafetyYellow,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )

                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {

                    TextButton(onClick = {

                            selectedIds.clear()
                            valesList.forEach { selectedIds.add(it.tipo to it.id) }

                        )
                    }

                    if (anySelected) {
                        IconButton(onClick = {
                            val selectedVales = valesList.filter { selectedIds.contains(it.tipo to it.id) }
                            promptDelete(selectedVales)
                        }) {
                            Icon(
                                Icons.Default.Delete,
                                contentDescription = "Eliminar selecciÃ³n",
                                tint = MineErrorRed

                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            LazyColumn(

            ) {

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.screens.home

import com.ttexplo.mobile.license.LicenseStatus
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.common.AutoShrinkText
import com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.theme.*

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun HomeScreen(
    onNavigateToEntradas: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToDespacho: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToAccesorios: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToDevoluciones: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToDevolucionAccesorios: () -> Unit = {},
    onNavigateToSettings: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToHistory: () -> Unit = {},
    onNavigateToLicense: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var licenseCheck by remember { mutableStateOf(LicenseManager.checkLicense(context)) }

    // Re-verificar licencia cada vez que HomeScreen vuelve al primer plano (ON_RESUME)
    val lifecycleOwner = androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalLifecycleOwner.current
    DisposableEffect(lifecycleOwner) {
        val observer = androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleEventObserver { _, event ->
            if (event == androidx.lifecycle.Lifecycle.Event.ON_RESUME) {
                licenseCheck = LicenseManager.checkLicense(context)
            }
        }
        lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.addObserver(observer)
        onDispose {
            lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.removeObserver(observer)
        }
    }

    val isOperationAllowed = licenseCheck.status == LicenseStatus.VALID || licenseCheck.status == LicenseStatus.EXPIRING_SOON

    fun handleGuardedAction(action: () -> Unit) {
        if (isOperationAllowed) {
            action()
        } else {
            onNavigateToLicense()
        }
    }
    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = {
                    Column {
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                        ) {
                            AutoShrinkText(
                                text = "T&T EXPLO MOBILE",
                                initialTextSize = 18.sp,
                                minTextSize = 14.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = MineSafetyYellow
                            )
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                                    .background(MineSafetyYellow.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                                    .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "v${com.ttexplo.mobile.BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME}",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                    color = MineSafetyYellow,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    fontSize = 11.sp
                                )
                            }
                        }
                        AutoShrinkText(
                            text = "Control de Explosivos en Sitio",
                            initialTextSize = 12.sp,
                            minTextSize = 10.sp,
                            color = MineTextSecondary,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal
                        )
                    }
                },
                actions = {
                    IconButton(onClick = onNavigateToSettings) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Settings,
                            contentDescription = "ConfiguraciÃ³n",
                            tint = MineTextPrimary
                        )
                    }
                },
                colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(
                    containerColor = MineDarkBackground
                )
            )
        },
        containerColor = MineDarkBackground
    ) { paddingValues ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(paddingValues)
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            // BANNER DE AVISO DE LICENCIA (SI FALTA <= 30 DÃAS O ESTÃ VENCIDA/BLOQUEADA)
            if (licenseCheck.status == LicenseStatus.EXPIRING_SOON) {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clickable { onNavigateToLicense() },
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MineDarkSurface),
                    border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.5.dp, MineHighExplosiveOrange),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(12.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Warning,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = MineHighExplosiveOrange,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                        )
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                text = "AVISO: LICENCIA PRÃ“XIMA A VENCER",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                color = MineHighExplosiveOrange,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "Faltan ${licenseCheck.daysRemaining} dÃ­as para el vencimiento (${licenseCheck.info?.expiresDate?.take(10)}). Toque aquÃ­ para renovar.",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                color = MineTextPrimary,
                                fontSize = 12.sp
                            )
                        }
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.ChevronRight,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = MineHighExplosiveOrange
                        )
                    }
                }
            } else if (!isOperationAllowed) {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clickable { onNavigateToLicense() },
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MineDarkSurface),
                    border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(2.dp, MineErrorRed),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(12.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Lock,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = MineErrorRed,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                        )
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                text = "TERMINAL BLOQUEADA POR LICENCIA",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                color = MineErrorRed,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = licenseCheck.message,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                color = MineTextPrimary,
                                fontSize = 12.sp
                            )
                        }
                        Button(
                            onClick = onNavigateToLicense,
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = MineErrorRed, contentColor = MineTextPrimary),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(4.dp),
                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text("ACTIVAR", fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Text(
                text = "OPERACIONES PRINCIPALES",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                color = MineTextMuted,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )

@Composable
fun MenuActionCard(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    accentColor: Color,
    icon: ImageVector,
    isCompact: Boolean = false,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .heightIn(min = if (isCompact) 74.dp else 88.dp)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
            .background(MineDarkSurface)
            .border(1.dp, MineBorderGray, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(if (isCompact) 42.dp else 50.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                .background(accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.15f))
                .border(1.dp, accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.4f), RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = icon,
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = accentColor,
                modifier = Modifier.size(if (isCompact) 22.dp else 26.dp)
            )
        }

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .padding(end = 4.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = title,
                initialTextSize = if (isCompact) 15.sp else 17.sp,
                minTextSize = 12.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = MineTextPrimary,
                maxLines = 2,
                softWrap = true
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            AutoShrinkText(
                text = subtitle,
                initialTextSize = 12.sp,
                minTextSize = 9.sp,
                color = MineTextSecondary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
                maxLines = 2,
                softWrap = true
            )
        }

        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Default.ChevronRight,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = MineTextMuted,
            modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
        )
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun LicenseScreen(
    canNavigateBack: Boolean = false,
    onNavigateBack: () -> Unit = {},
    onLicenseActivated: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val clipboardManager = remember {
        context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager
    }

    var licenseState by remember { mutableStateOf(LicenseManager.checkLicense(context)) }
    var tokenInput by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var errorMessage by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var successMessage by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    val formattedDeviceId = remember { LicenseManager.getFormattedDeviceId(context) }
    val rawDeviceId = remember { LicenseManager.getRawDeviceId(context) }

    // FunciÃ³n para activar token
    fun processTokenActivation(token: String) {
        val trimmed = token.trim()
        if (trimmed.isBlank()) {
            errorMessage = "Ingrese o escanee el cÃ³digo de activaciÃ³n TTEX."
            return
        }

        val result = LicenseManager.saveLicense(context, trimmed)
        if (result.isSuccess) {
            val info = result.getOrThrow()
            licenseState = LicenseManager.checkLicense(context)
            errorMessage = null
            successMessage = "Â¡Licencia activada con Ã©xito para ${info.client} (${info.tier})!"
            Toast.makeText(context, "Licencia activada correctamente", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        } else {
            val err = result.exceptionOrNull()?.message ?: "Error al validar la licencia."
            errorMessage = err
            successMessage = null
            Toast.makeText(context, "Error: $err", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }

    // Escucha de escÃ¡ner Zebra / OEM DataWedge
    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        val receiver = ZebraScannerReceiver { barcode ->
            if (barcode.isNotBlank()) {
                tokenInput = barcode.trim()
                processTokenActivation(barcode)
            }
        }
        ZebraScannerReceiver.register(context, receiver)
        onDispose {
            ZebraScannerReceiver.unregister(context, receiver)
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = {
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {

                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Key,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = MineSafetyYellow
                        )
                        Column {

                                minTextSize = 13.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,

                            )
                            AutoShrinkText(
                                text = "T&T Explo Mobile â€¢ Candado y Terminal",
                                initialTextSize = 11.sp,
                                minTextSize = 9.sp,
                                color = MineTextSecondary
                            )
                        }
                    }
                },
                navigationIcon = {
                    if (canNavigateBack) {
                        IconButton(onClick = onNavigateBack) {
                            Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Regresar", tint = MineTextPrimary)
                        }
                    }
                },
                colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(containerColor = MineDarkBackground)
            )
        },
        containerColor = MineDarkBackground
    ) { paddingValues ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(paddingValues)
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
        ) {

            // ==========================================
            // TARJETA 1: IDENTIFICADOR DE HARDWARE (ANDROID ID)
            // ==========================================
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),

                border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, MineAgentCyan.copy(alpha = 0.6f)),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)
            ) {

                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(14.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.PhonelinkLock, contentDescription = null, tint = MineAgentCyan)
                        Text(
                            text = "IDENTIFICADOR DE HARDWARE (DEVICE ID)",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                            color = MineAgentCyan,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                    }

                    Text(

        }
    }
