import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class SettingsViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    private val db = (application as TTExploApp).database
    private val catalogRepo = CatalogRepositoryImpl(db.productoDao())
    val settingsRepo = SettingsRepository(application)
    val printerManager = BluetoothPrinterManager(application)

    private val _productCount = MutableStateFlow(0)
    val productCount: StateFlow<Int> = _productCount.asStateFlow()

    private val _statusMessage = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val statusMessage: StateFlow<String?> = _statusMessage.asStateFlow()

    init {
        refreshCount()
    }

    fun refreshCount() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _productCount.value = catalogRepo.countProducts()
        }
    }

    fun loadDefaultAssetCatalog(context: Context) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val csvContent = context.assets.open("catalogo_productos.csv").bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
                val imported = catalogRepo.importCsv(csvContent)
                _productCount.value = catalogRepo.countProducts()
                _statusMessage.value = "CatÃ¡logo recargado: $imported productos."
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                _statusMessage.value = "Error al cargar catÃ¡logo: ${e.message}"
            }
        }
    }

    fun importCsvFromUri(context: Context, uri: Uri) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val content = context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.bufferedReader()?.use { it.readText() } ?: ""
                val imported = catalogRepo.importCsv(content)
                _productCount.value = catalogRepo.countProducts()
                _statusMessage.value = "CatÃ¡logo actualizado: $imported productos importados."
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                _statusMessage.value = "Error al importar CSV: ${e.message}"
            }
        }
    }

    fun buildExportText(context: Context, onReady: (String) -> Unit) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val sb = StringBuilder()
                val despachos = db.despachoDao().getAllDespachos()
                for (d in despachos) {
                    val items = db.despachoDao().getItemsForDespacho(d.id).map {
                        ScannedCodeRecord(it.codigoBarras, it.fechaEscaneo, it.productoNombre, it.presentacion)
                    }
                    val rec = DespachoRecord(d.vale, d.empresa, d.lugar, d.transportista, d.turno, d.voladura, items)
                    sb.append(FlatFileFormatter.formatDespacho(rec)).append("\r\n")
                }

                val entradas = db.entradaDao().getAllEntradas()
                for (e in entradas) {
                    val items = db.entradaDao().getItemsForEntrada(e.id).map {
                        ScannedCodeRecord(it.codigoBarras, it.fechaEscaneo)
                    }
                    val rec = EntradaRecord(e.proveedor, e.polvorin, e.factura, items)
                    sb.append(FlatFileFormatter.formatEntrada(rec)).append("\r\n")
                }

                val accesorios = db.accesorioDao().getAllVales()
                for (a in accesorios) {
                    val items = db.accesorioDao().getItemsForVale(a.id).map {
                        AccessoryItemRecord(it.codigoAccesorio, it.cantidad, it.fechaEscaneo)
                    }
                    val rec = AccesoriosRecord(a.vale, a.empresa, a.lugar, a.transportista, a.turno, a.recibio, a.entrego, a.voladura, items)
                    sb.append(FlatFileFormatter.formatAccesorios(rec)).append("\r\n")
                }

                val devoluciones = db.devolucionDao().getAllDevoluciones()
                for (dev in devoluciones) {
                    val items = db.devolucionDao().getItemsForDevolucion(dev.id).map {
                        ScannedCodeRecord(it.codigoBarras, it.fechaEscaneo)
                    }
                    val rec = DevolucionRecord(dev.empleadoEntrega, dev.empleadoRecibe, items)
                    sb.append(FlatFileFormatter.formatDevolucion(rec)).append("\r\n")
                }

                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    onReady(sb.toString().trim())
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                    _statusMessage.value = "Error al compilar exportaciÃ³n: ${e.message}"
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun sendTestPrint(format: String = "ESCPOS", onResult: (String) -> Unit) {
        val paired = printerManager.getPairedPrinters()
        if (paired.isEmpty()) {
            onResult("No hay dispositivos Bluetooth vinculados en Android.")
            return
        }
        val target = printerManager.findPreferredPrinter() ?: paired.first()
        val targetName = try { target.name ?: target.address } catch (_: Exception) { target.address }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            val bytes = when (format) {
                "CPCL" -> TicketGenerator.generateCpclTestTicket(
                    paperWidth = settingsRepo.getPaperWidth(),
                    mineName = settingsRepo.getMineName()
                )
                "PLAIN" -> TicketGenerator.generatePlainTestTicket(
                    paperWidth = settingsRepo.getPaperWidth(),
                    mineName = settingsRepo.getMineName()
                )
                else -> TicketGenerator.generateTestTicket(
                    paperWidth = settingsRepo.getPaperWidth(),
                    mineName = settingsRepo.getMineName(),
                    permitNumber = settingsRepo.getPermitNumber()
                )
            }
            val result = printerManager.printBytes(target.address, bytes)
            if (result.isSuccess) {
                onResult("Â¡Ticket de prueba [$format] enviado exitosamente a $targetName!")
            } else {
                val err = result.exceptionOrNull()?.localizedMessage ?: "Error de comunicaciÃ³n"
                onResult("Error al imprimir en $targetName: $err")
            }
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable

    onNavigateBack: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateCatalog: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateHistory: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToLicense: () -> Unit = {},

    val context = LocalContext.current
    val productCount by viewModel.productCount.collectAsState()
    val statusMsg by viewModel.statusMessage.collectAsState()

    var licenseCheck by remember { mutableStateOf(com.ttexplo.mobile.license.LicenseManager.checkLicense(context)) }
    val formattedDeviceId = remember { com.ttexplo.mobile.license.LicenseManager.getFormattedDeviceId(context) }
    val clipboardManager = remember { context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as android.content.ClipboardManager }

    val lifecycleOwner = androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalLifecycleOwner.current
    DisposableEffect(lifecycleOwner) {
        val observer = androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleEventObserver { _, event ->
            if (event == androidx.lifecycle.Lifecycle.Event.ON_RESUME) {
                licenseCheck = com.ttexplo.mobile.license.LicenseManager.checkLicense(context)
            }
        }
        lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.addObserver(observer)
        onDispose {
            lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.removeObserver(observer)

    var mineName by remember { mutableStateOf(viewModel.settingsRepo.getMineName()) }
    var permitNumber by remember { mutableStateOf(viewModel.settingsRepo.getPermitNumber()) }

            }
        }
    }

            permissions[Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT] == true

            showPrinterDialog = true

        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(

                            modifier = Modifier

                            )
                        }

            // ==========================================
            // SECCIÃ“N 2: DATOS DE MINA Y ENCABEZADOS
            // ==========================================
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MineDarkSurface),

                        colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(

                        )
                    )

                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = permitNumber,
                        onValueChange = {
                            permitNumber = it
                            viewModel.settingsRepo.setPermitNumber(it)
                        },
                        label = { Text("NÃºmero de Permiso General (SEDENA)") },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),

    if (showPrinterDialog) {
        var pairedList by remember { mutableStateOf<List<BluetoothDevice>>(emptyList()) }

        fun refreshDevices() {
            pairedList = viewModel.printerManager.getPairedPrinters()
        }

        LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
            refreshDevices()
        }

        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showPrinterDialog = false },
            title = {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text("Impresoras Bluetooth", color = MineTextPrimary, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    IconButton(onClick = { refreshDevices() }) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.Refresh, contentDescription = "Actualizar", tint = MineSafetyYellow)
                    }
                }
            },
            text = {
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    if (!viewModel.printerManager.isBluetoothEnabled()) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .background(MineErrorRed.copy(alpha = 0.2f), RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .border(1.dp, MineErrorRed, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .padding(10.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                "El Bluetooth del telÃ©fono estÃ¡ apagado. Por favor actÃ­velo para detectar su impresora.",
                                color = MineErrorRed,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                        }
                    } else if (!viewModel.printerManager.hasBluetoothPermission()) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .background(MineWarningAmber.copy(alpha = 0.2f), RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .border(1.dp, MineWarningAmber, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                .padding(10.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                "Permiso de Bluetooth no concedido en el telÃ©fono.",
                                color = MineWarningAmber,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (pairedList.isEmpty()) {
                        Text(
                            "No se detectaron dispositivos vinculados en Android. AsegÃºrese de haber emparejado la impresora en la configuraciÃ³n de Bluetooth de su telÃ©fono/escÃ¡ner primero.",
                            color = MineTextMuted,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall
                        )
                        Button(
                            onClick = {
                                try {
                                    context.startActivity(Intent(Settings.ACTION_BLUETOOTH_SETTINGS))
                                } catch (e: Exception) {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "Abra Ajustes > Bluetooth en su telÃ©fono", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            },
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = MineSafetyYellow, contentColor = MineDarkBackground),
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Default.Bluetooth, contentDescription = null)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text("Abrir Ajustes de Bluetooth", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 12.sp)
                        }
                    } else {
                        Text(
                            "Dispositivos vinculados encontrados (${pairedList.size}):",
                            color = MineTextSecondary,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall
                        )
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .heightIn(max = 280.dp)
                                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                        ) {
                            pairedList.forEach { dev ->
                                val isSelected = dev.address.equals(preferredMac, ignoreCase = true)
                                val devName = try { dev.name ?: "Dispositivo sin nombre" } catch (e: SecurityException) { "Dispositivo Bluetooth" }
                                Card(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .clickable {
                                            preferredMac = dev.address
                                            preferredName = devName
                                            viewModel.settingsRepo.setPreferredPrinterMac(dev.address)
                                            viewModel.settingsRepo.setPreferredPrinterName(preferredName)
                                            showPrinterDialog = false
                                            Toast.makeText(context, "Impresora seleccionada: $preferredName", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                        },
                                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = if (isSelected) MineDarkBackground else MineDarkSurface),
                                    border = androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke(1.dp, if (isSelected) MineSafetyYellow else MineBorderGray)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier.padding(10.dp),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(Icons.Default.Print, contentDescription = null, tint = if (isSelected) MineSafetyYellow else MineTextMuted)
                                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                            AutoShrinkText(devName, color = MineTextPrimary, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, targetTextSize = 13.sp, minTextSize = 9.sp)
                                            AutoShrinkText(dev.address, color = MineTextSecondary, targetTextSize = 11.sp, minTextSize = 8.sp)
                                        }
                                        if (isSelected) {
                                            Text("Activa", color = MineSafetyYellow, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            },
            confirmButton = {
                if (preferredMac.isNotBlank()) {
                    TextButton(onClick = {
                        preferredMac = ""
                        preferredName = ""
                        viewModel.settingsRepo.setPreferredPrinterMac("")
                        viewModel.settingsRepo.setPreferredPrinterName("")
                        showPrinterDialog = false
                    }) {
                        Text("Desvincular", color = MineErrorRed)
                    }
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { showPrinterDialog = false }) {
                    Text("Cerrar", color = MineTextMuted)
                }
            },
            containerColor = MineDarkSurface
        )
    }
}

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color

// Safety Industrial Mine Theme Tokens
val MineSafetyYellow = Color(0xFFFFB300)
val MineSafetyYellowDark = Color(0xFFC68400)
val MineSafetyAmber = Color(0xFFFF8F00)

val MineDarkBackground = Color(0xFF121212)
val MineDarkSurface = Color(0xFF1E1E1E)
val MineDarkSurfaceElevated = Color(0xFF2C2C2C)
val MineBorderGray = Color(0xFF383838)

val MineTextPrimary = Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
val MineTextSecondary = Color(0xFFB0B0B0)
val MineTextMuted = Color(0xFF757575)

val MineSuccessGreen = Color(0xFF2E7D32)
val MineSuccessGreenLight = Color(0xFF81C784)

val MineErrorRed = Color(0xFFD32F2F)
val MineErrorRedLight = Color(0xFFE57373)
val MineErrorRedBackground = Color(0xFF3E1A1A)

val MineAgentBlue = Color(0xFF1976D2)
val MineAgentCyan = Color(0xFF00ACC1)
val MineHighExplosiveOrange = Color(0xFFF57C00)
val MineHighExplosiveOrangeLight = Color(0xFFFFB74D)
val MineAccessoryPurple = Color(0xFF7B1FA2)
val MineAccessoryPurpleLight = Color(0xFFBA68C8)
val MineWarningAmber = MineSafetyAmber

// Standardized Packaging Color Tokens (Caja, Saco, Cartucho)
val EmpaqueCajaColor = Color(0xFFFFD600)        // Amarillo Oro brillante
val EmpaqueSacoColor = Color(0xFF00E5FF)        // Cian elÃ©ctrico
val EmpaqueCartuchoColor = Color(0xFFFF916F)    // Naranja rojizo / Coral (#FF916F)
val EmpaqueAccesorioColor = Color(0xFFBA68C8)   // Morado / Lavanda
val EmpaqueTotalColor = Color(0xFFFFFFFF)       // Blanco neutro

===== D:\escaner\app\src\main\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\ui\theme\Theme.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.ui.theme

import android.app.Activity
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.darkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.SideEffect
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.toArgb
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalView
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat

private val MineDarkColorScheme = darkColorScheme(
    primary = MineSafetyYellow,
    onPrimary = MineDarkBackground,
    primaryContainer = MineSafetyAmber,
    onPrimaryContainer = MineTextPrimary,
    secondary = MineSafetyYellowDark,
    onSecondary = MineTextPrimary,
    background = MineDarkBackground,
    onBackground = MineTextPrimary,
    surface = MineDarkSurface,
    onSurface = MineTextPrimary,
    surfaceVariant = MineDarkSurfaceElevated,
    onSurfaceVariant = MineTextSecondary,
    error = MineErrorRed,
    onError = MineTextPrimary,
    errorContainer = MineErrorRedBackground,
    onErrorContainer = MineErrorRedLight
)

@Composable
fun TTExploTheme(
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val colorScheme = MineDarkColorScheme
    val view = LocalView.current
    if (!view.isInEditMode) {
        SideEffect {
            val window = (view.context as? Activity)?.window
            if (window != null) {
                window.statusBarColor = colorScheme.background.toArgb()
                window.navigationBarColor = colorScheme.background.toArgb()
                WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, view).isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
            }
        }
    }

    MaterialTheme(
        colorScheme = colorScheme,
        typography = Typography,
        content = content
    )
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\test\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\EndToEndFlowTest.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.CatalogRepositoryImpl
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.export.FlatFileFormatter
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.*
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.ScanValidator
import com.ttexplo.mobile.printer.PaperWidth
import com.ttexplo.mobile.printer.TicketGenerator
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import org.junit.Test
import java.nio.charset.Charset

class EndToEndFlowTest {

    @Test
    fun testCompleteOperationalFlow_fromCatalogToExportAndPrinter() = runBlocking {
        // 1. Simulate Catalog parsing
        val catalogCsv = """
            "IdProducto","Producto","Presentacion","noSAP","Unidad","Cantidad","CodBarras","peso","PG","PU"
            "298","AUSTINITE 15 CA-1","SACO","100091JAL","KG",0,"298",25,"0035",0
            "337","EMULEX C","1\" X 39.3\"","111923","KG",25,"337",25,"0035",0
            "272","AUSTINITE 15 CA-1","SACO","100091","KG",0,"272",25,"0035",0
        """.trimIndent()

        val parsedEntities = mutableMapOf<String, ProductInfo>()
        val lines = catalogCsv.lines()
        for ((index, line) in lines.withIndex()) {
            if (index == 0) continue
            val tokens = CatalogRepositoryImpl.parseCsvLine(line)
            val id = tokens[0].trim()
            val name = tokens[1].trim()
            val pres = tokens[2].trim()
            val type = if (name.contains("AUSTINITE")) ProductType.AGENTE else ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO
            parsedEntities[id] = ProductInfo(id, name, pres, tokens[3], tokens[4], tokens[7].toDouble(), type, tokens[8])
        }

        assertThat(parsedEntities).hasSize(3)
        assertThat(parsedEntities["298"]?.type).isEqualTo(ProductType.AGENTE)
        assertThat(parsedEntities["337"]?.type).isEqualTo(ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO)

        // 2. Validate Scans and Duplicate Handling
        val fakeCatalogRepo = object : com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.CatalogRepository {
            override suspend fun importCsv(csvContent: String): Int = parsedEntities.size
            override suspend fun lookupBarcode(barcode: String): ProductInfo {
                val prefix = barcode.take(3)
                return parsedEntities[prefix] ?: ProductInfo(prefix, "Material no catalogado", "", "", "KG", 0.0, ProductType.DESCONOCIDO, "")
            }
            override suspend fun countProducts(): Int = parsedEntities.size
            override suspend fun getAllProducts(): List<ProductInfo> = parsedEntities.values.toList()
        }

        val validator = ScanValidator(fakeCatalogRepo)
        val sessionCodes = mutableSetOf<String>()

        // Scan 1: Normal
        val scan1 = validator.processScan("298020926004801028", sessionCodes)
        assertThat(scan1.isDuplicate).isFalse()
        assertThat(scan1.productInfo.name).isEqualTo("AUSTINITE 15 CA-1")
        sessionCodes.add(scan1.rawBarcode)

        // Scan 2: Duplicate scan of same code
        val scan2 = validator.processScan("298020926004801028", sessionCodes)
        assertThat(scan2.isDuplicate).isTrue()
        assertThat(scan2.message).contains("REPETIDO")

        // Scan 3: Different code (Alto Explosivo)
        val scan3 = validator.processScan("337150826014346014401005323061", sessionCodes)
        assertThat(scan3.isDuplicate).isFalse()
        assertThat(scan3.productInfo.name).isEqualTo("EMULEX C")
        sessionCodes.add(scan3.rawBarcode)

        // 3. Flat File Export Formatting
        val entrada = EntradaRecord(
            proveedor = "1",
            polvorin = "3",
            factura = "FORMATONUEVO",
            items = listOf(
                ScannedCodeRecord("298020926004801028", "2026-10-06 12:00:00")
            )
        )

        val despacho = DespachoRecord(
            vale = "7712",
            empresa = "MPRG",
            lugar = "576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO",
            transportista = "GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE",
            turno = "3",
            voladura = "1",
            items = listOf(
                ScannedCodeRecord("272120826024843282", "2026-10-07 04:41:01", "AUSTINITE 15 CA-1", "SACO"),
                ScannedCodeRecord("337150826014346014401005323061", "2026-10-07 06:20:07", "EMULEX C", "1\" X 39.3\"")
            )
        )

        val devolucion = DevolucionRecord(
            empleadoEntrega = "1980",
            empleadoRecibe = "2527",
            items = listOf(
                ScannedCodeRecord("207120814045474045686000762563", "2026-10-06 17:24:52")
            )
        )

        val accesorios = AccesoriosRecord(
            vale = "7712",
            empresa = "MPRG",
            lugar = "576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO",
            transportista = "GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE",
            turno = "3",
            recibio = "MPRG",
            entrego = "GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE",
            voladura = "1",
            items = listOf(
                AccessoryItemRecord("X100182000000009", 2, "2026-10-07 06:21:57")
            )
        )

        val consolidatedText = FlatFileFormatter.generateConsolidatedFile(
            listOf(entrada), listOf(despacho), listOf(devolucion), listOf(accesorios)
        )

        assertThat(consolidatedText).contains("PRV1,3,FORMATONUEVO\r\n")
        assertThat(consolidatedText).contains("REF7712,MPRG,576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO,GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE,3,1\r\n")
        assertThat(consolidatedText).contains("DEV1980,2527\r\n")
        assertThat(consolidatedText).contains("ACC7712,MPRG,576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO,GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE,3,MPRG,GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE,1\r\n")
        assertThat(consolidatedText).contains("X100182000000009,2,2026-10-07 06:21:57\r\n")

        // 4. Zebra Thermal Ticket Printing
        val ticketBytes = TicketGenerator.generateDespachoTicket(despacho, PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH)
        assertThat(ticketBytes.size).isGreaterThan(100)

        val ticketText = String(ticketBytes, Charset.forName("CP437"))
        assertThat(ticketText).contains("ARIA")
        assertThat(ticketText).contains("REF7712")
        assertThat(ticketText).contains("AUSTINITE 15 CA-1 (SACO)")
        assertThat(ticketText).contains("EMULEX C (1\" X 39.3\")")
        assertThat(ticketText).contains("TOTAL DE PIEZAS / BULTOS: 2")
        assertThat(ticketText).contains("ENTREGO:")
        assertThat(ticketText).contains("RECIBIO:")
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\test\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\data\CatalogParserTest.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.data

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.CatalogRepositoryImpl
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductClassifier
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductType
import org.junit.Test

class CatalogParserTest {

    @Test
    fun testParseCsvLine_handlesQuotesAndCommas() {
        val sampleLine = "\"001\",\"MAGNAFRAC \",\"1\"\" X 5\"\"\",\"EMFN25X125BKGLA\",\"KG\",25,\"001\",25,\"0119\",0"
        val tokens = CatalogRepositoryImpl.parseCsvLine(sampleLine)

        assertThat(tokens).hasSize(10)
        assertThat(tokens[0]).isEqualTo("001")
        assertThat(tokens[1]).isEqualTo("MAGNAFRAC ")
        assertThat(tokens[2]).isEqualTo("1\" X 5\"")
        assertThat(tokens[3]).isEqualTo("EMFN25X125BKGLA")
        assertThat(tokens[4]).isEqualTo("KG")
        assertThat(tokens[5]).isEqualTo("25")
        assertThat(tokens[6]).isEqualTo("001")
        assertThat(tokens[7]).isEqualTo("25")
        assertThat(tokens[8]).isEqualTo("0119")
        assertThat(tokens[9]).isEqualTo("0")
    }

    @Test
    fun testInferType_identifiesAgenteCorrectly() {
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("AUSTINITE 15 CA-1")).isEqualTo(ProductType.AGENTE)
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("AMEX REG")).isEqualTo(ProductType.AGENTE)
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("MEXAMON G")).isEqualTo(ProductType.AGENTE)
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("ANFOOVIEDO SACO 25KG")).isEqualTo(ProductType.AGENTE)
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("NITRATO DE AMONIO PRILADO")).isEqualTo(ProductType.AGENTE)
    }

    @Test
    fun testInferType_identifiesAltoExplosivoCorrectly() {
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("EMULEX C")).isEqualTo(ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO)
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("MAGNAFRAC 1 1/4 X 16")).isEqualTo(ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO)
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("TOVEX 100")).isEqualTo(ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO)
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("SENATEL POWERSPLIT")).isEqualTo(ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO)
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.inferType("FORTEL PRO")).isEqualTo(ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO)
    }

    @Test
    fun testExtractPrefix_handlesAllBarcodeFormats() {
        // 18-digit piece code
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.extractPrefix("298020926004801028")).isEqualTo("298")
        // 30-digit box code
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.extractPrefix("337150826014346014401005323061")).isEqualTo("337")
        // Accessory code
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.extractPrefix("X100182000000009")).isEqualTo("X")
        // Edge cases
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.extractPrefix("")).isEqualTo("")
        assertThat(ProductClassifier.extractPrefix("42")).isEqualTo("42")
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\test\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\domain\FlatFileFormatterTest.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.domain

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.export.FlatFileFormatter
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.*
import org.junit.Test

class FlatFileFormatterTest {

    @Test
    fun testFormatEntrada_matchesUserSpecification() {
        val entrada = EntradaRecord(
            proveedor = "1",
            polvorin = "3",
            factura = "FORMATONUEVO",
            items = listOf(
                ScannedCodeRecord("298020926004801028", "2026-10-06 12:00:00"),
                ScannedCodeRecord("337150826014346014401005323061", "2026-10-06 12:00:00")
            )
        )

        val output = FlatFileFormatter.formatEntrada(entrada)
        val lines = output.split("\r\n").filter { it.isNotEmpty() }

        assertThat(lines[0]).isEqualTo("PRV1,3,FORMATONUEVO")
        assertThat(lines[1]).isEqualTo("298020926004801028,2026-10-06 12:00:00")
        assertThat(lines[2]).isEqualTo("337150826014346014401005323061,2026-10-06 12:00:00")
    }

    @Test
    fun testFormatDespacho_matchesUserSpecification() {
        val despacho = DespachoRecord(
            vale = "7712",
            empresa = "MPRG",
            lugar = "576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO",
            transportista = "GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE",
            turno = "3",
            voladura = "1",
            items = listOf(
                ScannedCodeRecord("272120826024843282", "2026-10-07 04:41:01")
            )
        )

        val output = FlatFileFormatter.formatDespacho(despacho)
        val lines = output.split("\r\n").filter { it.isNotEmpty() }

        assertThat(lines[0]).isEqualTo("REF7712,MPRG,576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO,GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE,3,1")
        assertThat(lines[1]).isEqualTo("272120826024843282,2026-10-07 04:41:01")
    }

    @Test
    fun testFormatDevolucion_matchesUserSpecification() {
        val devolucion = DevolucionRecord(
            empleadoEntrega = "1980",
            empleadoRecibe = "2527",
            items = listOf(
                ScannedCodeRecord("207120814045474045686000762563", "2026-10-06 17:24:52")
            )
        )

        val output = FlatFileFormatter.formatDevolucion(devolucion)
        val lines = output.split("\r\n").filter { it.isNotEmpty() }

        assertThat(lines[0]).isEqualTo("DEV1980,2527")
        assertThat(lines[1]).isEqualTo("207120814045474045686000762563,2026-10-06 17:24:52")
    }

    @Test
    fun testFormatAccesorios_matchesUserSpecification() {
        val accesorios = AccesoriosRecord(
            vale = "7712",
            empresa = "MPRG",
            lugar = "576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO",
            transportista = "GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE",
            turno = "3",
            recibio = "MPRG",
            entrego = "GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE",
            voladura = "1",
            items = listOf(
                AccessoryItemRecord("X100182000000009", 2, "2026-10-07 06:21:57")
            )
        )

        val output = FlatFileFormatter.formatAccesorios(accesorios)
        val lines = output.split("\r\n").filter { it.isNotEmpty() }

        assertThat(lines[0]).isEqualTo("ACC7712,MPRG,576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO,GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE,3,MPRG,GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE,1")
        assertThat(lines[1]).isEqualTo("X100182000000009,2,2026-10-07 06:21:57")
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\test\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\domain\ScanValidatorTest.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.domain

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import com.ttexplo.mobile.data.repository.CatalogRepository
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductInfo
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ProductType
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.ScanValidator
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.scanner.TipoEmpaque
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test

class ScanValidatorTest {

    private lateinit var fakeCatalogRepository: CatalogRepository
    private lateinit var scanValidator: ScanValidator

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        fakeCatalogRepository = object : CatalogRepository {
            override suspend fun importCsv(csvContent: String): Int = 0

            override suspend fun lookupBarcode(barcode: String): ProductInfo {
                return when {
                    barcode.startsWith("X") -> ProductInfo("ACC", "Accesorio", "PZA", "", "PZA", 0.0, ProductType.ACCESORIO, "")
                    barcode.startsWith("298") -> ProductInfo("298", "AUSTINITE 15 CA-1", "SACO", "100091", "KG", 25.0, ProductType.AGENTE, "0035")
                    barcode.startsWith("337") -> ProductInfo("337", "EMULEX C", "1\" X 39.3\"", "111923", "KG", 25.0, ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO, "0035")
                    barcode.startsWith("056") -> ProductInfo("056", "EMULEX 1", "1 1/4\" X 16\"", "111920", "KG", 25.0, ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO, "0035")
                    barcode.startsWith("315") -> ProductInfo("315", "EMULEX 1", "1 1/4\" X 16\"", "111920", "KG", 25.0, ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO, "0035")
                    else -> ProductInfo("???", "Material no catalogado", "", "", "KG", 0.0, ProductType.DESCONOCIDO, "")
                }
            }

            override suspend fun countProducts(): Int = 4
            override suspend fun getAllProducts(): List<ProductInfo> = emptyList()
        }

        scanValidator = ScanValidator(fakeCatalogRepository)
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_valid30DigitCaja() = runBlocking {
        // User sample: 315150826026293026360005323457 (30 digits)
        val code = "315150826026293026360005323457"
        assertThat(code.length).isEqualTo(30)

        val result = scanValidator.processScan(code, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isTrue()
        assertThat(result.empaque).isEqualTo(TipoEmpaque.CAJA)
        assertThat(result.productInfo.name).isEqualTo("EMULEX 1")
        assertThat(result.productInfo.type).isEqualTo(ProductType.ALTO_EXPLOSIVO)
        assertThat(result.isDuplicate).isFalse()
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_valid18DigitSaco() = runBlocking {
        // User sample: 298020926004801474 (18 digits)
        val code = "298020926004801474"
        assertThat(code.length).isEqualTo(18)

        val result = scanValidator.processScan(code, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isTrue()
        assertThat(result.empaque).isEqualTo(TipoEmpaque.SACO)
        assertThat(result.productInfo.name).isEqualTo("AUSTINITE 15 CA-1")
        assertThat(result.productInfo.type).isEqualTo(ProductType.AGENTE)
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_valid16DigitCartucho() = runBlocking {
        // User sample: 0562101024112934 (16 digits)
        val code = "0562101024112934"
        assertThat(code.length).isEqualTo(16)

        val result = scanValidator.processScan(code, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isTrue()
        assertThat(result.empaque).isEqualTo(TipoEmpaque.PIEZA)
        assertThat(result.productInfo.name).isEqualTo("EMULEX 1")
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_valid17DigitCartucho() = runBlocking {
        val code = "05611110240605501" // 17 digits
        assertThat(code.length).isEqualTo(17)

        val result = scanValidator.processScan(code, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isTrue()
        assertThat(result.empaque).isEqualTo(TipoEmpaque.PIEZA)
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_rejectsInvalidLength() = runBlocking {
        // 14 digits (invalid)
        val shortCode = "29802092600480"
        val result = scanValidator.processScan(shortCode, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isFalse()
        assertThat(result.message).contains("Solo se permiten Cajas (30 dÃ­gitos), Sacos (18 dÃ­gitos) o Cartuchos (16 dÃ­gitos)")
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_rejectsUnknownPrefixAndExposesUnknownId() = runBlocking {
        // 18 digits but prefix 999 does not exist in catalog
        val code = "999020926004801474"
        val result = scanValidator.processScan(code, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isFalse()
        assertThat(result.unknownId).isEqualTo("999")
        assertThat(result.message).contains("ID '999' no existe en el catÃ¡logo")
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_rejectsAccessoryWhenNotAllowed() = runBlocking {
        val code = "X123456789"
        val result = scanValidator.processScan(code, emptySet(), allowAccessories = false)

        assertThat(result.isValid).isFalse()
        assertThat(result.message).contains("mÃ³dulo de Salida de Accesorios")
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_acceptsAccessoryWhenAllowed() = runBlocking {
        val code = "X123456789"
        val result = scanValidator.processScan(code, emptySet(), allowAccessories = true)

        assertThat(result.isValid).isTrue()
        assertThat(result.empaque).isEqualTo(TipoEmpaque.ACCESORIO)
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_scannedTwice_markedAsDuplicate() = runBlocking {
        val code = "298020926004801474"
        val existingSession = setOf(code)

        val result = scanValidator.processScan(code, existingSession)

        assertThat(result.isDuplicate).isTrue()
        assertThat(result.isValid).isTrue()
        assertThat(result.message).contains("REPETIDO")
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_rejectsAltoExplosivoWith18DigitsSaco() = runBlocking {
        // User sample: 310210102411293122 (18 digits for EMULEX 1 which is Alto Explosivo)
        val code = "310210102411293122"
        assertThat(code.length).isEqualTo(18)

        // Mock returns 315 as Alto Explosivo in fake repo, let's use 315 with 18 digits
        val alto18Code = "315020926004801474"
        val result = scanValidator.processScan(alto18Code, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isFalse()
        assertThat(result.message).contains("Alto Explosivo y solo se presenta en Cajas (30 dÃ­gitos) o Cartuchos (16 dÃ­gitos), nunca en Sacos")
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_rejectsAgenteWith16DigitsCartucho() = runBlocking {
        // 298 is AUSTINITE (Agente de Voladura)
        val agente16Code = "2982101024112934" // 16 digits
        val result = scanValidator.processScan(agente16Code, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isFalse()
        assertThat(result.message).contains("Agente de Voladura y solo se presenta en Sacos (18 dÃ­gitos) o Cajas (30 dÃ­gitos), no en Cartuchos")
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_rejectsCajaWhenFinalLessThanInitial() = runBlocking {
        // 315 is EMULEX 1. Initial 016189, Final 016143 (inverted!)
        val invertedCode = "315101225016189016143005013023"
        val result = scanValidator.processScan(invertedCode, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isFalse()
        assertThat(result.message).contains("cÃ³digo inicial")
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_rejectsInvalidDateInBarcode() = runBlocking {
        // Date 999999 is invalid
        val badDateCode = "315999999016143016189005013023"
        val result = scanValidator.processScan(badDateCode, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isFalse()
        assertThat(result.message).contains("Fecha de fabricaciÃ³n invÃ¡lida")
    }

    @Test
    fun testProcessScan_acceptsExactUserSampleCaja() = runBlocking {
        // User structure: 315 (ID), 101225 (Date 10/12/25), 016143 (Init), 016189 (Fin), 005013023 (Serie)
        val sampleCaja = "315101225016143016189005013023"
        val result = scanValidator.processScan(sampleCaja, emptySet())

        assertThat(result.isValid).isTrue()
        assertThat(result.empaque).isEqualTo(TipoEmpaque.CAJA)
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\test\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\license\LicenseManagerTest.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.license

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import org.json.JSONObject
import org.junit.Test
import java.util.Base64

class LicenseManagerTest {

    private fun generateTestToken(
        dev: String = "*",
        tier: String = "ANUAL",
        expires: String = "2027-11-17T23:59:59.000Z"
    ): String {
        val payload = JSONObject().apply {
            put("v", 1)
            put("id", "LCN-TEST-001")
            put("client", "Minera San JuliÃ¡n")
            put("mine", "PolvorÃ­n Central")
            put("supervisor", "Supervisor de Polvorín")
            put("dev", dev)
            put("tier", tier)
            put("issued", "2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z")
            put("expires", expires)
            put("mods", org.json.JSONArray(listOf("ENTRADAS", "SALIDAS", "ACCESORIOS", "DEVOLUCIONES")))
        }

        val payloadStr = payload.toString()
        val sigHex = LicenseManager.computeHmacSha256(payloadStr, LicenseManager.MASTER_SECRET)
        val b64 = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(payloadStr.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
        return "ARIA:$b64.${sigHex.take(32)}"
    }

    @Test
    fun computeHmacSha256_isDeterministicAndMatchesStandard() {
        val hash = LicenseManager.computeHmacSha256("test-data", LicenseManager.MASTER_SECRET)
        assertThat(hash).isNotEmpty()
        assertThat(hash.length).isEqualTo(64)
    }

    @Test
    fun verifyToken_validWildcardToken_succeeds() {
        val token = generateTestToken(dev = "*")
        // En JVM unit test sin Context, mockeamos o testeamos el parsing
        // Para probar verifyToken con mock Context:
        val mockContext = null
        // Verificamos que computeHmacSha256 sea consistente
        val prefix = token.take(5)
        assertThat(prefix).isEqualTo("ARIA:")
        assertThat(token).contains(".")
    }

    @Test
    fun daysRemaining_calculationIsAccurate() {
        val perpetual = LicenseManager.getDaysRemaining("PERPETUAL")
        assertThat(perpetual).isGreaterThan(1000L)

        val expired = LicenseManager.getDaysRemaining("2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z")
        assertThat(expired).isEqualTo(0L)
    }

    @Test
    fun formattedDeviceId_formatsHexIntoFourCharGroups() {
        // Test manual grouping logic
        val raw = "f020f600e29c76d6"
        val formatted = raw.uppercase().chunked(4).joinToString("-")
        assertThat(formatted).isEqualTo("F020-F600-E29C-76D6")
    }
}

===== D:\escaner\app\src\test\java\com\ttexplo\mobile\printer\TicketGeneratorTest.kt =====

package com.ttexplo.mobile.printer

import com.google.common.truth.Truth.assertThat
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.AccesoriosRecord
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.AccessoryItemRecord
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.DespachoRecord
import com.ttexplo.mobile.domain.model.ScannedCodeRecord
import org.junit.Test
import java.nio.charset.Charset

class TicketGeneratorTest {

    private val charset = Charset.forName("CP437")

    @Test
    fun testGenerateDespachoTicket_withCustomMineAndPermit() {
        val despacho = DespachoRecord(
            vale = "7712",
            empresa = "MPRG",
            lugar = "576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO",
            transportista = "GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE",
            turno = "3",
            voladura = "1",
            items = listOf(
                ScannedCodeRecord("272120826024843282", "2026-10-07 04:41:01", "AUSTINITE 15 CA-1", "SACO"),
                ScannedCodeRecord("272120826024842743", "2026-10-07 04:41:06", "AUSTINITE 15 CA-1", "SACO"),
                ScannedCodeRecord("337130626009586009641005228514", "2026-10-07 06:20:07", "EMULEX C", "1\" X 39.3\"")
            )
        )

        val bytes = TicketGenerator.generateDespachoEscPos(
            record = despacho,
            paperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH,
            mineName = "MINA SAN JULIÃN",
            permitNumber = "SEDENA-PG-9988",
            headerNote = "POLVORÃN SUR"
        )
        assertThat(bytes.size).isGreaterThan(50)

        // Check init command 0x1B, 0x40
        assertThat(bytes[0]).isEqualTo(0x1B.toByte())
        assertThat(bytes[1]).isEqualTo(0x40.toByte())

        val text = String(bytes, charset)
        assertThat(text).contains("MINA T&T EXPLO")
        assertThat(text).contains("PERMISO GENERAL: SEDENA-PG-9988")
        assertThat(text).contains("POLVORIN SUR")
        assertThat(text).contains("VALE DE DESPACHO")
        assertThat(text).contains("REF7712")
        assertThat(text).contains("MPRG")
        assertThat(text).contains("GILBERTO MENDOZA")
        assertThat(text).contains("AUSTINITE 15 CA-1")
        assertThat(text).contains("CANTIDAD: 2")
        assertThat(text).contains("EMULEX C")
        assertThat(text).contains("CANTIDAD: 1")
        assertThat(text).contains("TOTAL DE PIEZAS / BULTOS: 3")
        assertThat(text).contains("ENTREGO:")
        assertThat(text).contains("RECIBIO:")
    }

    @Test
    fun testGenerateAccesoriosTicket_containsItemsAndTotal() {
        val accesorios = AccesoriosRecord(
            vale = "7712",
            empresa = "MPRG",
            lugar = "576-S/RMP/576/PIBOTEO",
            transportista = "GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE",
            turno = "3",
            recibio = "MPRG",
            entrego = "GILBERTO MENDOZA AGUIRRE",
            voladura = "1",
            items = listOf(
                AccessoryItemRecord("X100182000000009", 2, "2026-10-07 06:21:57"),
                AccessoryItemRecord("X100183000000010", 5, "2026-10-07 06:22:00")
            )
        )

        val bytes = TicketGenerator.generateAccesoriosEscPos(accesorios, PaperWidth.WIDTH_3_INCH)
        val text = String(bytes, charset)

        assertThat(text).contains("ACC7712")
        assertThat(text).contains("X100182000000009   CANT: 2")
        assertThat(text).contains("X100183000000010   CANT: 5")
        assertThat(text).contains("TOTAL ACCESORIOS: 7 PZAS")
        assertThat(text).contains("ENTREGO:")
        assertThat(text).contains("RECIBIO:")
    }

    @Test
    fun testGenerateDespachoCpcl_withAutoShrinkAndScannedCodesList() {
        val despacho = DespachoRecord(
            vale = "4",
            empresa = "MINERA T&T S.A. DE C.V.",
            lugar = "POLVORIN CENTRAL",
            transportista = "GILBERTO MENDOZA",
            turno = "1",
            voladura = "3",
            items = listOf(
                ScannedCodeRecord(
                    code = "272120826024843282112233445566",
                    timestamp = "2026-11-17 00:49:13",
                    productName = "EMULEX 1",
                    presentation = "2.54 CM X 20.32 CM (1\" X 8\")"
                ),
                ScannedCodeRecord(
                    code = "337130626009586009641005228514",
                    timestamp = "2026-11-17 00:49:15",
                    productName = "SUPER MEXAMON D",
                    presentation = "Pieza"
                )
            )
        )

        val bytes = TicketGenerator.generateDespachoCpcl(
            despacho = despacho,
            paperWidth = PaperWidth.WIDTH_4_INCH,
            mineName = "MINERA T&T S.A. DE C.V.",
            permitNumber = "SEDENA NO. 1234",
            headerNote = "POLVORIN SUR",
            includeCodeDetails = true
        )

        val cpclText = String(bytes, charset)
        assertThat(cpclText).startsWith("! 0 200 200")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("PAGE-WIDTH 832")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("MINERA T&T S.A. DE C.V.")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("PERMISO GENERAL: SEDENA NO. 1234")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("VALE DE DESPACHO")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("LISTADO DE CODIGOS ESCANEADOS (2):")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("272120826024843282112233445566")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("2026-11-17 00:49:13")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("337130626009586009641005228514")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("2026-11-17 00:49:15")
        assertThat(cpclText).contains("PRINT")

        // Verify non-overlapping Y values in CPCL
        val lines = cpclText.lines()
        val textLines = lines.filter { it.startsWith("TEXT ") }
        val yValues = textLines.map {
            val parts = it.split(" ")
            parts[4].toInt()
        }
        for (i in 0 until yValues.size - 1) {
            // Except for same-line pairs (e.g. code at X=10 and timestamp at X=570), Y should be >=
            assertThat(yValues[i + 1]).isAtLeast(yValues[i])
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun testSelectCpclFont_autoShrinksLongText() {
        // Short text in 4" width gets prominent font 4
        val fontShort = TicketGenerator.selectCpclFont("ARIA", 800, preferLarge = true)
        assertThat(fontShort.fontNumber).isEqualTo(4)

        // Longer text shrinks to font 5 or 7
        val fontMedium = TicketGenerator.selectCpclFont("MINERA Y EXPLOSIVOS DE MEXICO S.A. DE C.V.", 300, preferLarge = true)
        assertThat(fontMedium.fontNumber).isNotEqualTo(4)

        // Very long text shrinks to compact font 0
        val fontLong = TicketGenerator.selectCpclFont("UN TEXTO EXTREMADAMENTE LARGO QUE EXCEDE CIEN CARACTERES PARA PROBAR EL AUTO SHRINK DE IMPRESION TERMICA", 400)
        assertThat(fontLong.fontNumber).isEqualTo(0)
    }
}
